## 980107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなこと言ってる場合じゃないわ。<line-break>うだうだ言わないで、さっさと来る！<end>"
EN: "We don't have time for this. Stop dawdling and get over here!<end>"
KO: "그런 말 할 때가 아니야. 투덜대지 말고 얼른 와!<end>"

## 980108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オッシ先生の話では、リベルダム方面に<line-break>向かう奇妙なゴブリンの３匹組を<line-break>見た人がいたそうよ。<line-break>他に手がかりもないし、まずは<line-break>リベルダムに行くしかなさそうね。<end>"
EN: "According to Master Ossi, someone saw a strange trio of Goblins heading toward Liberdam. We don't have any other leads, so it looks like we'll have to go to Liberdam first.<end>"
KO: "오시 선생님 말로는 리벨덤 쪽으로 향하는 이상한 고블린 3인조를 봤다는 사람이 있었대. 다른 단서도 없으니 우선 리벨덤으로 갈 수밖에 없겠네.<end>"

## 980109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、リベルダムに行くわよ。<line-break>くすっ。<value:$28>、<line-break>頼りにしてるからね。<end>"
EN: "All right, we're going to Liberdam. Heh. I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 리벨덤으로 가자. 후훗, <value:$28>, 믿고 있을게.<end>"

## 980110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、自由都市リベルダムに着いたわ。<line-break>もうすぐ夕方だけど、まだあいつらを<line-break>捜す時間はあるわよね。<end>"
EN: "Here we are―the Free City Liberdam. It's nearly evening, but we still have time to look for them, don't we?<end>"
KO: "자, 자유도시 리벨덤에 도착했어. 곧 저녁이지만 아직 녀석들을 찾을 시간은 있겠지.<end>"

## 980111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぅ…。確かに、この前は<line-break>暗闇で戦って不覚をとったわね…。<line-break>　<line-break>わかったわ。<line-break>今日はもう宿屋に行きましょ。<line-break>でも、明日は朝からゴブリン捜しよ。<end>"
EN: "Ugh... I suppose you're right. We did let them get the better of us last time, fighting in the dark. All right. Let's find an inn for tonight. But tomorrow morning, we're hunting goblins.<end>"
KO: "으으…. 확실히 지난번에는 어둠 속에서 싸우다 허를 찔렸지…. 알았어. 오늘은 이제 여관으로 가자. 하지만 내일은 아침부터 고블린 찾기야.<end>"

## 980112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、それと、あとで冒険者ギルドに<line-break>登録しておいた方がいいわね。<line-break>　<line-break>ギルドに登録しておけば、<line-break>うまくいくと、あいつらの情報が<line-break>手に入るわ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and we should register with the Adventurers' Guild later. If we do, we might be able to get some information on them.<end>"
KO: "아, 그리고 나중에 모험자 길드에 등록해 두는 게 좋아. 길드에 등록해 두면 운이 좋을 경우 녀석들 정보를 얻을 수 있어.<end>"

## 980113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、街に入りましょ。<end>"
EN: "Then let's head into the city.<end>"
KO: "그럼 도시로 들어가자.<end>"

## 980114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、知ってる？　賢者の森の<line-break>猫屋敷には、運命に選ばれた者しか<line-break>入れないんだって。<line-break>あの変なゴブリンたちを追って<line-break>私たちは猫屋敷まで来たわ。<line-break>偶然だけどね。<end>"
EN: "Did you know? They say only those chosen by fate can enter Cat Manor in Sage's Forest. We followed those strange goblins all the way there. By coincidence, of course.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 알아? 현자의 숲에 있는 고양이 저택에는 운명에 선택받은 자만 들어갈 수 있대. 그 이상한 고블린들을 쫓다가 우린 고양이 저택까지 왔어. 우연이긴 하지만.<end>"

## 980115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、それって、私たちが<line-break>運命に選ばれた者ってことよね。<line-break>　<line-break>なんだか、これからの旅が<line-break>おもしろくなりそうね。<end>"
EN: "But that means we're the ones chosen by fate, doesn't it? It feels as though our journey is about to get interesting.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그건 우리가 운명에 선택받은 자들이란 뜻이잖아. 왠지 앞으로 여행이 재미있어질 것 같네.<end>"

## 980116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、ぐずぐずしてられないわ。<line-break>早くエンシャントに戻って<line-break>ゴブリンたちを追わなくちゃね。<end>"
EN: "Come on, we can't waste any more time. We have to get back to Ancient and go after those goblins.<end>"
KO: "자, 꾸물거릴 때가 아니야. 빨리 엔샨트로 돌아가서 고블린들을 쫓아야 해.<end>"

## 980117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>。<line-break>やっと来たわね。<line-break>　<line-break>あいつらは賢者の森に向かったわ。<line-break>早く追うわよ。もちろん、<line-break>用意はできてるわよね？<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. There you are at last. They've headed for Sage's Forest. Let's hurry after them. You're ready, of course?<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>. 이제야 왔네. 그 녀석들은 현자의 숲으로 갔어. 빨리 쫓아가자. 준비는 됐지?<end>"

## 980118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、この扱いには<line-break>断固、抗議するゴブ…。<end>"
EN: "I-I vehemently protest this treatment, gob...<end>"
KO: "이, 이런 대우에는 단호히 항의한다 고브….<end>"

## 980119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、<value:$28>。<line-break>どこに行くつもりよ。<line-break>そっちに行くと、森の出口よ。<line-break>早くゴブリンを追うのよ。<line-break>わかったわね、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Where do you think you're going? That way leads out of the forest. Hurry up and go after the Goblin. Got it, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "잠깐, <value:$28>. 어디 갈 생각이야. 그쪽은 숲 출구잖아. 빨리 고블린을 쫓아. 알았지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 980120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マルーンを失った。<end>"
EN: "Lost Maroon.<end>"
KO: "마룬을 잃었다.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もちろん、できている<end>"
EN: "Of course I'm ready.<end>"
KO: "물론, 준비됐어.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ、できてない<end>"

## 980123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、私たちも<line-break>賢者の森に向かうわよ。<end>"
EN: "Then we're off to Sage's Forest too.<end>"
KO: "그럼 우리도 현자의 숲으로 가자.<end>"

## 980124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ何かすることがあるの？<line-break>…ここで待っててあげるから<line-break>急いで、行ってらっしゃい。<line-break>本当に、急いでよ！？<end>"
EN: "Is there still something you need to do? Fine, I'll wait here, so go take care of it. But hurry. I mean it!<end>"
KO: "아직 할 일이 남았어? …여기서 기다려 줄 테니 빨리 다녀와. 정말 빨리 와야 해!?<end>"

## 980125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつでも戻ってらっしゃい。<line-break>ここは、あなたの家なんですから。<end>"
EN: "Come back anytime. This is your home, after all.<end>"
KO: "언제든 돌아오세요. 여기는 당신의 집이니까요.<end>"

## 980126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、<value:$28>。<line-break>おかえりなさい。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>あなたはこうして顔を見せに<line-break>きてくれるけど、アイリーンは全然ね。<line-break>どう？　あの子は元気にしてる？<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. Welcome home. You come by to see me, but Irene never does. How is she? Is she well?<end>"
KO: "어머, <value:$28>. 돌아왔구나. 넌 이렇게 얼굴을 비추러 오는데 아이린은 전혀 안 오네. 어때? 그 아이는 잘 지내?<end>"

JP: "未使用になります：そう。<line-break>久しぶりに一緒に食事できるわね。<line-break>腕によりをかけて料理するわ。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 그래. 오랜만에 같이 식사할 수 있겠네. 솜씨를 발휘해서 요리해 줄게.<end>"

## 980130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずっと前から、<line-break>アイリーンにはあなたが必要だったの。<line-break>…あの子のこと、これからもお願いね。<end>"
EN: "Irene has needed you for a very long time. Please keep looking out for her.<end>"
KO: "아주 오래전부터 아이린에게는 당신이 필요했어요. …그 아이를 앞으로도 부탁해요.<end>"

## 980131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンは素直じゃないから、<line-break>いつまでも、あなたを<line-break>子供扱いするでしょうけど、<line-break>あの子、<line-break>本当はあなたのことを<line-break>信頼しているのよ。<end>"
EN: "Irene isn't very forthright, so she'll probably always treat you like a child. But the truth is, she trusts you.<end>"
KO: "아이린은 솔직하지 못해서 언제까지고 당신을 아이 취급하겠지만, 그 아이 사실은 당신을 믿고 있어.<end>"

## 980132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未使用になります：そう。<line-break>でも、あまり無理をしちゃダメよ。<line-break>　<line-break>いつでも、帰ってらっしゃい。<line-break>あなたの部屋は<line-break>そのままにしてあるから。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용입니다: 그래. 하지만 너무 무리하면 안 돼. 언제든 돌아오렴. 네 방은 그대로 두었으니까.<end>"

## 980134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未使用：こんなことになるなんて…<line-break>何かが…、どこかで…、<line-break>食い違ってしまったんでしょうね。<line-break>あの子は<line-break>オッシ先生を<line-break>とても尊敬していたのに…。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 이런 일이 되다니… 뭔가가… 어디선가… 어긋나 버린 거겠지. 그 아이는 오시 선생님을 정말 존경했는데….<end>"

## 980135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未使用：あの子が…<line-break>この町を襲うなんて…<line-break>あんなにこの町を愛していたのに…。<line-break>こんなことになるなんて…<line-break>何かが…、どこかで…、<line-break>食い違ってしまったんでしょうね。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 그 아이가… 이 마을을 공격하다니… 그렇게나 이 마을을 사랑했는데…. 이런 일이 되다니… 무언가가… 어디선가… 어긋나 버린 거겠죠.<end>"

## 980136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未使用：人なんて、いいかげんなものね。<line-break>この間まで、白い目で見ていた人たちが<line-break>エルメス将軍にお取りなしをって…。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 사람이라는 건 참 제멋대로네. 얼마 전까지만 해도 차가운 눈으로 보던 사람들이 에르메스 장군에게 잘 말해 달라니….<end>"

## 980137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの子は、まっすぐで純粋だから…<line-break>そんな子が、あんな人たちの中で<line-break>暮らしていくのは、難しいでしょうね。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>あの子のそばにいてあげてね。<line-break>力になってあげてね。<end>"
EN: "She's so earnest and pure... It must be difficult for someone like her to live among people like that. <value:$28>, please stay by her side. Be there for her.<end>"
KO: "그 아이는 올곧고 순수하니까…. 그런 아이가 저런 사람들 사이에서 살아가긴 힘들겠지. <value:$28>, 그 아이 곁에 있어 줘. 힘이 되어 줘.<end>"

## 980138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、あれね。<line-break>ディンガル帝国が<line-break>攻めてきたあとでしょう？　　　　<line-break>あの子がこの街を出たのが<line-break>気に入らない人がいるみたいなの。<line-break>私なら、平気よ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that? You mean after the Dyneskal Empire attacked? It seems some people resent her for leaving this town. But I'm fine.<end>"
KO: "아, 그거. 딩갈 제국이 쳐들어온 뒤 이야기지? 그 아이가 이 도시를 떠난 걸 못마땅하게 여기는 사람이 있는 모양이야. 나라면 괜찮아.<end>"

## 980139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの子にはあの子の考えがあって　<line-break>ディンガルの騎士になった。<line-break>私はあの子を信じてるわ。<end>"
EN: "Irene had her reasons for becoming a knight of Dyneskal. I believe in her.<end>"
KO: "그 아이는 그 아이 나름의 생각이 있어서 딘갈의 기사가 됐어. 나는 그 아이를 믿어.<end>"

## 980140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>あの子が、目指しているものを　　<line-break>見失ったとき…<line-break>あの子のそばにいてあげて。<line-break>力になってあげてほしいの。<line-break>お願いね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, if Irene ever loses sight of what she's striving for... please stay by her side. I want you to be there for her. Please.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 그 아이가 자신이 목표로 하던 걸 잃어버릴 때… 그 아이 곁에 있어줘. 힘이 되어줬으면 해. 부탁할게.<end>"

## 980141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの子にはあの子の考えがあって　<line-break>ディンガルの騎士になった…<line-break>私はあの子を信じてるわ。<end>"
EN: "Irene had her reasons for becoming a knight of Dyneskal... I believe in her.<end>"
KO: "그 아이는 그 아이 나름의 생각이 있어서 딘갈의 기사가 됐어…. 나는 그 아이를 믿어.<end>"

## 980143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつかあなたにもきっと<line-break>これという道が見つかるはずよ。<line-break>応援してるから、がんばりなさい。<end>"
EN: "Someday, I'm sure you'll find a path that feels truly your own. I'm rooting for you, so keep going.<end>"
KO: "언젠가 당신도 분명 '이거다' 싶은 길을 찾을 거야. 응원할 테니 힘내.<end>"

## 980144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ムリしない程度にがんばってね。　<line-break>いつでも戻ってくるのよ。<line-break>あなたはうちの子なんですから。<end>"
EN: "Do your best, but don't push yourself too hard. Come home whenever you like. You're my child, after all.<end>"
KO: "무리하지 않을 정도로 힘내. 언제든 돌아와. 너는 우리 아이니까.<end>"

## 980145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…大丈夫よ。<line-break>あの子なら、きっと無事。　　　　<line-break>そのうち帰ってくるわ。<line-break>それよりも目の前にいる<line-break>放っておけない子の方の<line-break>心配をしてあげなきゃね。<end>"
EN: "She'll be all right. I'm sure she's safe, and she'll come home before long. I should be worrying about the child right in front of me who can't be left alone.<end>"
KO: "…괜찮아. 그 아이라면 분명 무사해. 언젠가 돌아올 거야. 그보다 눈앞에 있는, 그냥 둘 수 없는 아이를 걱정해 줘야지.<end>"

## 980146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ、冗談よ。<line-break>あなたも頑張っているものね。　　<line-break>私は、わかっているつもりよ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, I'm only teasing. I know you're doing your best too. At least, I like to think I do.<end>"
KO: "우후후, 농담이야. 당신도 열심히 하고 있잖아. 난 알고 있다고 생각해.<end>"

## 980147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あなたがいてくれることが　　　<line-break>何よりも、なぐさめだわ。<line-break>　<line-break>さ、今日はあなたの好きなものを<line-break>作ってあげる。<line-break>楽しみにしていてね。<end>"
EN: "...Nothing comforts me more than having you here. Now, I'll make your favorite dish today. You can look forward to it.<end>"
KO: "…당신이 있어 주는 게 무엇보다 위로가 돼. 자, 오늘은 당신이 좋아하는 걸 만들어 줄게. 기대해.<end>"

## 980148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。<line-break>でも、遠慮なんてしないのよ。　　<line-break>あなたもうちの子だもの。<end>"
EN: "I see. But there's no need to be shy. You're my child too.<end>"
KO: "그래. 하지만 사양하지 마. 너도 우리 아이니까.<end>"

## 980149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そう。<line-break>たとえ、アイリーンが　　　　　　<line-break>いなくなっても…。<end>"
EN: "...I see. Even if Irene is no longer here...<end>"
KO: "…그래. 설령 아이린이 없어져도….<end>"

## 980150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンのこと<line-break>よろしくお願いします。　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Please look after Irene.<end>"
KO: "아이린을 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 980151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンという娘にあったら　　<line-break>よろしく、と伝えてください。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "If you meet a girl named Irene, please give her my regards.<end>"
KO: "아이린이라는 아이를 만나면 안부 전해 주세요.<end>"

## 980152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この間、アイリーンが<line-break>帰ってきたと思ったら、<line-break>ずっと、あなたのお話ばかり。　　<line-break><value:$28>さんは<line-break>本当にに頼れるお方なんですね。<line-break>これからも、娘をお願いします。<end>"
EN: "Irene came home the other day and talked of nothing but you. You truly are someone she can rely on, <value:$28>. Please continue to look after my daughter.<end>"
KO: "얼마 전 아이린이 돌아오더니 계속 당신 이야기만 하더군요. <value:$28> 씨는 정말 믿음직한 분이시군요. 앞으로도 딸아이를 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 980153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンは<line-break>少し気が強いところがありますが　<line-break>あれで、やさしい娘なんです。<line-break>娘のこと、<line-break>よろしくお願いしますね。<end>"
EN: "Irene can be a little headstrong, but beneath it all, she's a kind girl. Please look after my daughter.<end>"
KO: "아이린은 조금 기가 센 구석이 있지만 그래도 상냥한 아이예요. 제 딸을 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 980154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなことになるなんて…。<line-break>何かが、どこかで、<line-break>食い違ってしまったんでしょうね。<line-break>あの子は<line-break>オッシ先生を…<line-break>とても尊敬していたのに…。<end>"
EN: "How could it have come to this...? Something must have gone wrong somewhere. She had such great respect for Master Ossi...<end>"
KO: "이렇게 될 줄이야… 어딘가에서 뭔가 엇갈려 버린 거겠죠. 그 아이는 오시 선생님을 정말 존경했는데…<end>"

## 980155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの子がこの町を襲うなんて…。　<line-break>あんなにこの町を<line-break>愛していたのに…。<line-break>こんなことになるなんて…<line-break>何かが、どこかで、<line-break>食い違ってしまったんでしょうね。<end>"
EN: "I can't believe she would attack this town... She loved it so dearly. How could it have come to this? Something must have gone wrong somewhere.<end>"
KO: "그 아이가 이 도시를 습격하다니…. 그렇게 이 도시를 사랑했는데…. 이런 일이 되다니… 뭔가가, 어딘가에서 어긋나 버린 거겠지.<end>"

## 980156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人なんて、いいかげんなものですね。<line-break>これまで、白い目で見ていた人が<line-break>エルメス将軍にお取りなしをって。<end>"
EN: "People can be so fickle. The very ones who were so scornful before are now asking me to put in a good word with General Hermes.<end>"
KO: "사람이란 참 제멋대로군요. 지금까지 차가운 눈으로 보던 사람들이 에르메스 장군께 중재를 부탁하다니.<end>"

## 980157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの子は、まっすぐで純粋だから…<line-break>あんな人たちの中で暮らすのは、<line-break>難しいでしょうね。<line-break><value:$28>さん、<line-break>あの子の支えに…<line-break>力になってあげてください。<end>"
EN: "She's so earnest and pure... Living among people like that can't be easy for her. <value:$28>, please support her. Be there for her.<end>"
KO: "그 아이는 곧고 순수하니까… 그런 사람들 속에서 사는 건 어렵겠죠. <value:$28> 씨, 그 아이의 버팀목이 되어… 힘이 되어 주세요.<end>"

## 980158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、あれですか。<line-break>ディンガル帝国が<line-break>攻めてきたあとでしょう？　　　　<line-break>あの子がこの街を出たのが<line-break>気に入らない人がいるみたいで…。<line-break>ああ、私なら平気ですから…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that? You mean after the Dyneskal Empire attacked? It seems some people resent her for leaving this town. But don't worry about me. I'm perfectly fine.<end>"
KO: "아, 그거요. 딘갈 제국이 쳐들어온 뒤 이야기죠? 그 아이가 이 마을을 떠난 게 마음에 안 드는 사람이 있는 모양이라서…. 아, 저는 괜찮아요….<end>"

## 980159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの子にはあの子の考えがあって　<line-break>ディンガルの騎士になった…。<line-break>私はあの子を信じています。<end>"
EN: "Irene had her reasons for becoming a knight of Dyneskal... I believe in her.<end>"
KO: "그 아이는 그 아이 나름의 생각이 있어서 딘갈의 기사가 됐어요…. 저는 그 아이를 믿습니다.<end>"

## 980160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>あの子が、目指しているものを　　<line-break>見失ったとき…<line-break>あの子のそばにいてあげて。<line-break>力になってあげてください。<line-break>お願いします。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, if Irene ever loses sight of what she's striving for... please stay by her side and support her. Please.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 그 아이가 목표로 하는 것을 잃어버릴 때… 그 아이 곁에 있어주세요. 힘이 되어주세요. 부탁합니다.<end>"

## 980161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの子にはあの子の考えがあって<line-break>ディンガルの騎士になったのです。<line-break>私はあの子を信じています。<end>"
EN: "Irene had her reasons for becoming a knight of Dyneskal. I believe in her.<end>"
KO: "그 아이에게는 그 아이 나름의 생각이 있어서 딘갈의 기사가 된 겁니다. 저는 그 아이를 믿고 있어요.<end>"

## 980162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの子はきっといつか<line-break>自分の夢を勝ち取ります。<line-break>親バカかもしれませんがね。　　　<line-break>ただ、現実は残酷なものです。<end>"
EN: "I'm sure she'll seize the dream she's been pursuing someday. Perhaps that's just a doting mother talking. But reality can be cruel.<end>"
KO: "그 아이는 언젠가 반드시 자기 꿈을 이룰 겁니다. 부모의 욕심일지도 모르지만요. 다만 현실은 잔혹하지요.<end>"

## 980163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、夢をかなえたとしても、<line-break>それはあの子の思っていたものとは<line-break>違うものになるでしょう。<line-break>あの子が、傷つき<line-break>行き先を見失ったときには<line-break>力になってあげてください。<end>"
EN: "And even if her dream comes true, it may not be what she imagined. If she is hurt and loses her way, please be there for her.<end>"
KO: "또 꿈을 이룬다 해도 그건 그 아이가 생각했던 것과는 다른 모습이 될 거예요. 그 아이가 상처받고 갈 길을 잃었을 때 힘이 되어 주세요.<end>"

## 980164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンに<line-break>…娘にお会いになったのですか？　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You met Irene... my daughter?<end>"
KO: "아이린을… 제 딸을 만나신 겁니까?<end>"

## 980165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "娘は元気そうでしたか？　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Did my daughter seem well?<end>"
KO: "딸은 건강해 보였나요?<end>"

## 980166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の死んだ夫は騎士でした。<line-break>それで、あの子も、子供のころから<line-break>騎士になることにあこがれていました。<line-break>剣を習い、その腕も達者でした。<line-break>しかし、ロストールでは<line-break>女は騎士にはなれないのです。<end>"
EN: "My late husband was a knight, so Irene dreamed of becoming one from childhood. She learned swordsmanship and became quite skilled. But in Rostorl, women cannot become knights.<end>"
KO: "죽은 제 남편은 기사였습니다. 그래서 그 아이도 어릴 때부터 기사가 되는 걸 동경했죠. 검을 배웠고 솜씨도 훌륭했습니다. 하지만 로스톨에선 여자가 기사가 될 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 980167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大貴族リューガ家のご息女、<line-break>ノーブル伯が騎士になられたそうですが<line-break>あれは異例中の異例です。<end>"
EN: "I hear the daughter of the eminent House of Ryuga, the Countess of Noble, became a knight, but hers is an exception among exceptions.<end>"
KO: "대귀족 류가 가문의 영애인 노블 백작이 기사가 되셨다고 하지만 그건 정말 이례적인 일입니다.<end>"

## 980168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこで、あの子は<line-break>騎士になろうと<line-break>この国を出ていったのです。　　　　　<line-break>意地っ張りで、まっすぐな子です。<line-break>この町を出てからは、私にさえ<line-break>ほとんど会いに来てくれません。<end>"
EN: "So Irene left this country to become a knight. She's stubborn and unwavering. Since leaving this town, she has hardly ever come to see even me.<end>"
KO: "그래서 그 아이는 기사가 되겠다며 이 나라를 떠났습니다. 고집이 세고 곧은 아이예요. 이 마을을 떠난 뒤로는 저한테조차 거의 찾아오지 않네요.<end>"

## 980169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、そうですか。　　　　　　　　<line-break>元気そうでしたか。<line-break>…よかった。<line-break>また、アイリーンのことを<line-break>話に来てください。<line-break>ええと、失礼ですが…。<end>"
EN: "So... she seemed well? Oh, thank goodness. Please come and tell me about Irene again. Um, forgive me, but...<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까. 건강해 보였나요. …다행입니다. 또 아이린 이야기를 하러 와 주세요. 저, 실례지만…<end>"

## 980170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん…ですか。<line-break>また、いらしてくださいね。　　　　　<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... is that right? Please come again.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨…군요. 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 980171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんにちは、冒険者の方。<line-break>アイリーン・エルメスという娘と<line-break>会えたら、よろしく伝えてください。<line-break>気の強い娘なんで<line-break>ケンカになってしまうかも<line-break>しれませんが。<end>"
EN: "Good day, adventurer. If you happen to meet a girl named Irene Hermes, please give her my regards. She can be rather headstrong, so you may end up quarreling with her.<end>"
KO: "안녕하세요, 모험자님. 아이린 에르메스라는 아이를 만나면 안부 전해 주세요. 성격이 강한 아이라 싸움이 날지도 모르지만요.<end>"

## 980173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、いらっしゃいませー。<line-break>ここは、ロストールで一番大きな酒場<line-break>なんですよ。<line-break>あの…ご用がありましたら<line-break>なんでも私に言いつけてくださいね！<end>"
EN: "Oh, welcome! This is the biggest tavern in Rostorl. If there's anything you need, just let me know!<end>"
KO: "아, 어서 오세요. 여기는 로스톨에서 가장 큰 술집이에요. 저기… 볼일이 있으시면 뭐든 제게 말씀해 주세요!<end>"

## 980174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん……。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>おかえりなさい…。<line-break>うれしい……、<line-break>会いに来てくれるなんて…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>... Welcome back. I'm so happy... I can't believe you came to see me.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님……. 어서 오세요…. 기뻐요…… 만나러 와 주시다니….<end>"

## 980175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………？<line-break>あの、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>……背、伸びましたか？　　　<line-break>いえ、なんとなく、<line-break>とても大きく見えたから……。<line-break>やだ、なんだか私、変ですね。<end>"
EN: "...? Um, <value:$28>... have you grown taller? No, it's just... you somehow seem so much bigger. Oh dear, I'm acting a little strange, aren't I?<end>"
KO: "………………? 저, <value:$28> 씨… 키가 컸나요? 아니, 왠지 아주 커 보였거든요… 어머, 저 이상하네요.<end>"

## 980176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、なんだか<line-break>たくましくなりましたね。<line-break>まるで騎士さまみたいです。<line-break>できれば、これからは<line-break>私だけの騎士さまに……。<end>"
EN: "You seem so much stronger now, <value:$28>. Just like a knight. If only you could be my knight, and mine alone, from now on...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 왠지 듬직해지셨네요. 마치 기사님 같아요. 가능하다면 앞으로는 저만의 기사님으로…….<end>"

## 980177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………あ、いえ、すいません。<line-break>何言ってるんだろ私……。<end>"
EN: "Oh... no, I'm sorry. What am I saying...?<end>"
KO: "………아, 아니, 죄송해요. 나 무슨 말을 하는 거지…….<end>"

## 980178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さんの瞳……。<line-break>吸いこまれそうです…。<line-break>なんでも見通すような深い色……。　<line-break>きっと、冒険で色々なものを<line-break>見聞きしたからでしょうね。<line-break>私、頭のいい人って、あこがれます。<end>"
EN: "Your eyes, <value:$28>... I feel as if I could fall right into them. Such a deep color, as if they can see through anything... It must be because of everything you've seen and heard on your adventures. I admire clever people.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨의 눈동자……. 빨려 들어갈 것 같아요…. 무엇이든 꿰뚫어 보는 듯한 깊은 색……. 분명 모험을 하며 여러 가지를 보고 들었기 때문이겠죠. 저, 머리 좋은 사람을 동경해요.<end>"

## 980179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの、いえ、そうじゃなくって…。<line-break>ごめんなさい、私ったらまた<line-break>変なこと言っちゃって……。<end>"
EN: "Oh! Um, no, that's not what I meant... I'm sorry. There I go saying strange things again...<end>"
KO: "아, 아니, 그게 아니라…. 죄송해요, 저 또 이상한 말을 해 버렸네요…….<end>"

## 980180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふっ…なんだかうれしいです。<line-break>冒険に出る前と同じ、やさしい<line-break><value:$28>さんの顔……。<line-break>もし冒険がつらくて、こ～んな<line-break>しかめっつらになってたりしたら<line-break>どうしようって思ってました。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... This makes me so happy. You still have the same kind face you did before you left, <value:$28>. I was worried your adventures might be so hard that you'd come back with a great big scowl like this.<end>"
KO: "후후… 왠지 기뻐요. 모험을 떠나기 전과 똑같이 다정한 <value:$28> 씨 얼굴…. 모험이 너무 힘들어서 잔뜩 찌푸린 얼굴이 되어 있으면 어쩌나 했거든요.<end>"

## 980181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、やっぱり<value:$28>さんは<line-break><value:$28>さんでした。<line-break>私、うれしいです。<end>"
EN: "But <value:$28> really is still <value:$28>. I'm so glad.<end>"
KO: "그래도 역시 <value:$28> 씨는 <value:$28> 씨였어요. 저, 기뻐요.<end>"

## 980182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの……、<line-break><value:$28>さん……。<end>"
EN: "Um... <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "저, 저기…… <value:$28>님…….<end>"

## 980183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、いえ、なんでもないんです。<line-break>………………、<line-break>また、冒険に出られるんですよね？<end>"
EN: "Oh, no, it's nothing... You're going off on another adventure, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "아, 아니요, 아무것도 아니에요. ………………, 또 모험을 떠나실 거죠?<end>"

## 980184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですよね……。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あ、いえ、頑張ってください。<line-break>私も、応援してますからね。<end>"
EN: "Of course you are... Oh, never mind. Good luck. I'll be cheering you on.<end>"
KO: "그렇죠……. 아, 아니에요. 힘내세요. 저도 응원하고 있으니까요.<end>"

## 980185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec098-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん……、<line-break>絶対、また来てください。<line-break>私、待ってますから………。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... please, you must come back again. I'll be waiting...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님……, 꼭 다시 와 주세요. 저, 기다릴게요……….<end>"

## 990001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "や、やめてください！<end>"
EN: "P-please stop!<end>"
KO: "그, 그만해 주세요!<end>"

## 990002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいじゃねえかあ。<line-break>へへへ、へへへ…。<end>"
EN: "What's the harm? Heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "좋잖아아. 헤헤헤, 헤헤헤….<end>"

## 990003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "困ります、お客さん。<line-break>いや！　触らないで！<end>"
EN: "Please, you can't do that. No! Don't touch me!<end>"
KO: "곤란합니다, 손님. 싫어! 만지지 마세요!<end>"

## 990004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何しやがる！<end>"
EN: "Wh-what the hell are you doing?!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭘 하는 거야!<end>"

## 990005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひ、ひいい！<end>"
EN: "E-eek!<end>"
KO: "히, 히익!<end>"

## 990006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールの入り口まで来たとき　　　<line-break>君の悲鳴が聞こえた気がした。<line-break>　<line-break>だから、そこから全速力で走ってきた！<line-break>間に合ってよかった！<line-break>フェルムちゃん！　大丈夫かい！<end>"
EN: "I thought I heard you scream just as I reached the entrance to Rostorl. So I ran here as fast as I could! Thank goodness I made it in time! Ferme! Are you all right?<end>"
KO: "로스톨 입구까지 왔을 때 네 비명이 들린 것 같았어. 그래서 거기서 전속력으로 달려왔지! 늦지 않아서 다행이야! 페름! 괜찮아?<end>"

## 990007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様の愛するフェルムちゃんに<line-break>酔ってからむとは不埒な奴！<line-break>許せん！<end>"
EN: "You drunken lout, how dare you harass my beloved Ferme! I won't stand for it!<end>"
KO: "내가 사랑하는 페름에게 취해서 시비를 걸다니 불경한 녀석! 용서 못 해!<end>"

## 990008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん…。<line-break>い、いらっしゃいませ…。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>あ、べ、別になんでもないんです。<line-break>し、仕事に戻らなくっちゃ…！<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>... W-welcome... N-no, nothing's wrong. I-I have to get back to work...!<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님…. 어, 어서 오세요…. 아, 아뇨, 아무것도 아니에요. 이, 일하러 돌아가야 해…!<end>"

## 990009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、<value:$28>。<line-break>酒場に来るんだったら<line-break>先に言っといてよ。　　　<line-break>あたし、食べ終わったじゃない。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. You should've told me you were coming to the tavern. I've already finished eating.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>. 술집에 올 거면 미리 말하지. 나 벌써 다 먹었잖아.<end>"

## 990010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーあ、<value:$28>と一緒に<line-break>ご飯食べたかったのになー。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ま、いいわ。<line-break>んじゃ、また後でね！<end>"
EN: "Aw, I wanted to have a meal with you, <value:$28>. Oh well. See you later!<end>"
KO: "아아, <value:$28>과 같이 밥 먹고 싶었는데. 뭐, 됐어. 그럼 나중에 또 봐!<end>"

## 990011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何か…、すごい格好を<line-break>なさってる方ですね。<line-break>　<line-break>スタイルはいいし、<line-break>肌もきれいだし、<line-break>かわいい感じの方ですけど…。<end>"
EN: "That's quite an outfit. Such a lovely figure, beautiful skin, and a rather sweet look, but...<end>"
KO: "뭔가… 굉장한 차림을 하신 분이네요. 몸매도 좋고 피부도 깨끗하고 귀여운 느낌이긴 한데….<end>"

## 990013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここの料理<line-break>ホント、おいしかったよ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>ボクのオススメはねえ、<line-break>ソルバ魚のカルパッチョと<line-break>ジオールスターの唐揚げかな。<end>"
EN: "The food here was really good! I'd recommend the Solba fish carpaccio and the fried Geolstar.<end>"
KO: "여기 요리 정말 맛있었어. 내가 추천하는 건 솔바어 카르파초와 지오올스타 튀김이야.<end>"

## 990014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちゃんと食べてみてよ！<line-break>すっごくおいしかったんだから。<line-break>じゃあね！<end>"
EN: "You have to try them! They were so good. See you!<end>"
KO: "제대로 먹어 봐! 정말 맛있었으니까. 그럼 안녕!<end>"

## 990015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とても、気持ちよく<line-break>食べてくださる方ですね。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>あれだけ食べても、<line-break>あんなにいいスタイルだなんて…<line-break>ちょっとうらやましいですね。<end>"
EN: "What a pleasure it is to watch someone eat with such gusto. To eat that much and still have such a lovely figure... I'm a little envious.<end>"
KO: "정말 맛있게 드시는 분이네요. 그렇게 많이 먹고도 저렇게 좋은 몸매라니… 조금 부러워요.<end>"

## 990016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、<value:$28>…<line-break>今から食事かしら？　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>あなたがここで食事するのだったら<line-break>待ち合わせて２人で食事したかったわ。<line-break>ここのテラネ茄子はいけてるのよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>... Are you about to eat? If I'd known you were dining here, I'd have arranged to meet you so we could eat together. The Terane eggplant here is quite good.<end>"
KO: "어머, <value:$28>… 지금부터 식사하려고? 네가 여기서 먹을 거였으면 기다렸다가 둘이 같이 먹고 싶었는데. 여기 테라네 가지는 꽤 맛있어.<end>"

## 990017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルで食べるよりも<line-break>ロストールで食べる方が<line-break>おいしいなんて、おかしいわね。<line-break>それじゃ、私は行くわ。<line-break>今度、機会があれば、<line-break>２人で食事したいわね。<end>"
EN: "It's strange that the food tastes better in Rostorl than it does in Dyneskal. Well, I should be going. If the opportunity arises, I'd like to dine with you sometime.<end>"
KO: "딩갈에서 먹는 것보다 로스톨에서 먹는 게 더 맛있다니 이상하네. 그럼 난 갈게. 다음에 기회가 되면 둘이서 식사하고 싶네.<end>"

## 990018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ものすごく、おきれいな方ですよね。<line-break>背もスラッとして、物腰も上品で…。<line-break>　<line-break>それに、あの…、なんていうか<line-break>ものすごい…胸…ですよね。<line-break>なんか…圧倒されちゃいますよね。<end>"
EN: "Such a strikingly beautiful person, don't you think? Tall and slender, with such elegant manners... And, well, how should I put it... that chest is simply enormous. It's rather overwhelming, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "정말 아름다운 분이시죠. 키도 훤칠하고 몸가짐도 우아하고…. 그리고 그… 뭐랄까, 정말… 가슴이… 대단하죠. 왠지 압도당하잖아요.<end>"

## 990019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>だ。<line-break>声をかけようかな…。<line-break>そうしよっと！<line-break>あ、<value:$28>、<line-break>とっても、会いたかったの。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Oh, there's <value:$28>. Maybe I'll go say hello... Yes, I think I will! Oh, <value:$28>! I've really been wanting to see you!<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>이다. 말 걸어 볼까…. 그래야지! 아, <value:$28>, 정말 보고 싶었어.<end>"

## 990020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だって、ここの料理<line-break>とってもおいしいんだもん！<line-break>一緒に食べられたらなって思ったの！<end>"
EN: "It's just that the food here is so good! I was hoping we could eat together!<end>"
KO: "여기 음식이 정말 맛있잖아! 같이 먹을 수 있으면 좋겠다고 생각했어!<end>"

## 990021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、私、行くわね。<end>"
EN: "I'll be going, then.<end>"
KO: "그럼 나, 갈게.<end>"

## 990022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ものすごく、元気で<line-break>かわいらしい方ですよね。<line-break>　<line-break>どこか、小動物っぽくって<line-break>ちょっとしたことで<line-break>人の気を引くのがうまいみたい。<end>"
EN: "What a lively, adorable person―almost like a little animal, and apparently very good at drawing people's attention with the slightest little thing.<end>"
KO: "정말 활기차고 귀여운 분이죠. 어딘가 작은 동물 같고 사소한 일로 사람의 관심을 끄는 데 능숙한 것 같아요.<end>"

## 990041

EN: ""

## 990042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここ、あなたの行きつけの店だったの？<line-break>だーから、おいしくなかったのねー。<end>"
EN: "So this is your usual place? No wonder the food was so awful.<end>"
KO: "여기, 네 단골 가게였어? 그러니까 맛이 없었던 거구나.<end>"

## 990043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "味？　最悪ねー。<line-break>一言で言えば塩と油って感じかしら？<line-break>　<line-break>世界は驚きに満ちているって言う<line-break>あなたの言葉を、ホントに<line-break>今日は思い知らされたわー。<end>"
EN: "The taste? Dreadful. If I had to sum it up, I'd say salt and grease. You always say the world is full of surprises, and today I truly learned just how right you were.<end>"
KO: "맛? 최악이네. 한마디로 소금과 기름 맛이랄까? 세상은 놀라움으로 가득하다는 네 말을 오늘 정말 실감했어.<end>"

## 990044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こーんなものを食べて生きている<line-break>生物がいるんですもの。<line-break>ホントにまったくオドロキよねー。<end>"
EN: "To think there are creatures that live on food like this. It truly is astonishing.<end>"
KO: "이런 걸 먹고 사는 생물이 있잖아. 정말 놀랍다니까.<end>"

## 990045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それは、その、全部食べなきゃ、<line-break>店の人に悪いと思ったからよぉ！<line-break>　<line-break>別に、料理がおいしかったわけじゃ<line-break>絶対、絶対、絶対、絶対、ないわ！<line-break>何、細かいところ指摘してるのよ！<end>"
EN: "I-I only ate it all because I would've felt bad for the people here! It wasn't because the food was good. Absolutely, absolutely, absolutely, absolutely not! Why are you picking at every little detail?<end>"
KO: "그, 그건, 남기면 가게 사람한테 미안할 것 같아서 다 먹은 거야! 절대로 요리가 맛있어서 그런 건 아니야! 절대, 절대, 절대, 절대 아니라고! 왜 그런 사소한 걸 지적하는 거야!<end>"

## 990046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうね。<line-break>確かに堕落ね。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>人間ごときに気を使うなんて、　<line-break>高貴で優雅なエルフである<line-break>アタクシらしくないわ…。<end>"
EN: "Yes... You're right. I've certainly fallen far. Showing consideration for mere humans is hardly becoming of a noble and graceful Elf such as myself...<end>"
KO: "…그래. 확실히 타락했네. 인간 따위에게 신경을 쓰다니, 고귀하고 우아한 엘프인 나답지 않아…<end>"

## 990047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でもでも、こうなってしまったのも<line-break>あなたのせいなんですからね！<line-break>セキニンとってほしいわ！<end>"
EN: "But, but it's your fault I've become like this! I want you to take responsibility!<end>"
KO: "하지만 이렇게 된 것도 당신 탓이잖아요! 책임져 줬으면 좋겠어요!<end>"

## 990048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セキニンっていう言葉が<line-break>聞こえたんですけど…、<line-break>セキニンっていったい…？<end>"
EN: "I thought I heard the word “responsibility”... What exactly do you mean by that?<end>"
KO: "'책임'이라는 말이 들렸는데요… 책임이란 대체…?<end>"

## 990049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そ、そうですよね！<line-break>そんなわけないですよね！<end>"
EN: "Y-yes, of course! It couldn't possibly mean that!<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇죠! 그럴 리 없죠!<end>"

## 990050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、<value:$28>！<line-break>また、酒場に来てるぅ！　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>もう！　<value:$28>ってば<line-break>目を離すとすぐこれなんだから！<line-break>ダメだよ。お酒なんか飲んじゃ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>! You're at the tavern again! Honestly, <value:$28>! The moment I take my eyes off you, you're right back at it! You shouldn't be drinking!<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>! 또 술집에 왔어! 정말, <value:$28>는 눈만 떼면 이렇다니까! 안 돼. 술 마시면!<end>"

## 990051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そぉお？<line-break>なら、いいんだけど。<line-break>それじゃ、ルルアンタ、行くね。<end>"
EN: "Really? Well, if you say so. Lulluantha's going now, then.<end>"
KO: "그래애? 그럼 됐지만. 그럼 루루안타, 갈게.<end>"

## 990052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、かわいいリルビーですね。<line-break>世話好きで、まるでお母さん気取り。<line-break>　<line-break>まあ、男の人って誰でも、<line-break>多少、母親とか子供とかに弱い…<line-break>そういう傾向、ありますものね。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. What a sweet little Lilubee. So fond of looking after people, like a little mother. Then again, men do tend to have a soft spot for mothers and children, at least to some degree.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 귀여운 릴비네요. 남 챙기길 좋아해서 꼭 엄마라도 된 것처럼 굴고. 뭐, 남자들은 누구나 어느 정도 어머니나 아이 같은 것에 약한… 그런 경향이 있잖아요.<end>"

## 990053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごちそうさん。<end>"
EN: "Thanks for the meal.<end>"
KO: "잘 먹었어.<end>"

## 990054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございましたー。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much!<end>"
KO: "감사합니다.<end>"

## 990055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>さん。<line-break>…それで、あの方とは、<line-break>どういうご関係なんですか？<end>"
EN: "Um, <value:$28>... What exactly is your relationship with that person?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 씨. …그래서 그분과는 어떤 관계인가요?<end>"

## 990056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あの方とは<line-break>どういうご関係なんですか？<end>"
EN: "What exactly is your relationship with that person?<end>"
KO: "…그분과는 어떤 관계인가요?<end>"

## 990057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、いらっしゃいませー！<end>"
EN: "Oh, welcome!<end>"
KO: "아, 어서 오세요!<end>"

## 990058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…で、ある男の子が、ある女の子と　　<line-break>キスする仲…なんです。<line-break>　<line-break>女の子はとても、その男の子のことが<line-break>好きなんです。その男の子に会えないと<line-break>不安で泣きたくなるくらいなんです。<end>"
EN: "So... there's a boy and a girl, and they... kiss. The girl loves the boy very much. When she can't see him, she gets so anxious she feels like crying.<end>"
KO: "…그래서 어떤 남자아이가 어떤 여자아이와 키스하는 사이…예요. 여자아이는 그 남자아이를 아주 좋아해요. 못 만나면 불안해서 울고 싶을 만큼요.<end>"

## 990059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それなのに、その男の子の方は<line-break>他の女の子と、すごくいい雰囲気で<line-break>しゃべるんです。…その女の子の前で。<line-break>あ、説明すると、その女の子って<line-break>わ、私の、と、友達なんですけど…<line-break>ガルドランさん、どう…思います？<end>"
EN: "But the boy talks to another girl as if the two of them are very close... right in front of her. Oh, I should explain. She's m-my f-friend. Girldlan, what do you... think?<end>"
KO: "그런데 그 남자애는 다른 여자애와 아주 좋은 분위기로 이야기해요. …바로 그 여자애 앞에서요. 아, 설명하자면 그 여자애는 제, 제 친구인데…. 갈드란씨는 어떻게… 생각하세요?<end>"

## 990060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、恋する男の風上にも置けん奴だ。<line-break>俺様が愛について槍で語ってやろう。<line-break>　<line-break>そう、俺様の伝説化決定済みの槍、<line-break>名付けて、愛の一撃必殺槍でな。<line-break>誰だい、そのハレンチな奴は？<end>"
EN: "Hmph. That scoundrel is unworthy to call himself a man in love. I'll teach him about love with my spear―yes, the spear already destined to become legend! I call it the Spear of Love's Certain Death! Who is this shameless wretch?<end>"
KO: "흥, 사랑에 빠진 남자라면 부끄러워해야 할 놈이군. 이 몸이 사랑에 대해 창으로 가르쳐 주지. 그래, 전설이 될 게 확정된 나님의 창, 이름하여 사랑의 일격필살창으로! 누구야, 그 파렴치한 녀석은?<end>"

## 990062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フェルムちゃん！　君はやさしい！　<line-break>そんなクズ野郎すらかばうなんて！<line-break>　<line-break>そのやさしさを持つ君こそ、<line-break>勇者の口づけを受ける資格がある！<line-break>さあ、俺様の唇を存分に吸ってくれ！<end>"
EN: "Ferme! You are too kind! Defending even a worthless cur like that! A woman of such kindness is worthy of a Hero's kiss! Come, drink your fill from my lips!<end>"
KO: "페름 양! 넌 상냥해! 저런 쓰레기까지 감싸다니! 그 상냥함을 지닌 너야말로 용사의 입맞춤을 받을 자격이 있다! 자, 나님의 입술을 마음껏 빨아 줘!<end>"

## 990063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても、フェルムちゃんの<line-break>友達も不幸だ。そんなクズ野郎、<line-break>捨ててしまえばいいのに…。<line-break>そう、彼女も、フェルムちゃんのように<line-break>この愛の青春伝説の異名を持つ<line-break>俺様のような男に出会えていればな。<end>"
EN: "Still, I pity your friend, Ferme. She ought to cast aside a worthless cur like that. If only she too had met a man like me―the one they call the Legend of Love and Youth―just as you have.<end>"
KO: "그나저나 페름짱의 친구도 불행하군. 그런 쓰레기 같은 놈은 버리면 될 텐데…. 그래, 그녀도 페름짱처럼 '사랑의 청춘전설'이라는 별명을 가진 이 몸 같은 남자를 만났다면 좋았을 것을.<end>"

## 990064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様にかぎって、<line-break>君を苦しめることはない。<line-break>俺様は誓えるさ！<line-break>さあ、フェルムちゃん！<line-break>愛溢れる俺様と出会った幸福を、<line-break>この接吻で思う存分、味わうがいい！<end>"
EN: "I would never make you suffer. I swear it! Come, Ferme! With this kiss, savor to the fullest your good fortune in meeting a man as full of love as I!<end>"
KO: "이 몸이 너를 괴롭힐 리 없어. 맹세할 수 있지! 자, 페름! 사랑이 넘치는 이 몸을 만난 행복을 이 입맞춤으로 마음껏 맛봐라!<end>"

## 990065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、いやっ！<end>"
EN: "N-no!<end>"
KO: "시, 싫어!<end>"

## 990066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%4感じるぜ！　フェルムちゃん！<line-break>君の愛を君の拳から！！<end>ふっ、いいパンチだ。<line-break>未来の勇者の妻になるにふさわしい！<end>い、痛い…。でも、<line-break>…こういう愛の形もいいかもしれない。<end>人は、ときに愛に臆病になり、<line-break>愛から逃げようとする。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、これしきのパンチでは<line-break>俺様の伝説化決定の愛からは<line-break>逃げられないぜ！<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%4I can feel it, Ferme! The love in your fist!<end>Hah! A fine punch. Truly worthy of the future wife of a Hero!<end>O-ow... But perhaps this form of love has its charms too.<end>At times, people grow afraid of love and try to flee from it. But a punch like that cannot save you from my love, already destined for legend!<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%4느껴진다! 페름 양! 네 주먹에서 네 사랑이!!<end>훗, 좋은 펀치다. 미래의 용자의 아내가 되기에 걸맞아!<end>아, 아파…. 하지만… 이런 사랑의 형태도 괜찮을지도 모르겠군.<end>사람은 때때로 사랑 앞에서 겁을 먹고 사랑으로부터 달아나려 하지. 하지만 이 정도 펀치로는 전설이 되기로 정해진 나님의 사랑에서 도망칠 수 없다!<end>"

## 990067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は、これでさよならだ。<line-break>また来るぜ！　フェルムちゃん！<end>"
EN: "Farewell for today. I'll be back, Ferme!<end>"
KO: "오늘은 이걸로 작별이다. 또 올게! 페름아!<end>"

## 990068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>あ、あの…なんでもないです！<line-break>それじゃっ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>... Um, it's nothing! Goodbye!<end>"
KO: "…아, <value:$28>님. 저, 저기… 아무것도 아니에요! 그럼 이만!<end>"

## 990069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "久しぶりね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "It's been a long time, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "오랜만이네, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 990070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私…<line-break>あれから、カルラ様に助けられて…<line-break>カルラ様の配下になったの。　　<line-break>今の私はディンガル帝国黒鎧騎士、<line-break>東方攻略軍所属、青竜軍副将<line-break>アイリーン・エルメスよ。<end>"
EN: "I... After that, Lady Karula saved me, and I entered her service. Now I'm Irene Hermes, a Black-Armored Knight of the Dyneskal Empire, serving with the Eastern Campaign Army as deputy commander of the Azure Dragon Army.<end>"
KO: "나… 그 뒤 카를라 님께 도움받고… 카를라 님의 부하가 됐어. 지금 나는 딩갈 제국 흑갑기사, 동방공략군 소속 청룡군 부장 아이린 에르메스야.<end>"

## 990071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからはお互い、<line-break>それぞれの夢を追いましょ。<line-break>それじゃ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "From now on, let's each pursue our own dream. Goodbye, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "이제부터는 서로 각자의 꿈을 좇아요. 그럼, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 990072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しょせん、人間はひとりよ。<line-break>いつまでも、一緒にいられないの。<end>"
EN: "In the end, each of us is alone. We can't stay together forever.<end>"
KO: "결국 인간은 혼자야. 언제까지나 함께 있을 수는 없어.<end>"

## 990073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "久しぶりね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "It's been a long time, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "오랜만이네, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 990074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、カルラ様の配下になったの。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>今の私はディンガル帝国黒鎧騎士、<line-break>東方攻略軍所属、青竜軍副将<line-break>アイリーン・エルメスよ。<end>"
EN: "I've entered Lady Karula's service. Now I'm Irene Hermes, a Black-Armored Knight of the Dyneskal Empire, serving with the Eastern Campaign Army as deputy commander of the Azure Dragon Army.<end>"
KO: "나, 칼라 님의 부하가 됐어. 지금의 나는 딘갈 제국 흑갑기사, 동방공략군 소속 청룡군 부장 아이린 에르메스야.<end>"

## 990075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと、夢がかなったの。<line-break>どうしても、あなたに伝えたくて…。<end>"
EN: "At last, my dream has come true. I had to tell you...<end>"
KO: "드디어 꿈이 이루어졌어. 꼭 너한테 말하고 싶었어….<end>"

## 990076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと、夢がかなったの。<line-break>それじゃ。<end>"
EN: "At last, my dream has come true. Goodbye.<end>"
KO: "드디어 꿈이 이루어졌어. 그럼.<end>"

## 990077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>こんなところに来るなんて<line-break>どういうつもり？<line-break>今の私たちは敵同士なのよ！？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... What are you doing here? We're enemies now!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 이런 곳에 오다니 무슨 생각이야? 지금 우리는 적이나 다름없다고!?<end>"

## 990078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…早く、行きなさい。<line-break>今回だけは見逃してあげる。<line-break>もう私の前に姿を見せないで。<end>"
EN: "Go. Now. I'll let you go this once. Don't ever show yourself before me again.<end>"
KO: "…빨리 가. 이번만은 봐줄게. 다시는 내 앞에 모습을 보이지 마.<end>"

## 990079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あなたも懲りない人ね。<line-break>同じことを何回も言わせないで。<line-break>　<line-break>私たちは逆の道を歩いているの。<line-break>もう、ここには来ないで。<line-break>次は見逃してあげないから。<end>"
EN: "You never learn, do you? Don't make me tell you again. We've chosen different paths. Don't come back here. Next time, I won't let you go.<end>"
KO: "…당신도 참 질리지 않는 사람이네. 같은 말 몇 번이나 하게 하지 마. 우리는 반대의 길을 걷고 있어. 이제 여기 오지 마. 다음엔 봐주지 않을 테니까.<end>"

## 990080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなことになるなんて…<line-break>思ってもみなかった…。<end>"
EN: "I never imagined it would come to this...<end>"
KO: "이런 일이 될 줄은… 생각도 못 했어….<end>"

## 990082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル帝国青竜軍副将として、<line-break><value:$28>、お前を討つ！<end>"
EN: "As deputy commander of the Dyneskal Empire's Azure Dragon Army, I will strike you down, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "딘갈 제국 청룡군 부장으로서, <value:$28>, 너를 쓰러뜨리겠다!<end>"

## 990084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…行きなさい。<line-break>次はないわ。<end>"
EN: "Go. There won't be another chance.<end>"
KO: "…가. 다음은 없어.<end>"

## 990085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 990086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>こんな所で何をしているの！　<line-break>　<line-break>早く出ていきなさい。<line-break>今回だけは見逃してあげる。<line-break>もう私の前に顔を出さないで。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! What are you doing here? Get out, now. I'll let you go this once. Don't ever show your face before me again.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 이런 곳에서 뭐 하고 있는 거야! 빨리 나가. 이번만은 봐 줄게. 다시는 내 앞에 나타나지 마.<end>"

## 990087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ほんっとに<line-break>いつまでたっても子供なんだから！<end>"
EN: "Honestly, you never stop being such a child!<end>"
KO: "…정말이지 언제까지나 어린애라니까!<end>"

## 990089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたはロストールの貴族。<line-break>私はディンガルの青竜軍副将。　　　　<line-break>もう昔の私たちではないの。<line-break>早く出ていきなさい。<line-break>今回だけは見逃してあげる。<line-break>もう私の前に顔を出さないで。<end>"
EN: "You're a noble of Rostorl. I'm deputy commander of Dyneskal's Azure Dragon Army. We're not who we used to be. Get out, now. I'll let you go this once. Don't ever show your face before me again.<end>"
KO: "당신은 로스톨의 귀족. 나는 딘갈의 청룡군 부장. 이제 예전의 우리가 아니야. 빨리 나가. 이번만은 봐줄게. 다시는 내 앞에 나타나지 마.<end>"

## 990090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 990091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたも懲りない人ね、ノーブル伯？　<line-break>同じことを何回も言わせないで。<line-break>　<line-break>私たちはまったく逆の道を歩いている。<line-break>今の私は、昔のアイリーンではないの。<line-break>…もう、ここに来ないで！<end>"
EN: "You never learn, do you, Count of Noble? Don't make me tell you again. We've chosen completely opposite paths. I'm not the Irene you once knew. Please, don't come back!<end>"
KO: "당신도 참 안 질리네요, 노블 백작? 같은 말 몇 번이나 하게 하지 마. 우리는 완전히 반대 길을 걷고 있어. 지금의 나는 옛날의 아이린이 아니야. …이제 여기 오지 마!<end>"

## 990092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…どうして、<line-break>どうしてあなたがノーブル伯なの…。<end>"
EN: "Why? Why did you have to become the Count of Noble...?<end>"
KO: "…왜, 왜 당신이 노블 백작인 거야….<end>"

## 990093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノーブル伯<value:$28>！<line-break>こんな所で何をしているの！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>早く出ていきなさい。<line-break>あなたはこんな所に来られるような<line-break>人ではないはずよ。<end>"
EN: "Count <value:$28> of Noble! What are you doing here? Get out, now. Someone in your position shouldn't be coming to a place like this.<end>"
KO: "노블 백작 <value:$28>! 이런 데서 뭘 하고 있는 거야! 빨리 나가. 당신은 이런 곳에 와도 되는 사람이 아니잖아.<end>"

## 990094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>どういうつもりで<line-break>ここに来ているのかしら？<line-break>今の私たちは敵同士も同然よ？<end>"

KO: "<value:$28>…. 무슨 생각으로 여기 온 거야? 지금 우리는 적이나 다름없어.<end>"

## 990095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたはゼネテス配下の将軍。<line-break>私はディンガルの青竜軍副将。<line-break>もう、昔の私たちではないの。<line-break>…早く、行きなさい。<line-break>今回だけは見逃してあげる。<line-break>もう私の前に姿を見せないで！<end>"
EN: "You're a general under Xenetes. I'm deputy commander of Dyneskal's Azure Dragon Army. We're not who we used to be. Go. Now. I'll let you go this once. Don't ever show yourself before me again!<end>"
KO: "당신은 제네테스 휘하의 장군. 나는 딩갈 청룡군 부장. 이제 예전의 우리가 아니야. …빨리 가. 이번만은 봐줄게. 다시는 내 앞에 나타나지 마!<end>"

## 990100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、何の用？<line-break>ただ、私に会いに来た、<line-break>なんて言わないわよね？<end>"

## 990101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お互いに目指しているものが<line-break>違うんだから。私だっていつまでも<line-break>あなたの面倒を見てられないの！<line-break>わかった？　わかったら、<line-break>あなたも早く私から卒業して、<line-break>一人前の冒険者になってよね！<end>"
EN: "We're after different things now. I can't keep looking after you forever! Understand? Then it's time you stopped relying on me and became a proper adventurer in your own right!<end>"
KO: "서로 목표로 하는 게 다르잖아. 나도 언제까지나 네 뒤치다꺼리를 해 줄 순 없어! 알겠어? 알았으면 너도 빨리 나한테서 졸업해서 한 사람 몫 하는 모험가가 돼.<end>"

## 990102

## 990103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、今度は何？<line-break>私は忙しいんだから、<line-break>いつまでも私をあてにされても…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... What is it this time, <value:$28>? I'm busy. You can't keep relying on me forever...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. <value:$28>, 이번엔 또 뭐야? 난 바쁜데 언제까지나 나한테 기대도….<end>"

## 990104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え、違うの？　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>そ、そう…。<line-break>　<line-break>…そ、それなら、何しに来たのよ？<line-break>こんな所に来るヒマがあるなら、<line-break>ギルドで仕事でもこなしたら！<end>"
EN: "What, that's not it? Oh. I-I see... Then what did you come for? If you have time to waste coming here, go take a job at the Guild!<end>"
KO: "어, 아니야? 그, 그래…. …그, 그럼 뭐 하러 온 거야? 이런 데 올 시간이 있으면 길드에서 일이라도 해!<end>"

## 990105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう私は守ってあげられないんだから、<line-break>ちゃんと剣の腕も磨くのよ。<line-break>それに…。<end>"
EN: "I can't protect you anymore, so make sure you keep honing your swordsmanship. And...<end>"
KO: "이제 내가 지켜 줄 수는 없으니까 검 실력도 제대로 갈고닦아. 그리고….<end>"

## 990106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…もういいわ。早く行きなさい。<line-break>あまり部外者に、この辺を<line-break>うろついてもらいたくないの。　<end>"
EN: "Never mind. Go, now. I don't want outsiders wandering around here.<end>"
KO: "…이제 됐어. 빨리 가. 외부인이 이 근처를 돌아다니는 건 별로 원치 않아.<end>"

## 990107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…気をつけるのよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"

## 990108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…せっかくだけど、今は忙しいの。<line-break>時間があれば、手合わせぐらいなら<line-break>かまわないけれど…。<end>"

## 990114

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたはゼネテス配下の将軍。<line-break>私はディンガルの青竜軍副将。<line-break>もう、昔の私たちではないわ。<line-break>…早く、行きなさい。<line-break>今回だけは見逃してあげる。<line-break>もう私の前に姿を見せないで！<end>"
EN: "You're a general under Xenetes. I'm deputy commander of Dyneskal's Azure Dragon Army. We're not who we used to be. Go. Now. I'll let you go this once. Don't ever show yourself before me again!<end>"
KO: "당신은 제네테스 휘하의 장군. 나는 딩갈 청룡군 부장. 이제 예전의 우리가 아니야. …빨리 가. 이번만은 봐줄게. 다시는 내 앞에 나타나지 마!<end>"

## 990130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたも懲りない人ね？<line-break>同じことを何回も言わせないで。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>私たちはまったく逆の道を歩いている。<line-break>今の私は、昔のアイリーンではないの。<line-break>…もう、ここには来ないで！<end>"
EN: "You never learn, do you? Don't make me tell you again. We've chosen completely opposite paths. I'm not the Irene you once knew. Don't ever come back here!<end>"
KO: "당신도 정말 질리지 않네? 같은 말을 몇 번이나 하게 하지 마. 우리는 완전히 반대 길을 걷고 있어. 지금의 나는 옛날 아이린이 아니야. …이제 여기 오지 마!<end>"

## 990131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…どうして、<line-break>どうしてあなたがゼネテスの配下に…。<end>"
EN: "Why? Why are you serving under Xenetes...?<end>"
KO: "…왜, 왜 당신이 제네테스의 부하로….<end>"

## 990132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールの将、<value:$28>！<line-break>こんな所で何をしている！<line-break>早く出ていきなさい。<line-break>あなたはこんな所に来ていい<line-break>人間ではないはずよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, general of Rostorl! What are you doing here? Get out, now. This isn't a place you should be.<end>"
KO: "로스톨의 장군, <value:$28>! 이런 곳에서 뭘 하고 있는 거야! 빨리 나가. 당신은 이런 곳에 와도 될 사람이 아니잖아.<end>"

## 990133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、何の用かしら？<line-break>あなたも、青竜軍に加わりたいの？<line-break>　<line-break>それなら、私がカルラ様との間を<line-break>取り持ってあげるわ。<line-break>あなたなら立派な騎士になるもの。<end>"
EN: "What brings you here, <value:$28>? Do you want to join the Azure Dragon Army too? If so, I'll put in a word with Lady Karula for you. You'd make a splendid knight.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 무슨 일이야? 너도 청룡군에 들어오고 싶어? 그렇다면 내가 카를라 님과 연결해 줄게. 너라면 훌륭한 기사가 될 테니까.<end>"

## 990134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、残念ね。あなたと私がいれば、<line-break>青竜軍もますます強くなれるのに。<line-break>　<line-break>あなたも、早く、落ち着ける場所が<line-break>見つかるといいわね。<line-break>それじゃあね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that's a shame. With both of us, the Azure Dragon Army could grow stronger still. I hope you find somewhere you can settle down soon. See you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그래, 아쉽네. 너와 내가 함께라면 청룡군도 더욱 강해질 텐데. 너도 빨리 마음 놓고 머물 곳을 찾으면 좋겠어. 그럼 잘 있어, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 990135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、どうしたの？<line-break>私に用？　それともカルラ様？<end>"
EN: "What is it, <value:$28>? Did you need me, or Lady Karula?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 왜 그래? 나한테 볼일이야? 아니면 칼라 님?<end>"

## 990136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何も用がないのなら、どうして　　　　<line-break>こんな所に来てるのよ。<line-break>　<line-break>あいにく、<line-break>私は次の戦いの準備で忙しいの。<line-break>今度、また冒険の話でも聞かせて。<end>"
EN: "If you don't need anything, why did you come all the way here? Unfortunately, I'm busy preparing for the next battle. You can tell me about your adventures another time.<end>"
KO: "아무 용건도 없으면 왜 이런 데 온 거야. 공교롭게도 난 다음 전투 준비로 바빠. 다음에 또 모험 이야기나 들려줘.<end>"

## 990137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、でも、また用もなく来てはダメよ。<line-break>ここは軍隊なんだから。公私の混同は<line-break>軍人の最もさけるべきことだもの。<line-break>それじゃあね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "But don't come back again without a reason. This is an army, after all. A soldier must never mix personal and official affairs. See you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, 그래도 볼일 없이 또 오면 안 돼. 여긴 군대니까. 공사를 구분 못 하는 건 군인이 가장 피해야 할 일이야. 그럼 또 봐, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 990138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、冒険の方はどう？<line-break>何かおもしろいことでもあった？<end>"
EN: "How are your adventures going, <value:$28>? Has anything interesting happened?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 모험은 어때? 뭔가 재미있는 일이라도 있었어?<end>"

## 990139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇ、そんなことがあったんだ。　　　<line-break>やるじゃない、<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>私の方も日々いろんな仕事があってね。<line-break>軍隊を構成するのは人間だもの。<line-break>マニュアル通りにはいかないわ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, really? Not bad, <value:$28>. I've got all sorts of work to deal with every day, too. An army is made up of people. Things don't always go according to the manual.<end>"
KO: "헤에, 그런 일이 있었구나. 제법인데, <value:$28>. 나도 매일 이런저런 일이 있어. 군대를 이루는 건 사람이니까 매뉴얼대로만 되진 않아.<end>"

## 990140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "問題にぶつかるたびに、<line-break>カルラ様のすごさを実感するわ。<line-break>私はまだまだだもの。<line-break>私も負けてられないわね。<line-break>それじゃあね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Every problem I face reminds me how remarkable Lady Karula is. I still have a long way to go. I'll have to work hard to keep up. See you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "문제에 부딪힐 때마다 카를라 님의 대단함을 실감해. 난 아직 멀었거든. 나도 질 수 없지. 그럼 안녕, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 990141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec099-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>じゃない、<line-break>じゃ、忙しいから。<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't <value:$28>. Sorry, I'm busy.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>잖아. 그럼 난 바쁘니까.<end>"

## 1000001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec100-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "久しぶりね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "It's been a while, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "오랜만이네, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1000002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec100-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、カルラ様の配下になったの。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>今の私はディンガル帝国黒鎧騎士、<line-break>東方攻略軍所属、青竜軍副将<line-break>アイリーン・エルメスよ。<end>"
EN: "I've entered Lady Karula's service. Now I'm Irene Hermes, a Black-Armored Knight of the Dyneskal Empire, serving with the Eastern Campaign Army as deputy commander of the Azure Dragon Army.<end>"
KO: "나, 칼라 님의 부하가 됐어. 지금의 나는 딘갈 제국 흑갑기사, 동방공략군 소속 청룡군 부장 아이린 에르메스야.<end>"

## 1000003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec100-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと、夢がかなったの。<line-break>どうしても、あなたに伝えたくて…。<end>"
EN: "My dream has finally come true. I just had to tell you...<end>"
KO: "드디어 꿈이 이루어졌어. 꼭 너한테 말하고 싶었어….<end>"

## 1000004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec100-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと、夢がかなったの。<line-break>それじゃ。<end>"
EN: "My dream has finally come true. Well, goodbye.<end>"
KO: "드디어 꿈이 이루어졌어. 그럼.<end>"

## 1010001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>帝国に弓ひく身でありながら<line-break>私の前に出てくるなんていい度胸ね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You have some nerve showing your face to me when you've taken up arms against the Empire.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 제국에 반기를 든 몸으로 내 앞에 나타나다니 배짱이 좋구나.<end>"

## 1010002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつまでも解放軍に味方してても<line-break>なんにもならないわよ。<line-break>私たちの方についたらどう？<end>"
EN: "You'll get nowhere if you keep siding with the Liberation Army. Why not join us instead?<end>"
KO: "언제까지 해방군 편을 들어도 아무 소용 없어. 우리 쪽으로 오는 게 어때?<end>"

## 1010003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう言うと思った。<line-break>相変わらず、バカね。　<line-break>　<line-break>今度、私と目があったら<line-break>すぐに逃げることね。<line-break>…首が飛ぶわよ。<end>"
EN: "I knew you'd say that. You're as foolish as ever. Next time our eyes meet, you'd better run at once... or you'll lose your head.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 말할 줄 알았어. 여전히 바보네. 다음에 나와 눈이 마주치면 바로 도망쳐. …목이 날아갈 거야.<end>"

## 1010004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "早く、行きなさい。<end>"
EN: "Go. Quickly.<end>"
KO: "빨리 가세요.<end>"

## 1010005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ほんっとに<line-break>しょうがない…奴。<end>"
EN: "Honestly... what am I going to do with you?<end>"
KO: "…정말 어쩔 수 없는… 녀석.<end>"

## 1010006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>、<line-break>まだ、解放軍に味方してるの？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Are you still siding with the Liberation Army?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>, 아직도 해방군 편을 들고 있어?<end>"

## 1010007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>。<line-break>時勢を見る目は持つべきね。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>いつまでも昔のまま、なんてことは<line-break>ないのよ。時の流れの速さを嘆いても<line-break>なんにもならないわ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You need to learn to read the times. Nothing stays as it was forever. Lamenting how quickly the world changes won't do you any good.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>. 시대의 흐름을 보는 눈은 가져야 해. 언제까지나 예전 그대로일 수는 없어. 시간의 흐름이 빠르다고 한탄해 봐야 소용없어.<end>"

## 1010008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私たちがなんらかの形で<line-break>決着をつけるときが必ず来るわ。<line-break>それまで、せいぜい死なないでね。<end>"
EN: "One way or another, the day will come when we have to settle this. Until then, do try not to die.<end>"
KO: "…우리도 어떤 형태로든 결판을 낼 때가 반드시 올 거야. 그때까지는 죽지 말고 살아 있어.<end>"

## 1010009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…皮肉なものね、<line-break>運命なんて。<end>"
EN: "Fate is ironic, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "…운명이라는 것도 참 얄궂네.<end>"

## 1010010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>こんな所で何をしている！　<line-break>　<line-break>…早く出ていきなさい！<line-break>今回だけは見逃してあげる。<line-break>もう、私の前に顔を出さないで！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! What are you doing here? Get out, now! I'll let you go this once. Don't ever show your face to me again!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 이런 곳에서 뭐 하는 거야! …빨리 나가! 이번만은 봐줄게. 다시는 내 앞에 나타나지 마!<end>"

## 1010011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>どういうつもりでここに来ているの？<line-break>次の私たちの目標は<line-break>わかっているはずよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... What were you thinking, coming here? You know what our next target is.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 무슨 생각으로 여기에 온 거야? 다음 우리 목표는 알고 있을 텐데.<end>"

## 1010012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールはもう目と鼻の先。<line-break>無駄な抵抗などせずに、<line-break>さっさと降伏することね。<line-break>その方が、犠牲者も少なくなるわ。<line-break>あなたも、命を失わなくて<line-break>すむでしょうし。<end>"
EN: "Rostorl is almost within our reach. Don't put up a pointless resistance. Surrender now. That will mean fewer casualties, and it may save your life as well.<end>"
KO: "로스톨은 이제 코앞이야. 쓸데없는 저항 따윈 하지 말고 빨리 항복해. 그 편이 희생자도 줄어들 거야. 너도 목숨을 잃지 않아도 될 테고.<end>"

## 1010013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>時の流れはあなたが考えているより<line-break>もっと早いものよ。<line-break>よく考えてみることね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... The times move far more quickly than you realize. Think carefully about that.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>. 시간의 흐름은 네가 생각하는 것보다 훨씬 빨라. 잘 생각해 봐.<end>"

## 1010014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ほんっとに<line-break>しょうがない、奴…。<end>"
EN: "Honestly... what am I going to do with you?<end>"
KO: "…정말 어쩔 수 없는 녀석….<end>"

## 1010015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>あなたも懲りない人ね、ノーブル伯？<line-break>いつまでも昔のよしみが通用すると<line-break>思わないでもらいたいわ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You never learn, do you, Count of Noble? Don't think you can rely on our old friendship forever.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 당신도 참 끈질기네, 노블 백작? 언제까지나 옛정이 통할 거라 생각하지 않았으면 해.<end>"

## 1010016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちが同じ道を歩くことは<line-break>もうあり得ない。<line-break>あなたが、今の道を捨てない限り…。<line-break>降伏する気なら、<line-break>私がカルラ将軍との間を<line-break>取り持ってあげてもいいけど…？<end>"
EN: "We can never walk the same path again, not unless you abandon the path you're on. If you're willing to surrender, I could speak to General Karula on your behalf...?<end>"
KO: "우리가 같은 길을 걷는 일은 이제 없어. 당신이 지금의 길을 버리지 않는 한…. 항복할 생각이라면 내가 칼라 장군과 사이를 주선해 줘도 되는데…?<end>"

## 1010017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…早く行きなさい。<line-break>戦場で運命の女神が教えてくれるわ。<line-break>どちらの道が正しかったのか。<end>"
EN: "Go, quickly. On the battlefield, the Goddess of Fate will reveal which path was right.<end>"
KO: "…빨리 가. 전장에서 운명의 여신이 가르쳐 줄 거야. 어느 길이 옳았는지.<end>"

## 1010018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノーブル伯<value:$28>！<line-break>こんな所で何をしている！　<line-break>　<line-break>早く出ていきなさい！<line-break>こんな所に来ていい身分だと<line-break>思っているの？<end>"
EN: "Count <value:$28> of Noble! What are you doing here? Get out, now! Do you think someone in your position has any right to come here?<end>"
KO: "노블 백작 <value:$28>! 이런 곳에서 뭘 하는 겁니까! 빨리 나가세요! 이런 곳에 와도 되는 신분이라고 생각합니까?<end>"

## 1010019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほんっとに<line-break>いつまでたっても子供なんだから！<end>"
EN: "Honestly, you'll always be a child!<end>"
KO: "정말이지 언제까지나 어린애라니까!<end>"

## 1010020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1010026

## 1010030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>お互いに目指しているものが違うの。<line-break>だから、あなたは早く私から卒業して<line-break>あなたの目指しているものを…<end>"

## 1010031

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…빨리 가.<end>"

## 1010032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…がんばってね、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Good luck, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…힘내, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1010033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、私はあなたを守れないの。<line-break>もう、私は…。<end>"
EN: "I can't protect you anymore. I...<end>"
KO: "이제 난 너를 지켜 줄 수 없어. 이제 난….<end>"

## 1010034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…もういいわ。<line-break>早く行きなさい。<end>"
EN: "Enough. Go, quickly.<end>"
KO: "…이제 됐어. 어서 가.<end>"

## 1010035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…気をつけるのよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Take care, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…조심해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1010036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…せっかくだけど、今は忙しいの。<line-break>時間があれば、手合わせぐらいなら<line-break>かまわないけれど…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Sorry, but I'm busy right now. If I had time, I wouldn't mind sparring with you...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. …모처럼이지만 지금은 바빠. 시간이 있으면 대련 정도는 괜찮지만….<end>"

## 1010037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…もう、あなたは私抜きで<line-break>行かなければならないの。<end>"
EN: "From now on, you'll have to go on without me.<end>"
KO: "…이제 너는 나 없이 가야 해.<end>"

## 1010038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "立派な冒険者になってね、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Become a fine adventurer, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "훌륭한 모험자가 되렴, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1010039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私、軍務で忙しいの。<line-break>あなたも早く私から卒業して。<line-break>それじゃ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'm busy with my military duties. It's time you moved on from me. See you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…난 군무로 바빠. 너도 이제 나한테서 졸업해. 그럼, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1010040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>帝国に弓ひく身でありながら<line-break>私の前に出てくるなんて<line-break>いい度胸ね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You have some nerve showing your face to me when you've taken up arms against the Empire.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 제국에 반기를 든 몸으로 내 앞에 나타나다니 배짱이 좋구나.<end>"

## 1010041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつまでも解放軍に味方してても<line-break>なんにもならないわよ。<line-break>私たちの方についたらどう？<end>"
EN: "You'll get nowhere if you keep siding with the Liberation Army. Why not join us instead?<end>"
KO: "언제까지 해방군 편을 들어도 아무 소용 없어. 우리 쪽으로 오는 게 어때?<end>"

## 1010042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう言うと思った。<line-break>相変わらず、バカね。　<line-break>　<line-break>今度、私と目があったら<line-break>すぐに逃げることね。<line-break>…首が飛ぶわよ。<end>"
EN: "I knew you'd say that. You're as foolish as ever. Next time our eyes meet, you'd better run at once... or you'll lose your head.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 말할 줄 알았어. 여전히 바보네. 다음에 나와 눈이 마주치면 바로 도망쳐. …목이 날아갈 거야.<end>"

## 1010043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "早く、行きなさい。<end>"
EN: "Go. Quickly.<end>"
KO: "빨리 가세요.<end>"

## 1010044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>、<line-break>まだ、解放軍に味方してるの？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Are you still siding with the Liberation Army?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>, 아직도 해방군 편을 들고 있어?<end>"

## 1010045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>。<line-break>時勢を見る目くらいは持つべきね。<line-break>　<line-break>いつまでも昔のまま、なんてことは<line-break>ないのよ。時の流れの速さを嘆いても<line-break>なんにもならないわ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You need to learn to read the times. Nothing stays as it was forever. Lamenting how quickly the world changes won't do you any good.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>. 시대를 보는 눈 정도는 가져야 해. 언제까지나 옛날 그대로일 순 없어. 세월이 빠르다고 한탄해 봐야 아무 소용없어.<end>"

## 1010046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私たちがなんらかの形で<line-break>決着をつけるときが必ず来るわ。<line-break>それまで、せいぜい死なないでね。<end>"
EN: "One way or another, the day will come when we have to settle this. Until then, do try not to die.<end>"
KO: "…우리도 어떤 형태로든 결판을 낼 때가 반드시 올 거야. 그때까지는 죽지 말고 살아 있어.<end>"

## 1010047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>、あなたと私は<line-break>目指しているものが違うの！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you and I are striving for different things!<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>, 당신과 나는 목표로 하는 게 달라!<end>"

## 1010049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あなたとの決着をつけるのに<line-break>この場所はふさわしくないわ。　<line-break>　<line-break>その力があるなら、<line-break>私たちに挑んできなさい。<line-break>いつでも、踏みつぶしてあげる。<end>"
EN: "This isn't the place to settle things between us. If you're truly that strong, come challenge us. We'll crush you anytime.<end>"
KO: "…당신과 결판을 내기에 이곳은 어울리지 않아. 그 힘이 있다면 우리에게 도전해 와. 언제든 짓밟아 줄게.<end>"

## 1010050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>こんな所で何をしている！　<line-break>　<line-break>早く出ていきなさい！<line-break>今回だけは見逃すけど<line-break>もう私の前に顔を出すないで！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! What are you doing here? Get out, now! I'll let you go this once, but don't ever show your face to me again!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 이런 곳에서 뭘 하고 있어! 빨리 나가! 이번만은 봐주겠지만 다시는 내 앞에 나타나지 마!<end>"

## 1010051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…次は…ないわ。<end>"
EN: "There won't be a next time.<end>"
KO: "…다음은… 없어.<end>"

## 1010052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>どういうつもりでここに来ているの？<line-break>次の私たちの目標は<line-break>わかっているはず…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... What were you thinking, coming here? You know what our next target is...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 무슨 생각으로 여기에 온 거야? 다음 우리 목표는 알고 있을 텐데….<end>"

## 1010053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールはもう私たちの目と鼻の先。<line-break>無駄な抵抗などせずに、<line-break>さっさと降伏することね。<line-break>その方が、犠牲者も少なくなるわ。<line-break>あなたも、命を失わなくて<line-break>すむでしょうし。<end>"
EN: "Rostorl is almost within our reach. Don't put up a pointless resistance. Surrender now. That will mean fewer casualties, and it may save your life as well.<end>"
KO: "로스톨은 이제 코앞이야. 쓸데없이 저항하지 말고 빨리 항복해. 그래야 희생자도 줄고 당신도 목숨을 잃지 않을 테니까.<end>"

## 1010054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、それって<line-break>貴族の誇り…かしら？<end>"
EN: "Heh. Is that what you call a noble's pride?<end>"
KO: "후후, 그게 귀족의 긍지…인 걸까?<end>"

## 1010055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…決着をつける日を<line-break>楽しみにしているわ。<end>"
EN: "I'm looking forward to the day we settle this.<end>"
KO: "…결판을 낼 날을 기대하고 있을게.<end>"

## 1010056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>あなたも懲りない人ね、ノーブル伯？<line-break>いつまでも昔のよしみが通用すると<line-break>思わないでもらいたいわ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You never learn, do you, Count of Noble? Don't think you can rely on our old friendship forever.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 당신도 참 끈질기네, 노블 백작? 언제까지나 옛정이 통할 거라 생각하지 않았으면 해.<end>"

## 1010057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちが同じ道を歩くことは<line-break>もうあり得ない。<line-break>あなたが、今の道を捨てない限り…。<line-break>降伏するというのなら、<line-break>私がカルラ将軍との間を<line-break>取り持ってあげてもいいけど…？<end>"
EN: "We can never walk the same path again, not unless you abandon the path you're on. If you're willing to surrender, I could speak to General Karula on your behalf...?<end>"
KO: "우리가 같은 길을 걷는 일은 이제 있을 수 없어. 네가 지금의 길을 버리지 않는 한…. 항복하겠다면 내가 카를라 장군과 사이를 중재해 줄 수도 있는데…?<end>"

## 1010058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "埋めようがない溝があるのかしら？<line-break>私たちの間には…。<line-break>　<line-break>時の流れはあなたが考えているより<line-break>もっと早いものよ。<line-break>よく考えてみることね。<end>"
EN: "Is there a gulf between us that can never be bridged? The times move far more quickly than you realize. Think carefully about that.<end>"
KO: "메울 수 없는 골이 있는 걸까? 우리 사이에는… 시간의 흐름은 네가 생각하는 것보다 훨씬 빠른 법이야. 잘 생각해봐.<end>"

## 1010059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノーブル伯<value:$28>！<line-break>こんな所で何をしている！　<line-break>　<line-break>早く出ていきなさい！<line-break>こんな所に来ていい身分だと<line-break>思っているの？<end>"
EN: "Count <value:$28> of Noble! What are you doing here? Get out, now! Do you think someone in your position has any right to come here?<end>"
KO: "노블 백작 <value:$28>! 이런 곳에서 뭘 하는 겁니까! 빨리 나가세요! 이런 곳에 와도 되는 신분이라고 생각합니까?<end>"

## 1010060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度会うときは<line-break>戦場ね。<end>"
EN: "The next time we meet, it will be on the battlefield.<end>"
KO: "다음에 만날 땐 전장이겠네.<end>"

## 1010061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…あなたはゼネテスの配下よ。<line-break>どういうつもりでここに来たの？<line-break>次の私たちの目標がわからないの？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You serve under Xenetes. What were you thinking, coming here? Don't you know what our next target is?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. …당신은 제네테스의 부하잖아. 무슨 생각으로 여기 온 거야? 다음 목표가 뭔지 모르는 거야?<end>"

## 1010062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールはもう目と鼻の先。　　<line-break>無駄な抵抗などせずに、<line-break>さっさと降伏することね。<line-break>その方が、犠牲者も少なくなるわ。<line-break>あなたも命を失わなくて<line-break>すむでしょうし。<end>"
EN: "Rostorl is almost within our reach. Don't put up a pointless resistance. Surrender now. That will mean fewer casualties, and it may save your life as well.<end>"
