## 1010063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今度会うときは<line-break>戦場…というわけね。<end>"
EN: "So the next time we meet... it will be on the battlefield.<end>"
KO: "…다음에 만날 때는전장…이라는 거네.<end>"

## 1010064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>あなたも懲りない人ね。<line-break>いつまでも昔のよしみが通用すると<line-break>思わないでもらいたいわ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You never learn, do you? Don't think you can rely on our old friendship forever.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 당신도 참 끈질기네. 언제까지나 옛정이 통할 거라 생각하지 않았으면 해.<end>"

## 1010065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちが同じ道を歩くことは<line-break>もう、あり得ない。<line-break>あなた、今の道を捨てない限り…。<line-break>降伏するというのなら、<line-break>私がカルラ将軍との間を<line-break>取り持ってあげてもいいけど…。<end>"
EN: "We can never walk the same path again, not unless you abandon the path you're on. If you're willing to surrender, I could speak to General Karula on your behalf...<end>"

## 1010066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたと戦場で決着がつけられそうね。<line-break>…武人としてはうれしい答えだわ。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、一度よく考えてみるといいわ。<line-break>時の流れはあなたが考えているよりも<line-break>もっと早いものなのよ。<end>"
EN: "It seems we'll be able to settle this on the battlefield. As a warrior, I'm glad to hear that answer. But you should think it over carefully. The times move far more quickly than you realize.<end>"
KO: "당신과 전장에서 결판을 낼 수 있겠네. …무인으로서는 기쁜 대답이야. 하지만 한 번 잘 생각해 봐. 시간의 흐름은 네가 생각하는 것보다 훨씬 빠르니까.<end>"

## 1010067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールの<value:$28>！<line-break>こんな所で何をしている！　<line-break>早く出ていきなさい！<line-break>こんな所に来ていい身分だと<line-break>思っているの？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> of Rostorl! What are you doing here? Get out, now! Do you think someone in your position has any right to come here?<end>"
KO: "로스톨의 <value:$28>! 이런 곳에서 뭘 하고 있지! 어서 나가! 이런 곳에 와도 되는 신분이라고 생각해?<end>"

## 1010068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦場で会いましょ。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'll see you on the battlefield, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "전장에서 만나자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1010069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、何の用かしら？<line-break>あなたも、青竜軍に加わりたいの？　<line-break>　<line-break>それなら、私がカルラ様との間を<line-break>取り持ってあげるわ。<line-break>味方はひとりでも欲しいところなの。<end>"
EN: "What can I do for you, <value:$28>? Do you want to join the Azure Dragon Army too? If so, I'll speak to Lady Karula on your behalf. We need every ally we can get.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 무슨 일이야? 너도 청룡군에 들어오고 싶어? 그렇다면 내가 카를라 님과 연결해 줄게. 아군은 한 명이라도 더 필요하거든.<end>"

## 1010070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、残念ね。あなたと私がいれば、<line-break>青竜軍もますます強くなれるのに。<line-break>　<line-break>あなたも、早く、落ち着ける場所が<line-break>見つかるといいわね。<line-break>それじゃあね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that's a shame. With both of us, the Azure Dragon Army could grow stronger still. I hope you find somewhere you can settle down soon. See you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그래, 아쉽네. 너와 내가 함께라면 청룡군도 더욱 강해질 텐데. 너도 빨리 마음 놓고 머물 곳을 찾으면 좋겠어. 그럼 잘 있어, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1010071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、どうしたの？<line-break>私に用？　それともカルラ様？<end>"
EN: "What's the matter, <value:$28>? Did you need me, or Lady Karula?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 왜 그래? 나한테 볼일이야? 아니면 칼라 님?<end>"

## 1010072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何も用がないのなら、どうして　　　　<line-break>こんな所に来てるのかしら。<line-break>　<line-break>あいにく、私はこの前の戦いの処理で<line-break>忙しいの。今度、また冒険の話でも<line-break>聞かせて。<end>"
EN: "If you don't need anything, why are you here? I'm afraid I'm busy dealing with the aftermath of the last battle. Tell me about your adventures another time.<end>"
KO: "아무 용건도 없다면 왜 이런 곳까지 온 거지? 공교롭게도 난 지난 전투의 뒷처리로 바빠. 다음에 또 모험 이야기를 들려줘.<end>"

## 1010073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、用もないのに来てはダメよ。<line-break>ここは軍隊なんだから。公私の混同は<line-break>軍人の最もさけるべきことだもの。<line-break>それじゃあね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "But don't come here when you have no business here. This is an army. A soldier must never mix personal and official matters. See you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "하지만 볼일도 없는데 오면 안 돼. 여긴 군대니까. 공사를 구분하지 못하는 건 군인이 가장 피해야 할 일이야. 그럼 안녕, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1010074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、冒険の方はどう？<line-break>何かおもしろいことでもあった？<end>"
EN: "How are your adventures going, <value:$28>? Has anything interesting happened?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 모험은 어때? 뭔가 재미있는 일이라도 있었어?<end>"

## 1010075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇ、そんなことがあったの。　　　<line-break>やるわね、<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>私の方もようやく軍隊が<line-break>どういうものか、わかってきたわ。<line-break>でも、まだダメなところもあるわね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, really? Not bad, <value:$28>. I'm finally starting to understand how an army works too, though I've still got a ways to go.<end>"
KO: "헤에, 그런 일이 있었구나. 제법인데, <value:$28>. 나도 이제야 군대가 어떤 곳인지 조금 알 것 같아. 그래도 아직 부족한 점은 있네.<end>"

## 1010076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "問題にぶつかるたびに、カルラ様の<line-break>すごさに感心しちゃうわね。私より<line-break>年下なのに立派にこなしているもの。<line-break>私も負けてられないわ。<line-break>それじゃあね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Every time I run into a problem, I'm impressed all over again by Lady Karula. She's younger than I am, but she handles everything so capably. I can't afford to fall behind. See you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "문제가 생길 때마다 카를라 님의 대단함에 감탄하게 되네. 나보다 어린데도 훌륭히 해내니까. 나도 질 수 없지. 그럼 또 봐, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1010077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec101-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私、軍務で忙しいの。<line-break><value:$28>、それじゃ。<end>"
EN: "I'm busy with my military duties. See you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…나, 군무로 바빠. <value:$28>, 그럼 이만.<end>"

## 1020001

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（…閉まっているようだ。）<end>"

## 1020002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来てくれた、<value:$28>。<line-break>ちょうどいいタイミングだ。<line-break>ディンガル軍の部隊が動き出したらしい。　<line-break>もうじき、この平民街にやってくるだろう。<line-break>その前にけりを付けなくてはな。<end>"
EN: "Glad you could make it, <value:$28>. Your timing couldn't be better. It seems a unit of the Dyneskal Army has begun moving. They'll reach the commoners' quarter before long. We need to settle this before they do.<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다, <value:$28>. 마침 좋은 때야. 딘갈군 부대가 움직이기 시작한 모양이다. 곧 이 평민가로 올 거야. 그 전에 결판을 내야 한다.<end>"

## 1020003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先生。来ました！<line-break>ディンガル軍がこちらに向かってきます。<line-break>先頭にいるのは間違いなくアイリーンです。<end>"
EN: "Master! I'm here! The Dyneskal Army is headed this way. Irene is definitely leading them.<end>"
KO: "선생님. 왔습니다! 딩갈군이 이쪽으로 오고 있습니다. 선두에 있는 건 틀림없이 아이린입니다.<end>"

## 1020004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、行くとするか。<line-break><value:$28>、お前も来るだろう？<end>"
EN: "Then we'd better get going. You're coming too, aren't you, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "그럼 출발할까. <value:$28>, 너도 올 거지?<end>"

## 1020005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先生。<line-break>本当に２人だけで行くつもりですか？<line-break>いくらなんでも、危険すぎます。<end>"
EN: "Master, do you really mean to go with only the two of you? It's far too dangerous.<end>"
KO: "선생님. 정말 두 분만 가실 생각입니까? 아무리 그래도 너무 위험합니다.<end>"

## 1020006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいんだよ。俺の弟子の問題だからな。<line-break>それよりもお前ら、あとのことは頼んだぜ。<end>"
EN: "It's fine. This is my disciple's problem. More importantly, I'm counting on the rest of you to handle things here.<end>"
KO: "됐어. 내 제자의 문제니까. 그보다 너희, 뒤는 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1020007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先生が心配だ。<line-break>死に場所を捜しているような気がして…。<line-break>先生を、よろしく頼む。アイリーンもな。<end>"
EN: "I'm worried. I can't shake the feeling Master is looking for a place to die... Please look after Master. And Irene too.<end>"
KO: "선생님이 걱정돼. 죽을 곳을 찾는 것 같아서…. 선생님을 잘 부탁해. 아이린도.<end>"

## 1020008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先生が心配です。<line-break>何か死に場所を捜しているような雰囲気で…。<line-break>先生を、よろしくお願いします。<end>"
EN: "I'm worried about Master. It's almost as if Master is looking for a place to die... Please look after Master.<end>"
KO: "선생님이 걱정됩니다. 뭔가 죽을 자리를 찾고 계신 것 같은 분위기라서…. 선생님을 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1020009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "선생님을 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1020011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーン将軍。<line-break>全部隊、配置につきました。<line-break>ご命令があり次第、作戦を開始できます。<end>"
EN: "General Irene. All units are in position. We can begin the operation as soon as you give the order.<end>"
KO: "아이린 장군. 전 부대 배치를 마쳤습니다. 명령만 내리시면 작전을 개시할 수 있습니다.<end>"

## 1020012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ…わかった。<line-break>ご苦労。<end>"
EN: "Yes... Understood. Good work.<end>"
KO: "아아… 알았다. 수고했다.<end>"

## 1020013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーン将軍。本当にこの作戦を<line-break>実行してもよろしいのですか。<line-break>このままでは…<end>"
EN: "General Irene, are you certain we should carry out this operation? If this continues...<end>"
KO: "아이린 장군. 정말 이 작전을 실행해도 되는 겁니까. 이대로라면…<end>"

## 1020014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカなことを聞くな！<line-break>カルラ様の判断は正しい。<line-break>我らはそれに従うまでだ。<end>"
EN: "Don't ask such foolish questions! Lady Karula's judgment is sound. Our duty is simply to obey.<end>"
KO: "바보 같은 걸 묻지 마! 칼라 님의 판단은 옳다. 우리는 따르기만 하면 돼.<end>"

## 1020015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいおい。自分でも迷っているのに、<line-break>他人には信じろなんていうのは、<line-break>無茶な話なんじゃないか。<end>"
EN: "Now, now. You're wavering yourself, yet you expect everyone else to have faith? That's asking a bit much, don't you think?<end>"
KO: "이봐. 자기도 망설이면서 남한테는 믿으라고 하는 건 너무 억지 아니야?<end>"

## 1020016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、まさか…。<end>"
EN: "I-It can't be...<end>"
KO: "서, 설마….<end>"

## 1020017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せ、先生…。<end>"
EN: "M-Master...<end>"
KO: "서, 선생님….<end>"

## 1020018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーン、無茶なことはやめろ。<line-break>お前には、この作戦の指揮は無理だ。<line-break>それはお前が劣っているからではない。<end>"
EN: "Irene, don't do anything reckless. You can't lead this operation. Not because you lack ability.<end>"
KO: "아이린, 무모한 짓은 그만둬. 너에겐 이 작전의 지휘가 무리야. 네가 뒤떨어져서 그런 게 아니다.<end>"

## 1020019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何を…。<end>"
EN: "W-What are you...<end>"
KO: "무, 무슨….<end>"

## 1020020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前、カルラの下で<line-break>それなりに名を挙げているんだろ？<line-break>それにしては、お粗末な用兵じゃねえか。<line-break>お前には、心の優しいお前には、<line-break>無力な市民を、ましてや同朋を、一方的に<line-break>武力でねじ伏せるなんて無理なんだよ。<end>"
EN: "You've made quite a name for yourself under Karula, haven't you? For all that, this is some sorry generalship. You can't do this. You're too kindhearted to crush helpless civilians by force―especially your own people.<end>"
KO: "너, 카를라 밑에서 제법 이름을 날리고 있다며? 그런데 이 정도로 허술한 용병술이라니. 너한텐, 마음씨 착한 너한텐 힘없는 시민을, 하물며 동포를 일방적으로 무력으로 찍어누르는 건 무리야.<end>"

## 1020021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前、心が泣いてるじゃねえか。<line-break>そんなに自分を追い込むもんじゃない。　<line-break>痛々しくて見てられないぜ。<end>"
EN: "Your heart's crying out. Don't push yourself this hard. I can't stand to watch you suffer like this.<end>"
KO: "너, 마음이 울고 있잖아. 그렇게까지 자신을 몰아붙이지 마. 안쓰러워서 보고 있을 수가 없다.<end>"

## 1020022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、黙れ！<line-break>私は、東方攻略軍所属<line-break>青竜軍副将アイリーン・エルメス！<line-break>自分から出向いてくれて好都合だわ。<line-break>先生、そして、<value:$28>。<line-break>私たちは決着をつける運命にある！<end>"
EN: "S-Shut up! I am Irene Hermes, deputy commander of the Azure Dragon Army, Eastern Campaign Army! You've saved me the trouble by coming here yourselves. Master―and <value:$28>. We are destined to settle this once and for all!<end>"
KO: "다, 닥쳐! 나는 동방 공략군 소속 청룡군 부장 아이린 에르메스! 스스로 와 줬으니 잘됐군. 선생님, 그리고 <value:$28>. 우리는 결판을 낼 운명이야!<end>"

## 1020023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカ野郎！　運命なんて言葉に逃げるな！<line-break>お前は俺の弟子だ。<line-break>俺がもう一度、性根を叩き直してやる！<end>"
EN: "You damned fool! Don't hide behind words like destiny! You're my disciple. It's time I knocked some sense into you again!<end>"
KO: "바보 자식! 운명 같은 말로 도망치지 마! 넌 내 제자다. 내가 다시 한번 정신을 뜯어고쳐 주마!<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1020030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1020031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いったい、今まで、何を見てきたんだ！<line-break>お前の心の葛藤はなんなんだ！<line-break>お前が追い求めていたのはこんなものなのか！<line-break>そんなこともわからんような奴を<line-break>弟子にした覚えはねえ！<end>"
EN: "What the hell have you been looking at all this time? What is this struggle inside you? Is this really what you've been striving for? I don't remember taking on any disciple too blind to understand even that!<end>"
KO: "대체 지금까지 뭘 보고 온 거야! 네 마음의 갈등은 뭐였어! 네가 좇던 게 이런 거였나! 그런 것도 모르는 녀석을 제자로 삼은 기억은 없어!<end>"

## 1020032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う…えぐ…。<end>"
EN: "Ngh... hic...<end>"
KO: "으… 흐윽….<end>"

## 1020033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悲しいときに泣けないような奴が、<line-break>苦しいときに泣けないような奴が、<line-break>人のために何かできると思っているのか？<end>"
EN: "If you can't cry when you're sad, if you can't cry when you're hurting, do you really think you can do anything for anyone else?<end>"
KO: "슬플 때 울지도 못하는 녀석이, 괴로울 때 울지도 못하는 녀석이 남을 위해 뭔가 할 수 있다고 생각하냐?<end>"

## 1020034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぅ…あ…。<end>"
EN: "Uhh... ah...<end>"
KO: "으으… 아….<end>"

## 1020035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "너한텐 이 녀석이 있잖아. 울고 싶을 때는 실컷 울면 돼.<end>"

## 1020037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ～あ。でっけえ声だなあ。<line-break>これは、しばらく止まりそうにないな。　<line-break>…ま、これで大丈夫か。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>しばらく、アイリーンのお守りをしてな。<line-break>俺はディンガル軍の動向を見てくる。<end>"
EN: "Good grief, that's some voice. Doesn't sound like she'll be stopping anytime soon. Still, I think she'll be all right now. <value:$28>, look after Irene for a while. I'm going to see what the Dyneskal Army is doing.<end>"
KO: "아아, 목소리 크네. 당분간은 멈출 것 같지 않군. …뭐, 이걸로 괜찮겠지. <value:$28>. 잠시 아이린을 돌봐 줘. 난 딘갈군의 움직임을 보고 오겠다.<end>"

## 1020038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "泣きたいときには<line-break>思いっきり泣けばいいんだよ。<end>"
EN: "When you need to cry, just let it all out.<end>"
KO: "울고 싶을 때는 마음껏 울면 돼.<end>"

## 1020039

## 1020040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1020041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>俺はしばらく、アイリーンのお守りだ。<line-break>お前はディンガル軍の動向を見といてくれや。<line-break>頭を失ったんだ。<line-break>もう、とんずらするしかないだろうよ。<line-break>お前たちも行ってきてくれ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I'll be looking after Irene for a while. Keep an eye on the Dyneskal Army for me. They've lost their leader, so running away is about their only choice now. The rest of you go too.<end>"

## 1020042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml

## 1020048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーン。お前が負けたのは、　　　<line-break>技量が足りないからじゃない。<line-break>お前に迷いがあったからだ。<line-break>カルラのやり方への疑念が、そのまま、<line-break>お前の剣を迷わせているんだ。<end>"
EN: "Irene, you didn't lose because you lacked skill. You lost because you were torn. Your doubts about Karula's methods made your sword waver too.<end>"
KO: "아이린. 네가 진 건 기량이 부족해서가 아니야. 네게 망설임이 있었기 때문이지. 카를라의 방식에 대한 의심이 그대로 네 검을 흔들고 있어.<end>"

## 1020050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーン、帰ってこい。<line-break>お前に圧制者は似合わない。<end>"
EN: "Come back, Irene. Being an oppressor doesn't suit you.<end>"
KO: "아이린, 돌아와. 네겐 압제자가 어울리지 않아.<end>"

## 1020051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先生。私はもう戻れません。<line-break>ですが、これ以降、<line-break>街をねらうようなこともしません。<line-break>私は<line-break>騎士、アイリーン・エルメスですから。<end>"
EN: "Master, I can't go back anymore. But I won't threaten this city again. Because I am a knight: Irene Hermes.<end>"
KO: "선생님. 전 이제 돌아갈 수 없습니다. 하지만 앞으로는 마을을 노리는 일도 하지 않겠습니다. 저는 기사 아이린 에르메스니까요.<end>"

## 1020053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…やれやれ。ま、これで一安心だな。<line-break>あの目をしてれば大丈夫だろうよ。<line-break>あいつもあいつなりに考えを出せたようだ。<line-break>だが、あいつが俺たちの前に姿を見せるのは<line-break>もうないのかもしれないな。<line-break>さびしいか、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Good grief. Well, that's one worry off my mind. With that look in her eyes, she'll be all right. Seems she's found her own answer. But she may never show herself before us again. Are you going to miss her, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "휴, 이걸로 한시름 놓았군. 저 눈빛이라면 괜찮겠지. 녀석도 나름대로 답을 찾은 모양이야. 하지만 이제 우리 앞에 모습을 드러내지 않을지도 모르겠네. 쓸쓸하냐, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1020054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まぁ、いい。俺の青臭い弟子どもも、<line-break>ようやくひとり立ちできそうだからな。<line-break>戻るとするか。<end>"
EN: "Well, never mind. Looks like those green disciples of mine are finally ready to stand on their own. Let's head back.<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 됐다. 내 풋내기 제자들도 이제 겨우 독립할 수 있을 것 같으니 돌아갈까.<end>"

## 1020055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…やれやれ。ま、これで一安心だな。<line-break>あの目をしてれば大丈夫だろうよ。<line-break>あいつもあいつなりに考えを出せたようだ。<line-break>お前にも世話になったな。<line-break>ありがとうよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>それじゃあ、戻るとしようか。<end>"
EN: "Good grief. Well, that's one worry off my mind. With that look in her eyes, she'll be all right. Seems she's found her own answer. You helped too. Thanks, <value:$28>. Now, let's head back.<end>"
KO: "…이런. 뭐, 이걸로 한시름 놓았군. 저 눈빛이면 괜찮겠지. 저 녀석도 저 녀석 나름대로 답을 낸 모양이야. 너한테도 신세 졌군. 고맙다, <value:$28>. 그럼 돌아가 볼까.<end>"

## 1020056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>せめて、苦しまないように<line-break>殺してあげる。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... At least I'll make sure you don't suffer when I kill you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 적어도 고통스럽지 않게 죽여줄게.<end>"

## 1020057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え…ど、どうして…。<end>"
EN: "W-What...? Why...?<end>"
KO: "어… 왜, 왜….<end>"

## 1020058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカ野郎が…。<line-break>アイリーンも<value:$28>も、<line-break>俺の大事な弟子だろうが…。　　　　　<line-break>弟子のピンチを救うのも、<line-break>不出来な弟子をいさめるのも、<line-break>師匠の役目と昔っから決まってんだよ。<end>"
EN: "You damned fool... Irene and <value:$28> are both precious disciples of mine. Saving a disciple in peril and setting a wayward one straight―that's always been a master's duty.<end>"
KO: "바보 녀석…. 아이린도 <value:$28>도 내 소중한 제자잖아…. 제자가 위험할 때 구해 주는 것도, 못난 제자를 타이르는 것도 예부터 스승의 몫이라고 정해져 있다고.<end>"

## 1020059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せ、先生！<end>"
EN: "M-Master!<end>"
KO: "서, 선생님!<end>"

## 1020060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前には無理なんだよ、アイリーン。<line-break>心を鬼にする、なんてのはな。<end>"
EN: "You can't do it, Irene. You can't harden your heart like that.<end>"
KO: "너한텐 무리야, 아이린. 마음을 독하게 먹는다는 건 말이지.<end>"

## 1020061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しゃべらないで、先生！<line-break>今、医者を連れてきます！<end>"
EN: "Don't talk, Master! I'll get a doctor!<end>"
KO: "말하지 마세요, 선생님! 지금 의사를 데려올게요!<end>"

## 1020062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカ野郎…。俺は武人だぜ。<line-break>こんなにばっさり切られちまって　<line-break>…医者もくそもあるかよ。<line-break>…自分の心を裏切らなければ<line-break>手に入らないようなものは、<line-break>理想じゃないんだよ…アイリーン。<end>"
EN: "You fool... I'm a warrior. After a wound like this... what the hell is a doctor going to do? Anything you can only attain by betraying your own heart... isn't an ideal at all, Irene.<end>"
KO: "바보 자식…. 나는 무인이야. 이렇게 크게 베였는데… 의사가 무슨 소용이냐. …자기 마음을 배신해야만 손에 넣을 수 있는 건 이상이 아니야… 아이린.<end>"

## 1020063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先生…。<line-break>うぅ…こ、こんなことって…。<end>"
EN: "Master... No... This can't be happening...<end>"
KO: "선생님…. 으윽… 이, 이런 일이….<end>"

## 1020064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "泣いてるのか…。<line-break>その涙が出るのなら…<line-break>もう、思い残すことは…ない…<end>"
EN: "You're crying... If you can still shed those tears... then I have... no regrets...<end>"
KO: "울고 있는 건가…. 그 눈물이 나온다면…이제 미련은… 없다…<end>"

## 1020065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや…こんなのいやー！<line-break>先生、目を開けて！　先生ー！<end>"
EN: "No... I don't want this! Open your eyes, Master! Masteeer!<end>"
KO: "싫어… 이런 건 싫어! 선생님, 눈 떠요! 선생님!<end>"

## 1020066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしよう…。<line-break>ねぇ、<value:$28>。<line-break>私、どうしよう…。<line-break>私、先生、殺しちゃった…。<line-break>殺しちゃったよー！<end>"
EN: "What do I do...? Tell me, <value:$28>. What do I do...? I killed Master... I killed Master!<end>"
KO: "어쩌지…. 있잖아, <value:$28>. 나 어떡하지…. 나, 선생님을 죽여 버렸어…. 죽여 버렸다고!<end>"

## 1020067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして…どうして、<line-break>目を開けてくれないの…。<line-break>目を開けてよ…先生…。<end>"
EN: "Why...? Why won't you open your eyes...? Open them... Master...<end>"
KO: "왜… 왜 눈을 안 떠 주는 거야…. 눈을 떠 줘… 선생님….<end>"

## 1020068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>先生のこと、お願い。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Please take care of Master.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 선생님을 부탁해.<end>"

## 1020069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配しないで。<line-break>もう、街を襲ったりはしない。<line-break>先生が命をかけて教えてくれたから…。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. I won't attack the city again. Master staked everything, even life itself, to teach me that...<end>"
KO: "걱정하지 마. 이제 마을을 습격하지 않을 거야. 선생님이 목숨을 걸고 가르쳐 주셨으니까….<end>"

## 1020070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>カルラ様を裏切った罪、<line-break>償ってもらうわよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You'll pay for betraying Lady Karula.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 카를라 님을 배신한 죄, 갚게 해주겠어.<end>"

## 1020071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノーブル伯…。<line-break>貴族のくせにこんな所に出てきて…。<line-break>まず、あなたから殺してあげる。<end>"
EN: "Count of Noble... For a noble to come all the way out here... I'll kill you first.<end>"
KO: "노블 백작…. 귀족 주제에 이런 곳까지 나오다니…. 우선 당신부터 죽여 줄게.<end>"

## 1020083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みんな…みんな、死んじゃう。<line-break>あいつも…先生も…<line-break>私…私が…殺した…。<end>"
EN: "Everyone... Everyone dies. That person too... Master too... I... I killed them...<end>"
KO: "모두… 모두 죽어 버려. 그 녀석도… 선생님도… 내가… 내가… 죽였어….<end>"

## 1020084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みんな…どうして行っちゃうの…。<line-break>私…みんなに…<line-break>生きて欲しかったのに…。<line-break>争うことのない…<line-break>誰も殺されない…<line-break>それを…めざしてたのに…。<end>"
EN: "Why does everyone go away...? I wanted everyone... to live. No fighting... no one being killed... That's what I was striving for...<end>"
KO: "다들… 왜 가 버리는 거야…. 나… 모두가… 살아 있었으면 했는데…. 싸움도 없고… 아무도 죽지 않는… 그런 걸… 바랐는데….<end>"

## 1020085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人には、それぞれの<line-break>思い、事情があるものよ。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>大人になるって、<line-break>そういうものをわかってあげられる、<line-break>そういうことじゃないかしら？<end>"
EN: "People have their own feelings, their own circumstances. Perhaps growing up means learning to understand that.<end>"
KO: "사람마다 저마다의 생각과 사정이 있는 법이야. 어른이 된다는 건 그런 걸 이해해 줄 수 있다는 뜻 아닐까?<end>"

## 1020086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルメスさんは、もうここには<line-break>いないわ。ひとりっきりのお嬢さんが　<line-break>亡くなったんですって。<line-break>もしかして、<line-break>あなた、<value:$28>さん？<end>"
EN: "Hermes isn't here anymore. I heard Hermes's only daughter died. Are you <value:$28>, by any chance?<end>"
KO: "에르메스 씨는 이제 여기 없어요. 하나뿐인 따님이 돌아가셨대요. 혹시 당신, <value:$28> 씨인가요?<end>"

## 1020087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルメスさんから伝言があるわ。<line-break>ごめんなさい、そう伝えてって。<line-break>もう、この街に戻ることはないそうよ。<end>"
EN: "Hermes left you a message: “I'm sorry.” That's what I was asked to tell you. I hear Hermes won't be coming back to this town.<end>"
KO: "에르메스 씨에게서 전언이 있어. 미안하다고 전해 달래. 이제 이 도시로 돌아올 일은 없을 거래.<end>"

## 1020088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルメスさんはもう、ここには<line-break>いないわ。ひとりっきりのお嬢さんが　<line-break>亡くなったんですって。<line-break>もう、この街に戻ることはないそうよ。<end>"
EN: "Hermes isn't here anymore. I heard Hermes's only daughter died. Apparently, Hermes won't be coming back to this town.<end>"
KO: "에르메스 씨는 이제 여기 없어요. 하나뿐인 따님이 돌아가셨대요. 이제 이 도시로 돌아오지 않을 거라고 해요.<end>"

## 1020089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec102-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたし、<line-break>最近ここに越してきたの。<line-break>この辺のことには詳しくないわ。　　　<end>"
EN: "I only moved here recently, so I don't know much about the area.<end>"
KO: "나, 최근에 여기로 이사 왔어. 이 근처 일은 잘 몰라.<end>"

## 1030001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイス！　　　　　　　<line-break>奴だ！<line-break>　<line-break>お前の父、<line-break>ロティを殺したのは奴だ！<line-break>あの非市民だ！<end>"
EN: "Khryuseis! That's the one! That noncitizen killed your father, Lottie!<end>"
KO: "크류세이스! 저놈이다! 네 아버지 로티를 죽인 건 저놈이야! 저 비시민이다!<end>"

## 1030002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お父さま…、いえ、<line-break>市民ロティ・クロイス殺害の罪で<line-break>あなたを逮捕します。<end>"
EN: "Father... No. I hereby arrest you for the murder of the citizen Lottie Krois.<end>"
KO: "아버님… 아니, 시민 로티 크로이스 살해 혐의로 당신을 체포합니다.<end>"

## 1030003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しらを切るつもりなの？<line-break>市民アンティノ・マモンから<line-break>証言を得ているのよ。<line-break>あなたがクロイス宛てに<line-break>毒の入った酒を届けたのを<line-break>彼は目撃したのよ！<end>"
EN: "Are you going to deny it? We have testimony from the citizen Antino Mammon. He saw you deliver poisoned wine intended for Krois!<end>"
KO: "시치미 뗄 생각이야? 시민 안티노 마몬에게 증언을 받았어. 네가 크로이스에게 독이 든 술을 배달하는 걸 그가 목격했다고!<end>"

## 1030004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ！　私は見たのだ！<line-break>奴がロティ殺しの犯人だ！<line-break>市民警官、奴を逮捕しろ！<end>"
EN: "Yes! I saw it! That noncitizen murdered Lottie! Citizen officer, arrest that noncitizen!<end>"
KO: "그래! 내가 봤다! 저놈이 로티를 죽인 범인이다! 시민 경찰, 저놈을 체포해라!<end>"

## 1030005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってよ。その人、<line-break>やってないって<line-break>言ってるじゃない！<end>"
EN: "Wait! The accused says, “I didn't do it”!<end>"
KO: "잠깐. 그 사람, 자기는 안 했다고 하잖아!<end>"

## 1030006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、下等な非市民ね。<line-break>非市民は黙っていなさい。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>これはリベルダムの問題なの。<line-break>非市民のあなたに<line-break>口出しする権利はないわ。<end>"
EN: "Another lowly noncitizen. Be silent. This is Liberdam's affair. A noncitizen like you has no right to interfere.<end>"
KO: "또 천한 비시민이군. 비시민은 입 다물고 있어. 이건 리벨덤의 문제야. 비시민인 당신에게 끼어들 권리는 없어.<end>"

## 1030007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだよ。非市民非市民って<line-break>いっつも見下して。<end>"
EN: "What's with all this “noncitizen” talk? You're always looking down on us.<end>"
KO: "뭐야. 비시민, 비시민 하면서 늘 깔보잖아.<end>"

## 1030008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "事実、非市民なんて<line-break>ろくでもない食い詰め者ばかりよ。<line-break>　<line-break>そんな非市民の言うことなんて<line-break>聞くことはないわ。<line-break>さ、あの非市民を逮捕なさい。<end>"
EN: "The fact is, noncitizens are nothing but penniless riffraff. We needn't listen to anything one of them says. Now arrest that noncitizen.<end>"
KO: "사실 비시민 같은 건 변변찮은 빈털터리뿐이야. 그런 비시민 말을 들을 필요 없어. 자, 저 비시민을 체포해.<end>"

## 1030009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして、そんなに<line-break>非市民をバカにするんだ！<line-break>　<line-break>リベルダムの繁栄は<line-break>非市民の労働が<line-break>支えているんだぞ！<end>"
EN: "Why do you have such contempt for noncitizens? Liberdam's prosperity rests on their labor!<end>"
KO: "왜 그렇게 비시민을 무시하는 거야! 리벨덤의 번영은 비시민들의 노동이 떠받치고 있다고!<end>"

## 1030010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカが汗を流して、利口者がもうける。<line-break>これがリベルダムのやり方ですの。<line-break>　<line-break>あと、よそ者に<line-break>口を出させないというのもね。<line-break>従順な人だけいていただければいいの。<end>"
EN: "Fools sweat, and the clever profit. That is how Liberdam works. And we don't allow outsiders to meddle in our affairs. We only need those willing to obey.<end>"
KO: "바보가 땀 흘리고 영리한 자가 돈을 번다. 이게 리벨덤의 방식이에요. 그리고 외지인이 끼어들지 못하게 하는 것도 그렇고요. 순종적인 사람만 있으면 됩니다.<end>"

## 1030011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気に入らないのなら<line-break>出ていっていただいても結構よ。　　　<line-break>代わりならいくらでもいるから<end>"
EN: "If you don't like it, you are quite welcome to leave. There are any number of others to take your place.<end>"
KO: "마음에 안 들면 나가도 좋아. 대신할 사람은 얼마든지 있으니까.<end>"

## 1030012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだよ、なんだよ！<line-break>そんなに市民は偉いの？　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>この人は無実だって言っているのに<line-break>非市民だから逮捕が許される…<line-break>そんなのないよ！<end>"
EN: "What, what?! Are citizens really that special? This person says, “I didn't do it!” And you think it's all right to make the arrest just because the accused is a noncitizen? That's not right!<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 뭐야! 시민이 그렇게 잘났어? 이 사람은 무죄라고 하는데 비시민이라고 체포해도 된다니… 그런 게 어디 있어!<end>"

## 1030013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まずいぞ、クリュセイス。　　　<line-break>言い過ぎだ。<line-break>　<line-break>非市民どもが、騒ぎを聞きつけて<line-break>集まって来るぞ。<line-break>ここは、引き上げよう。<end>"
EN: "This is bad, Khryuseis. You've said too much. This commotion will draw the noncitizen rabble. We should leave.<end>"
KO: "안 되겠어, 크류세이스. 말이 지나쳤다. 비시민들이 소란을 듣고 몰려올 거야. 여기서는 물러나자.<end>"

## 1030014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き上げるですって、<line-break>アンティノおじさま？<line-break>父を殺した者が目の前にいるのに？　<line-break>非市民のクズどもなんて<line-break>いくら集まってもクズはクズですわ。<end>"
EN: "Withdraw, Uncle Antino? With my father's murderer standing right before us? However many of those noncitizen scum gather, scum is still scum.<end>"
KO: "물러나자고요, 안티노 아저씨? 아버지를 죽인 자가 눈앞에 있는데? 비시민 쓰레기들은 아무리 모여도 쓰레기는 쓰레기예요.<end>"

## 1030015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クズだと！<end>"
EN: "Scum?!<end>"
KO: "쓰레기라고!<end>"

## 1030016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いかん、引き上げるぞ。<line-break>クリュセイスを守れ！<end>"
EN: "No, we must withdraw! Protect Khryuseis!<end>"
KO: "안 돼, 철수한다. 크류세이스를 지켜라!<end>"

## 1030017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…覚えてなさい。<line-break>絶対に罪を償わせるから！<end>"
EN: "Remember this. I will make you pay for your crime!<end>"
KO: "…기억해 둬. 반드시 죗값을 치르게 할 테니까!<end>"

## 1030018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危なかったね。<line-break>大丈夫？<end>"
EN: "That was close. Are you all right?<end>"
KO: "위험했네. 괜찮아?<end>"

## 1030019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクはエステル。<line-break>キミは？　　　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "I'm Esther. What's your name?<end>"
KO: "난 에스텔이야. 넌?<end>"

## 1030020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇー、<value:$28>か。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ところで<value:$28>、<line-break>ボクの名前、覚えた？<end>"
EN: "Oh, so you're <value:$28>. By the way, <value:$28>, do you remember my name?<end>"
KO: "헤에, <value:$28>인가. 그런데 <value:$28>, 내 이름 기억했어?<end>"

## 1030021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よしよし、正解正解。<line-break>人の名前と顔を覚えるのは<line-break>人付き合いの基本だからね。　<line-break>じゃ、<value:$28>。<line-break>またどこかで会えるといいね。<end>"
EN: "That's right! Remembering people's names and faces is the first rule of getting along with others. Well, <value:$28>, I hope we meet again somewhere.<end>"
KO: "그래 그래, 정답. 사람 이름과 얼굴을 기억하는 건 인간관계의 기본이지. 그럼 <value:$28>, 또 어디선가 만나면 좋겠네.<end>"

## 1030022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひどいなあ。ボクの名前、<line-break>さっき教えたばっかだよ。　　　　　<line-break>しかも、ボク、キミの恩人じゃない。<line-break>エ・ス・テ・ル！<line-break>ボクはこの名前結構好きなんだから、<line-break>間違えないでよ、もう！<end>"
EN: "That's awful. I only just told you my name! And didn't I just save you? Es-ther! I happen to like my name, so get it right next time!<end>"
KO: "너무하네. 내 이름 방금 가르쳐 줬잖아. 게다가 나는 네 은인이고. 에·스·텔! 나 이 이름 꽤 좋아하니까 틀리지 마, 정말!<end>"

## 1030023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ま、いいか。<line-break>それじゃあね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Oh, never mind. See you around, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 됐나. 그럼 안녕, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1030024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どこをどう聞き間違えたら<line-break>そんな風になるんだよ！　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>エ・ス・テ・ル！<line-break>ボクはこの名前結構好きなんだから、<line-break>間違えないでよ、もう！<end>"
EN: "How could you possibly mishear it that badly? Es-ther! I happen to like my name, so get it right next time!<end>"
KO: "어디를 어떻게 잘못 들어야 그렇게 되는 거야! 에·스·텔! 난 이 이름 꽤 좋아하니까 틀리지 마, 정말!<end>"

## 1030025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ま、いいか。<line-break>それじゃあね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Oh, never mind. See you around, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 됐나. 그럼 안녕, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1030026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ちなよ。<end>"
EN: "Hold it.<end>"
KO: "기다려.<end>"

## 1030027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、親友に毒入りの酒が<line-break>届けられたって時に、ぼーっと<line-break>それを見てたのかい？<end>"
EN: "When someone delivered poisoned wine to your closest friend, you just stood there and watched?<end>"
KO: "친구에게 독이 든 술이 배달됐는데 멍하니 보고만 있었던 거야?<end>"

## 1030028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、いや。ただ、奴が、<line-break>クロイス邸に毒入りの酒を<line-break>持って行くところをだな…。<end>"
EN: "N-no. I only saw that person taking poisoned wine to the Krois residence...<end>"
KO: "아, 아니. 다만 저자가 크로이스 저택에 독이 든 술을 가져가는 걸 말이지….<end>"

## 1030029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇ、よく毒入りだってわかったな。<line-break>その届けものが毒だなんて、誰にも<line-break>わからなかったろうに。<end>"
EN: "Oh? Impressive that you knew it was poisoned. No one could have known what was in that delivery.<end>"
KO: "헤에, 용케 독이 든 걸 알았군. 그 배달물이 독이라는 건 아무도 몰랐을 텐데.<end>"

## 1030030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、しかし！　奴の態度は<line-break>あからさまに不審だったのだ！<end>"
EN: "B-but the way that person was acting was obviously suspicious!<end>"
KO: "하, 하지만! 녀석의 태도는 누가 봐도 수상했다!<end>"

## 1030031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。で、結局あんたは<line-break>その不審な奴が親友の家を訪れた時は<line-break>何もせずに見ていたんだな？<end>"
EN: "Right. So when this suspicious character visited your closest friend's home, you did nothing but watch?<end>"
KO: "그런가. 그런데 결국 그 수상한 녀석이 절친의 집을 찾아갔을 때 당신은 아무것도 하지 않고 보고만 있었군?<end>"

## 1030032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ち、違う…いや！<line-break>遠かったのだ！　<line-break>追いかけたが追いつけなかったのだ！<end>"
EN: "Th-that's not... No! I was too far away! I gave chase, but I couldn't catch up!<end>"
KO: "아, 아니야… 아니! 멀리 있었어! 쫓아갔지만 따라잡지 못했단 말이다!<end>"

## 1030033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほどね。<line-break>じゃあ整理するとこういうことかな。<line-break>　<line-break>あんたは遠くから、怪しい奴が<line-break>クロイス邸に毒入りの酒を<line-break>持ってきたところを目撃したわけだ。<end>"
EN: "All right, let's make sure I have this straight. From a distance, you saw a suspicious character bringing poisoned wine to the Krois residence.<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 그럼 정리하면 이런 거지. 당신은 멀리서 수상한 녀석이 크로이스 저택에 독 든 술을 가져오는 걸 목격한 거야.<end>"

## 1030034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、遠くから見たそいつの姿形が<line-break><value:$28>に似ていると。<line-break>それだけで判断しているんだな？<line-break>ああ、もっとも、遠くから<line-break>毒入りの酒を見破るくらいなら<line-break>人の見分けくらい簡単かな？<end>"
EN: "And the person you saw from so far away looked like <value:$28>. That's all you're basing this on? Then again, if you could spot poisoned wine from that distance, I suppose recognizing a person would be easy.<end>"
KO: "그래서 멀리서 본 그 사람 모습이 <value:$28>와 닮았다고. 그것만으로 판단한 거군? 뭐, 멀리서 독 든 술을 알아볼 정도라면 사람 구별쯤은 쉽겠지만?<end>"

## 1030035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "小うるさい非市民ね。<line-break>市民に向かって無礼だわ！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>それに、あなたの言っていることは、<line-break>この非市民が父殺しの犯人でない<line-break>という証拠にもならなくてよ！<end>"
EN: "What an irritating noncitizen. Show some respect when speaking to a citizen! Besides, nothing you've said proves that this noncitizen did not murder my father!<end>"
KO: "시끄러운 비시민이네. 시민에게 무례해! 게다가 네 말은 이 비시민이 아버지를 죽인 범인이 아니라는 증거도 안 돼!<end>"

## 1030036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、<value:$28>を<line-break>しょっぴくには無理があるだろ？<end>"
EN: "But that's hardly enough to haul <value:$28> away, is it?<end>"
KO: "하지만 <value:$28>을 잡아가는 건 무리잖아?<end>"

## 1030037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>って言ったわね。<line-break>覚えてらっしゃい。<end>"
EN: "You said <value:$28>, didn't you? Just you wait.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>이라고 했지. 기억해 둬.<end>"

## 1030038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、助かってよかった。<line-break>ぬれぎぬ、災難だったな。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>しかし、奴らが、<line-break>あれで諦めたとは思えない。<line-break>気をつけろよ。<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Well, at least you're safe. Being falsely accused is rotten luck. But I doubt that was enough to make them give up. Watch yourself, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 살아서 다행이다. 누명이라니 재수 없었군. 하지만 놈들이 이걸로 포기했다고는 생각 안 해. 조심해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1030039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…レーグ。<end>"
EN: "Reig...<end>"
KO: "…레그.<end>"

## 1030040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…うぬは行くといい。<line-break>あとは我が引き受けた。<end>"
EN: "You may go. Leave the rest to me.<end>"
KO: "…너는 가도 좋다. 나머지는 내가 맡겠다.<end>"

## 1030041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レーグ、どいて！<line-break>あなたには関わりのないこ…。<end>"
EN: "Reig, move! This has nothing to do with y―<end>"
KO: "레그, 비켜! 너와는 상관없는 이….<end>"

## 1030042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あなたが、そ、そう言うなら、<line-break>今は引き上げるわ。<end>"
EN: "If... if you insist, then I will withdraw for now.<end>"
KO: "…당신이, 그, 그렇게 말한다면 지금은 물러날게.<end>"

## 1030043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "覚えてなさい！<line-break>父の仇は必ず…！<end>"
EN: "Remember this! I will avenge my father...!<end>"
KO: "두고 봐! 아버지의 원수는 반드시…!<end>"

## 1030044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…強くなれ。<line-break>それだけだ。<end>"
EN: "Grow stronger. That is all.<end>"
KO: "…강해져라. 그것뿐이다.<end>"

## 1030045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>。ついさっき、<line-break>えらくうまみのある仕事が入ったんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>報酬はなんと、３０００００ギアだ。<line-break>これだけありゃあ、しばらく美味いメシ<line-break>食っていけるぜ。どうだい？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. A very lucrative job just came in. The reward is a whopping 300,000 Gea. With that much, you can eat well for quite a while. What do you say?<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 방금 아주 짭짤한 일이 들어왔어. 보수는 무려 300000기어다. 이 정도면 한동안 맛있는 밥 먹고 살 수 있겠지. 어때?<end>"

## 1030046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうこなくちゃな。<line-break>こんな話、滅多にないからな。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>頼みたいのは、この珍しい宝石、<line-break>ナイトメアの雫さ。<line-break>ロストールのギルドに届けてくれ。<end>"
EN: "That's what I like to hear. Opportunities like this don't come along often. I want you to deliver this rare gemstone, the Nightmare's Tear, to the Guild in Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "그래야지. 이런 기회는 좀처럼 없으니까. 부탁할 건 이 희귀한 보석, 나이트메어의 물방울이야. 로스톨 길드에 전해 줘.<end>"

## 1030047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、こいつは保管が難しくてな、<line-break>普通に持ち歩いたら２週間ともたずに<line-break>露のように消えるんだ。気をつけろよ。<end>"
EN: "There's just one thing: it's difficult to preserve. Carry it around normally, and it'll vanish like dew in less than two weeks. Be careful.<end>"
KO: "다만 이건 보관이 어려워. 평범하게 들고 다니면 2주도 못 가 이슬처럼 사라지지. 조심해.<end>"

## 1030048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかい。残念だ。<line-break>あんたを見込んで頼んだのにな。<line-break>こんなに分のいい仕事はないんだぜ。　<end>"
EN: "I see. That's a shame. I asked because I thought you were the right one for the job. You won't find a better deal than this.<end>"
KO: "그래. 아쉽군. 널 믿고 부탁한 건데. 이렇게 조건 좋은 일도 드물다고.<end>"

## 1030049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、すまん。お前さんに呼ばれたもんで<line-break>ついつい勢いで来たけど、俺、実は<line-break>今ちょっとヤボ用があるんだわ。<line-break>悪いが俺抜きで頑張ってくれ。<line-break>いや、ホント、ゴメン。<line-break>じゃあ、またな。<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Oh, sorry. You called, so I came running without thinking, but I've actually got something I need to take care of right now. Sorry, but you'll have to manage without me. I really am sorry. See you around, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, 미안. 네가 불러서 기세 좋게 와 버렸는데 사실 지금 좀 볼일이 있어. 미안하지만 나 없이 힘내 줘. 아니, 진짜 미안해. 그럼 또 보자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1030050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、彼にも色々都合があるようですし、<line-break>今回はあきらめた方がよさそうですね。<line-break>他の方に呼びかけてみてはどうですか？<end>"
EN: "Well, it seems he has other commitments, so it would be best to give up on him this time. Why not call for someone else?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그 사람에게도 여러 사정이 있는 것 같으니 이번에는 포기하는 편이 좋겠네요. 다른 분에게 부탁해 보는 건 어떻습니까?<end>"

## 1030051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、また会ったな。<end>"
EN: "Hey. We meet again.<end>"
KO: "여어, 또 만났군.<end>"

## 1030052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は、お前さん方の敵だ。<line-break>悪いが、ナイトメアの雫は<line-break>いただく。<end>"
EN: "Today, I'm your enemy. Sorry, but I'll be taking the Nightmare's Tear.<end>"
KO: "오늘은 자네들의 적이다. 미안하지만 나이트메어의 물방울은 받아 가겠다.<end>"

## 1030053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less>50よう、あんた<line-break><value:$28>だな？<end><value:$2B>の<value:$28>…<line-break>お前さんのことだな？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less>50Hey, you're <value:$28>, right?<end><value:$28> of <value:$2B>... That's you, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less>50여어, 자네가 <value:$28>지?<end><value:$2B>의 <value:$28>… 자네 말이지?<end>"

## 1030054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強盗が名乗るのもおこがましいが<line-break>俺はツェラシェルと言う。<line-break>　<line-break>お前さんの持っている<line-break>ナイトメアの雫をもらいに来た。<line-break>悪いな。<end>"
EN: "Making introductions is a bit rich for a robber, but I'm Zeraschel. I've come to take the Nightmare's Tear you've got. Sorry.<end>"
KO: "강도가 이름을 대는 것도 우습지만 난 체라셸이라고 해. 네가 가진 나이트메어의 물방울을 받으러 왔다. 미안하군.<end>"

## 1030055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァイ！　ヴィア！<end>"
EN: "Vai! Via!<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1030058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1030059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…大丈夫だ。心配するな。<line-break>さあ、もう行くぞ。<end>"
EN: "I'm all right. Don't worry. Come on, we're leaving.<end>"
KO: "…괜찮다. 걱정 마라. 자, 이제 가자.<end>"

## 1030060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめろ。殺すな。<end>"
EN: "Stop. Don't kill them.<end>"

## 1030061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、彼らは兄さんを<line-break>殺そうとしました。<end>"
EN: "But they tried to kill you, Brother.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그들은 오빠를 죽이려 했습니다.<end>"

## 1030062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺の言うことを聞け。<line-break>お前らには、<line-break>手に血を塗らせたくない。<end>"
EN: "Do as I say. I don't want blood on your hands.<end>"
KO: "…내 말 들어. 너희 손에 피를 묻히게 하고 싶지 않아.<end>"

## 1030063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "目的のものはいただいた。<line-break>それでいい。行くぞ。<end>"
EN: "We have what we came for. That's enough. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "목표한 건 손에 넣었다. 그걸로 됐어. 가자.<end>"

## 1030064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、ブツはもらっていく。<line-break>じゃあな。<end>"
EN: "We'll be taking the goods, then. See you around.<end>"
KO: "그럼 물건은 가져가겠다. 잘 있어.<end>"

## 1030070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "目が覚めたようね。<line-break>どう？　闘技場で迎える目覚めは？<end>"
EN: "Looks like you're awake. So, how does it feel to wake up in the arena?<end>"
KO: "정신이 든 모양이네. 어때? 투기장에서 맞는 아침은?<end>"

## 1030071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先日話した通り、今日から闘技場で<line-break>戦ってもらいます。あなた自身の<line-break>借金のために。<line-break>さあ、もう試合の準備は<line-break>整っています。行きなさい。<end>"
EN: "As I told you the other day, starting today, you'll be fighting in the arena to pay off your debt. Everything is ready for your match. Now go.<end>"
KO: "지난번에 말했듯 오늘부터 투기장에서 싸워야 합니다. 당신 자신의 빚을 위해서요. 자, 시합 준비는 이미 끝났습니다. 가세요.<end>"

## 1030072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいですね？　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>必ず、<value:$28>を<line-break>殺しなさい。<line-break>あくまで試合中の事故としてです。<line-break>手法は問いません。<line-break>成功したら、さらに報酬を払います。<end>"
EN: "Do you understand? You are to kill <value:$28>, without fail. It must appear to be an accident during the match. I don't care how you do it. Succeed, and you will receive an additional reward.<end>"
KO: "알겠죠? 반드시 <value:$28>를 죽이세요. 어디까지나 시합 중의 사고로 보이게요. 방법은 묻지 않겠습니다. 성공하면 보수를 더 드리죠.<end>"

## 1030073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっくっく。試合中の事故は<line-break>闘技場ではよくあることだからな。　<line-break>　<line-break>闘技場好きのクリュセイスならではの<line-break>趣向というわけだ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. Accidents during a match are common enough in the arena. Just the sort of scheme one would expect from arena-loving Khryuseis.<end>"
KO: "크크크. 경기 중 사고는 투기장에선 흔한 일이니까. 투기장을 좋아하는 크류세이스다운 취향인 셈이지.<end>"

## 1030074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おじさま。あまりそのようなことを　<line-break>おっしゃらないでください。<line-break>　<line-break>闘技場をこのような形で汚すことには<line-break>心がとがめているのです。ですが、<line-break>これもお父様の仇を討つため…。<end>"
EN: "Please, don't speak of it that way. It pains my conscience to sully the arena like this. But this, too, is to avenge Father...<end>"
KO: "아저씨, 그런 말씀은 너무 하지 마세요. 투기장을 이런 식으로 더럽히는 건 양심에 걸립니다. 하지만 이것도 아버지의 원수를 갚기 위해서….<end>"

## 1030075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きっと、お父様も<line-break>お喜びになられるはずです…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sure Father would be pleased...<end>"
KO: "분명 아버님도 기뻐하실 거예요….<end>"

## 1030076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "目が覚めたようね。　　　　　　　<line-break>剣闘士<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>今日も闘技場で戦ってもらいます。<line-break>もう試合の準備は整っています。<line-break>さぁ、行きなさい。<end>"
EN: "So you're awake, Gladiator <value:$28>. You'll be fighting in the arena again today. Everything is ready for your match. Now go.<end>"
KO: "정신이 든 모양이군요. 검투사 <value:$28>. 오늘도 투기장에서 싸워 주셔야 합니다. 이미 시합 준비는 끝났습니다. 자, 가세요.<end>"

## 1030077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "試合の準備はできているようね。<line-break>でも、もうその必要はなくなったわ。<end>"
EN: "Looks like everything's ready for the match. But that won't be necessary anymore.<end>"
KO: "시합 준비는 끝난 모양이네. 하지만 이제 그럴 필요는 없어졌어.<end>"

## 1030078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたには、<line-break>別の仕事をしてもらうことにしたわ。<line-break>　<line-break>せっかく、高いお金を出したのに、<line-break>間違って死なれでもしたら、<line-break>もったいないでしょう？<end>"
EN: "I've decided to give you a different job. After paying so much for you, it would be a waste if you happened to die, wouldn't it?<end>"
KO: "당신에게는 다른 일을 맡기기로 했어. 모처럼 비싼 돈을 냈는데 잘못해서 죽기라도 하면 아깝잖아?<end>"

## 1030079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、ぐずぐずしていないで、　　　　<line-break>ついてきなさい。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Now, stop dawdling and follow me.<end>"
KO: "자, 꾸물거리지 말고 따라오세요.<end>"

## 1030080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さっそくですが、あなたには<line-break>解放軍に加わっていただきます。<end>"
EN: "Let us get straight to the point. You will be joining the Liberation Army.<end>"
KO: "곧바로 말씀드리죠. 당신은 해방군에 합류해 주셔야 합니다.<end>"

## 1030081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルに占領されたロセンを　　　<line-break>解放するための軍隊です。<line-break>　<line-break>リベルダムの有力商人が資金を出資し、<line-break>同志を募って、<line-break>極秘の内に組織したのです。<end>"
EN: "It is an army formed to liberate Rocen from Dyneskal occupation. Influential merchants in Liberdam funded it, recruited like-minded people, and organized it in utmost secrecy.<end>"
KO: "딩갈에게 점령된 로센을 해방하기 위한 군대입니다. 리벨덤의 유력 상인들이 자금을 대고 동지를 모아 극비리에 조직했습니다.<end>"

## 1030082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですが、人手が足りているわけ　　<line-break>ではないのです。<line-break>　<line-break>特に危険な任務を実行できる<line-break>人材は不足しています。<end>"
EN: "That does not mean we have all the people we need. We are especially short of those capable of carrying out dangerous missions.<end>"
KO: "하지만 인력이 충분한 건 아닙니다. 특히 위험한 임무를 수행할 수 있는 인재가 부족합니다.<end>"

## 1030083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこで、あなたには、お金ではなく<line-break>体で払っていただくことにしました。<line-break>その方が、有効ですもの。<line-break>ただ、カン違いしないでほしいわ。<line-break>わたくしはあなたのことを<line-break>許したわけではないのですから。<end>"
EN: "So I have decided that instead of paying in coin, you will pay with your body. It will be far more useful that way. But do not misunderstand me. I have not forgiven you.<end>"
KO: "그래서 당신에게 돈이 아니라 몸으로 갚게 하기로 했습니다. 그쪽이 더 유용하니까요. 다만 착각하지 마세요. 제가 당신을 용서한 건 아니니까요.<end>"

## 1030084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍の活動は命がけのもの。　　<line-break>捕まったら必ず殺されるわ。<line-break>せいぜい、がんばることね。<end>"
EN: "The Liberation Army's work is a matter of life and death. If you are caught, you will certainly be killed. Do try your best.<end>"
KO: "해방군 활동은 목숨을 거는 일이야. 붙잡히면 반드시 죽게 돼. 기껏해야 열심히 해 보라고.<end>"

## 1030085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その密書を解放軍の本部、<line-break>ロセン支部の順に届けてから、<line-break>私のもとに持ち帰るのが最初の仕事よ。<line-break>本部はリベルダムの東に１日、支部は<line-break>ロセンの南に２日の場所にあります。<line-break>１ケ月以内に必ず成し遂げなさい。<end>"
EN: "Your first task is to deliver the Secret Letter to Liberation Army headquarters, then to the Rocen branch, and afterward bring it back to me. Headquarters is one day's journey east of Liberdam, and the branch is two days south of Rocen. You are to complete the task within one month, without fail.<end>"
KO: "그 밀서를 해방군 본부, 로센 지부 순서로 전달한 뒤 내게 가져오는 것이 첫 번째 임무야. 본부는 리벨덤에서 동쪽으로 하루, 지부는 로센에서 남쪽으로 이틀 거리다. 한 달 안에 반드시 완수해.<end>"

## 1030086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、その腕輪があったわね。<line-break>わかりました。はずしてあげます。<end>"
EN: "Ah, yes. That bracelet. Very well, I will remove it for you.<end>"
KO: "아, 그 팔찌가 있었죠. 알겠습니다. 빼 드릴게요.<end>"

## 1030087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、これで剣闘からは解放。<line-break>解放軍の仕事、しっかり頼みましたよ。<end>"
EN: "There. You are free from gladiatorial combat now. I expect you to carry out your work for the Liberation Army properly.<end>"
KO: "자, 이제 검투에서는 해방이야. 해방군 일, 확실히 부탁할게.<end>"

## 1030088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ。残念だな、クリュセイス。<line-break>本当に、悪運の強い奴だ。<end>"
EN: "Damn. What a pity, Khryuseis. That one really does have the devil's own luck.<end>"
KO: "큭. 아쉽군, 크류세이스. 정말 지독하게 운 좋은 녀석이야.<end>"

## 1030089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…悪運の強さだけではありません。<line-break>あの人、本当に強くなる…。<end>"
EN: "Luck isn't all there is to it. That fighter is going to become truly strong...<end>"
KO: "…운이 좋은 것뿐만은 아닙니다. 그 사람, 정말 강해지고 있어요….<end>"

## 1030090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…何を言っている、<line-break>クリュセイス？<end>"
EN: "What are you saying, Khryuseis?<end>"
KO: "…무슨 말을 하는 거냐, 크류세이스?<end>"

## 1030091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、いえ…、おじさま。<line-break>父の仇を討てず、残念です。<end>"
EN: "Oh, no... Uncle. I'm only disappointed that my father wasn't avenged.<end>"
KO: "아, 아니요… 아저씨. 아버지의 원수를 갚지 못해 아쉽습니다.<end>"

## 1030092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、まだまだ機会はある。<line-break>そう、落胆しないことだ。<end>"
EN: "There will be plenty more opportunities. Don't be so disheartened.<end>"
KO: "하지만 아직 기회는 많아. 그렇게 낙담하지 마.<end>"

## 1030094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>バカな人。　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…お父様、<line-break>これでお父様も満足して<line-break>いらっしゃいますよね？　　　　　<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... What a fool. Father... You're satisfied now, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 바보 같은 사람. …아버지, 이걸로 아버지도 만족하시겠죠?<end>"

## 1030095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "負けてばかりだと、いつまでたっても　<line-break>借金は減らないわよ。<line-break>　<line-break>また戦ってもらいますから、<line-break>それまでは、自由になさい。<line-break>次に目覚めるまでの、わずかな時間を。<end>"
EN: "If you keep losing, you'll never pay down your debt. You will fight again. Until then, you are free to do as you please for the little time you have before you awaken again.<end>"
KO: "계속 지기만 하면 빚은 언제까지고 줄지 않아. 또 싸워 줘야 하니까, 그때까지는 자유롭게 지내렴. 다음에 깨어날 때까지의 짧은 시간을.<end>"

## 1030096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがに多少はやるようね。<line-break>その手でお父様を殺したのですもの。<line-break>　<line-break>殺人で手を汚しているあなたに<line-break>とっては、これ以上ない<line-break>お金の稼ぎ方なのでしょうね。<end>"
EN: "You do have some skill, I'll grant you that. You killed Father with those very hands, after all. For someone whose hands are already stained with murder, I suppose there could be no better way to earn money.<end>"
KO: "역시 조금은 하는군요. 그 손으로 아버님을 죽였으니까요. 살인으로 손을 더럽힌 당신에겐 이보다 좋은 돈벌이는 없겠죠.<end>"

## 1030097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、今日の賞金だけでは　　　　　　<line-break>あなたの補償金の額には<line-break>とてもではないけど、足りないわ。<line-break>またここで戦ってもらうわ。<line-break>それまでは、自由になさい。<line-break>次に目覚めるまでの、わずかな時間を。<end>"
EN: "But today's prize money is nowhere near enough to cover the compensation you owe. You will fight here again. Until then, you are free to do as you please for the little time you have before you awaken again.<end>"
KO: "하지만 오늘 상금만으로는 당신의 배상금에 턱없이 부족해. 또 여기서 싸워 줘야 해. 그때까지는 자유롭게 지내. 다음에 깨어날 때까지의 짧은 시간을.<end>"

## 1030098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やるわね。今日の相手はそれなりに　<line-break>名のある闘士なのに。<line-break>　<line-break>あなたも武器で生きている人だから、<line-break>そう簡単に負けたりは<line-break>しないのでしょうけど。<end>"
EN: "Not bad. Your opponent today was a fighter of some renown. Then again, you make your living with a weapon, so I suppose you wouldn't lose so easily.<end>"
KO: "제법이네. 오늘 상대는 나름 이름 있는 투사인데. 당신도 무기로 먹고사는 사람이니 쉽게 지진 않겠지만.<end>"

## 1030099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、この程度ではまだ、<line-break>あなたの補償金の額には足りないわ。　<line-break>　<line-break>またここで戦ってもらうわ。<line-break>それまでは、自由になさい。<line-break>次に目覚めるまでの、わずかな時間を。<end>"
EN: "But this still isn't enough to cover the compensation you owe. You will fight here again. Until then, you are free to do as you please for the little time you have before you awaken again.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이 정도로는 아직 네 배상금 액수에 못 미쳐. 또 여기서 싸워 줘야 해. 그때까지는 자유롭게 지내. 다음에 깨어날 때까지의 짧은 시간을.<end>"

## 1030100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく無事に済んだわね。<line-break>今日の相手はかなり危険だったのに。　<line-break>　<line-break>あなたの腕にかかれば、<line-break>大したことないのかもしれないけど、<line-break>少し、ひやっとしたわ。<end>"
EN: "You came through that safely. Today's opponent was quite dangerous. Perhaps it was nothing for someone of your skill, but you gave me a bit of a scare.<end>"
KO: "용케 무사했네. 오늘 상대는 꽤 위험했는데. 네 실력이라면 별거 아닐지도 모르지만 조금 가슴이 철렁했어.<end>"

## 1030101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…言っておきますけど、<line-break>あなたの命は問題ではありません。<line-break>補償金が無駄になるから、それだけよ。<line-break>あなたには、補償金の額だけ<line-break>しっかり働いてもらわないと<line-break>困りますから、そのつもりで。<end>"
EN: "Let me make one thing clear: your life is not my concern. I simply don't want the compensation to go to waste. I need you to work off the full amount, so bear that in mind.<end>"
KO: "…말해 두겠지만, 당신 목숨이 걱정돼서 그러는 건 아니에요. 보상금이 헛되게 되니까, 그뿐이에요. 보상금 액수만큼은 확실히 일해 줘야 곤란하지 않으니 그렇게 알아두세요.<end>"

## 1030102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec103-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "またここで戦ってもらうわ。<line-break>それまでは、自由になさい。<line-break>次に目覚めるまでの、わずかな時間を。<end>"
EN: "You will fight here again. Until then, you are free to do as you please for the little time you have before you awaken again.<end>"
KO: "또 여기서 싸워 줘. 그때까지는 자유롭게 지내. 다음에 깨어날 때까지의 짧은 시간을.<end>"

## 1030103

JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break>借金剣闘士の称号を得た。<end>"

## 1040002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（…な、何だ…？<line-break>　なんだか、体が浮いてるような…。）<end>"
EN: "(W-what's happening...? It feels like my body's floating...)<end>"
KO: "(…뭐, 뭐지…? 왠지 몸이 떠 있는 것 같은….)<end>"

## 1040003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "剣闘士<value:$28>、<line-break>もう試合の時間ですよ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>さっきから観客は興奮しっぱなしです。<line-break>さあ、早く天武陣の方へ！<end>"
EN: "Gladiator <value:$28>, it's time for your match. The crowd has been worked up for a while now. Hurry to the arena floor!<end>"
KO: "검투사 <value:$28>, 이제 시합 시간이야. 아까부터 관중들이 계속 흥분해 있어. 자, 빨리 천무진으로!<end>"

## 1040004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、ちょうどよかった。<line-break>ちょっと頼まれてくれないかな？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Oh, perfect timing. Could I ask you to do me a small favor?<end>"
KO: "오, 마침 잘 왔네. 부탁 하나 들어주지 않을래?<end>"

## 1040005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これなんだけどな。<line-break>ロティ・クロイスさんの屋敷に　<line-break>届けてほしいんだ。<line-break>町の中で片付く届けものを<line-break>頼まれるなんて珍しいんだが…。<line-break>どうだ、引き受けてくれないか？<end>"
EN: "I'd like you to deliver this to Lottie Krois's estate. I don't often get asked to handle a delivery that stays within town... What do you say? Will you take it?<end>"
KO: "이건데 말이야. 로티 크로이스 씨의 저택에 전해 줬으면 해. 마을 안에서 끝나는 배달을 부탁받다니 드문 일인데…. 어때, 맡아 주지 않을래?<end>"

## 1040006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お安いご用<end>"
EN: "No problem.<end>"
KO: "식은 죽 먹기지<end>"

## 1040007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かったるいな<end>"
EN: "Sounds like a pain.<end>"
KO: "귀찮네<end>"

## 1040008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな話は普通受けないんだが、<line-break>この町の有力な武器商人の依頼でな。　<line-break>断るに断れなかったんだよ。<line-break>クロイス邸のある居住区は<line-break>ここから広場を突っ切ってすぐの所だ。<line-break>散歩がてら、よろしく頼むよ。<end>"
EN: "I don't usually accept jobs like this, but the request came from a prominent arms merchant in town. I couldn't very well turn it down. The Krois estate is in the residential district, just across the square from here. Think of it as a stroll. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "이런 일은 보통 안 맡는데, 이 마을의 유력한 무기상 의뢰라 거절하기도 어렵더라고. 크로이스 저택이 있는 주거구는 여기서 광장을 가로질러 가면 금방이야. 산책 삼아 부탁하지.<end>"

## 1040009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃあ、そうだよな。<line-break>普通こんな話は受けないんだがな。　　<line-break>いや、悪かったな。他を当たるよ。<end>"
EN: "Fair enough. I don't normally accept jobs like this either. Sorry to bother you. I'll ask someone else.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그렇겠지. 보통은 이런 이야기를 받아들이지 않으니까. 아니, 미안했어. 다른 데 알아볼게.<end>"

## 1040010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何かご用ですか？　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ロティ様に届けもの？<line-break>そうですか、それはわざわざ<line-break>ご苦労さまでした。<end>"
EN: "Can I help you? You have a delivery for Master Lottie? I see. Thank you for coming all this way.<end>"
KO: "무슨 용건이십니까? 로티 님께 전해 드릴 물건이라고요? 그렇군요, 수고하셨습니다.<end>"

## 1040011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに受け取りました。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ところで…、なんですかこれは？<line-break>重さからすると水のようですが…。<line-break>きっと、お酒か何かですね。<end>"
EN: "I've received it. By the way... what is this? Judging by the weight, it seems to be water. Probably liquor or something.<end>"
KO: "확실히 받았습니다. 그런데… 이게 뭡니까? 무게로 보면 물 같은데… 분명 술 같은 거겠군요.<end>"

## 1040012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや？　差出人の名前がない…。　<line-break>まあたぶん、中にカードでも<line-break>入っているのでしょうね。<end>"
EN: "Oh? There's no sender's name... Well, I expect there's a card inside.<end>"
KO: "어라? 보내는 사람 이름이 없네…. 뭐, 아마 안에 카드라도 들어 있겠지.<end>"

## 1040013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残念だよ、<value:$28>君。<line-break>これほど仕事が遅いとは…。<line-break>手紙は別の者に届けてもらった。<line-break>もっとできる冒険者だと<line-break>思っていたのだが…。<end>"
EN: "I'm disappointed, <value:$28>. I never thought you'd take this long. I had someone else deliver the letter. I thought you were a more capable adventurer.<end>"
KO: "아쉽군, <value:$28>군. 이렇게 일이 늦을 줄이야…. 편지는 다른 사람에게 맡겼다. 좀 더 유능한 모험자라고 생각했는데….<end>"

## 1040014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイス様の手紙を　　　　　　　<line-break>届けてくれたようだな。<line-break>　<line-break>助かるよ。ああいう仕事に<line-break>慣れている人材が、解放軍には少ない。<line-break>これからも協力よろしく頼むよ。<end>"
EN: "It seems you delivered Lady Khryuseis's letter. Thank you. The Liberation Army has few people experienced in that sort of work. I hope we can continue to count on your help.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스 님의 편지를 전해 준 모양이군. 도움이 됐어. 그런 일에 익숙한 인재가 해방군에는 적거든. 앞으로도 협력을 부탁할게.<end>"

## 1040015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "早く届けてくれ。<line-break>重要な手紙なんだ。　　　　<end>"
EN: "Deliver it quickly. It's an important letter.<end>"
KO: "빨리 전해 줘. 중요한 편지다.<end>"

## 1040016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイス様からの手紙か。<line-break>ご苦労だった。少し待ってもらえるか。<line-break>我々が読んだあと、他に運んでほしいのだ。<end>"
EN: "A letter from Lady Khryuseis? Well done. Could you wait a moment? Once we've read it, we'd like you to take it elsewhere.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스 님의 편지인가. 수고했다. 잠깐 기다려 주겠나. 우리가 읽은 뒤 다른 곳으로도 옮겨 줬으면 한다.<end>"

## 1040017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待たせたな。次はロセン支部に<line-break>クリュセイス様の手紙を持っていってくれ。<line-break>支部はロセンの南に２日行った所にある。<end>"
EN: "Sorry to keep you waiting. Now take Lady Khryuseis's letter to the Rocen branch. It's two days south of Rocen.<end>"
KO: "기다리게 했군. 다음은 로센 지부에 크류세이스 님의 편지를 가져다줘. 지부는 로센에서 남쪽으로 이틀 가면 있다.<end>"

## 1040018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残念だよ、<value:$28>君。<line-break>これほど仕事が遅いとは…。<line-break>手紙は別の者に届けてもらった。<line-break>もっとできる冒険者だと<line-break>思っていたのだが…。<end>"
EN: "I'm disappointed, <value:$28>. I never thought you'd take this long. I had someone else deliver the letter. I thought you were a more capable adventurer.<end>"
KO: "아쉽군, <value:$28>군. 이렇게 일이 늦을 줄이야…. 편지는 다른 사람에게 맡겼다. 좀 더 유능한 모험자라고 생각했는데….<end>"

## 1040019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイス様の手紙を　　　　　　　<line-break>届けてくれたようだな。<line-break>　<line-break>助かるよ。ああいう仕事に<line-break>慣れている人材が、解放軍には少ない。<line-break>これからも協力よろしく頼むよ。<end>"
EN: "It seems you delivered Lady Khryuseis's letter. Thank you. The Liberation Army has few people experienced in that sort of work. I hope we can continue to count on your help.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스 님의 편지를 전해 준 모양이군. 도움이 됐어. 그런 일에 익숙한 인재가 해방군에는 적거든. 앞으로도 협력을 부탁할게.<end>"

## 1040020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "早く届けてくれ。<line-break>重要な手紙なんだ。　　　　<end>"
EN: "Deliver it quickly. It's an important letter.<end>"
KO: "빨리 전해 줘. 중요한 편지다.<end>"

## 1040021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイス様からの手紙か。<line-break>ご苦労だった。少し待ってもらえるか。<line-break>我々が読んだあと、他に運んでほしいのだ。<end>"
EN: "A letter from Lady Khryuseis? Well done. Could you wait a moment? Once we've read it, we'd like you to take it elsewhere.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스 님의 편지인가. 수고했다. 잠깐 기다려 주겠나. 우리가 읽은 뒤 다른 곳으로도 옮겨 줬으면 한다.<end>"

## 1040022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待たせたな。では、この手紙を<line-break>クリュセイス様のもとに返してくれ。<line-break>それで、任務完了だ。<end>"
EN: "Sorry to keep you waiting. Now return this letter to Lady Khryuseis. That will complete the mission.<end>"
KO: "기다리게 했군. 그럼 이 편지를 크류세이스 님께 돌려줘. 그걸로 임무 완료다.<end>"

## 1040023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったくどういうつもりなんだ！<line-break>だいたい、あの手紙の内容を<line-break>私は知らんぞ。<end>"
EN: "What in the world are you thinking? I don't even know what that letter says!<end>"
KO: "대체 무슨 생각인 거냐! 애초에 그 편지 내용은 나도 모른다고.<end>"

## 1040024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンのカルラ軍の動向を<line-break>記した文章です。<end>"
EN: "It's a report on the movements of Karula's Army in Rocen.<end>"
KO: "로센의 카를라군 동향을 기록한 문서입니다.<end>"

## 1040025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "重要な文書ではないか！　　　　　<line-break>それを<value:$28>に任せるなど。<line-break>　<line-break>クリュセイス、何を考えている？<line-break>あいつはお前の父の仇なのだぞ！<end>"
EN: "Then it's an important document! And you entrusted it to <value:$28>? Khryuseis, what are you thinking? That's the one who killed your father!<end>"
KO: "중요한 문서잖아! 그걸 <value:$28>에게 맡기다니. 크류세이스, 무슨 생각이냐? 저자는 네 아버지의 원수다!<end>"

## 1040026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかっています。<line-break>ですが…。<end>"
EN: "I know. But...<end>"
KO: "…알고 있습니다. 하지만….<end>"

## 1040027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まさか、他に犯人がいるなどとは<line-break>考えていないだろうな？<end>"
EN: "Surely you don't think someone else did it?<end>"
KO: "설마 다른 범인이 있다고 생각하는 건 아니겠지?<end>"

## 1040028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空メッセージ<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "빈 메시지<end>"

## 1040029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>のこと、<line-break>少し調べさせました。<end>"
EN: "I had someone look into <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>에 대해 조금 조사하게 했습니다.<end>"

## 1040030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと？<end>"
EN: "What did you say?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고?<end>"

## 1040031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者としての評判は、　　　　<line-break>悪くありませんでした。<line-break>　<line-break>それに、闘技場での戦い…。<line-break>あの場所であれだけ戦える人が、<line-break>毒などという方法で…<end>"
EN: "The adventurer's reputation wasn't bad. And then there was that fight in the arena... Someone who could fight that well, resorting to poison...<end>"
KO: "모험자로서의 평판은 나쁘지 않았습니다. 게다가 투기장에서의 싸움…. 그곳에서 그렇게 싸울 수 있는 사람이 독 같은 방법으로…<end>"

## 1040032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "落ち着け、クリュセイス！<line-break>そんな理由で、<line-break>奴の容疑が晴れるものか！<end>"
EN: "Get hold of yourself, Khryuseis! That's hardly enough to clear that scoundrel of suspicion!<end>"
KO: "진정해, 크류세이스! 그런 이유로 녀석의 혐의가 풀릴 리 없어!<end>"

## 1040033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様が、お見えです。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> is here to see you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님이 오셨습니다.<end>"

## 1040034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました。通しなさい。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Show the visitor in.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 들여보내세요.<end>"

## 1040035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ちっ！<end>"
EN: "Tch!<end>"
KO: "…칫!<end>"

## 1040036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕方あるまい…。<end>"
EN: "No choice, then...<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없군….<end>"

## 1040037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どこで何をしていたのか　　　　<line-break>知らないけど…。<line-break>二流以下の仕事だわ。<line-break>手紙はもう１通書いて、<line-break>他の者に運んでもらいました。<line-break>その手紙は、返してもらうわね。<end>"
EN: "I don't know where you went or what you were doing, but this was second-rate work at best. I wrote another letter and had someone else deliver it. I'll have the first letter back now.<end>"
KO: "어디서 뭘 하고 있었는지는 모르겠지만…. 이류 이하의 일이야. 편지는 이미 한 통 더 써서 다른 사람에게 맡겼어. 그 편지는 돌려받을게.<end>"

## 1040038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、解放軍は、しばらくの間<line-break>カルラ軍の出方を見守りつつ、<line-break>戦力の充実をはかります。<line-break>カルラ軍は<line-break>まだ動く気配がないようだから、<line-break>我々もうかつには動けないわ。<end>"
EN: "For the time being, the Liberation Army will watch Karula's Army and strengthen our forces. They show no sign of moving yet, so we cannot afford to act rashly either.<end>"
KO: "자, 해방군은 당분간 칼라군의 움직임을 지켜보며 전력을 보강하겠습니다. 칼라군은 아직 움직일 기색이 없으니 우리도 경솔하게 움직일 수는 없어요.<end>"

## 1040039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうよ、運んでもらった手紙には、<line-break>今話した内容が書かれていたの。<line-break>　<line-break>人手不足は相変わらずだから、<line-break>また、こういう伝令を<line-break>頼むかもしれないわ。<end>"
EN: "Yes, the letter I had delivered contained what I just told you. We're still short-handed, so I may ask you to carry another message like this.<end>"
KO: "그래, 운반해 준 편지에는 방금 말한 내용이 적혀 있었어. 인력 부족은 여전하니까 또 이런 전령 일을 부탁할지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1040040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度は一流の仕事を見せてほしいわね。<line-break>近い内に、<line-break>またここに来てちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "Next time, I hope you'll show me first-rate work. Come back here again soon.<end>"
KO: "이번엔 일류다운 솜씨를 보여 줬으면 좋겠네. 조만간 다시 여기로 와 줘.<end>"

## 1040041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがね、<value:$28>。<line-break>でも、このくらい当然のことだわ。<end>"
EN: "Just what I'd expect from you, <value:$28>. Though this much should be a matter of course.<end>"
KO: "역시 <value:$28>. 하지만 이 정도는 당연하지.<end>"

## 1040042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうよ、運んでもらった手紙には、<line-break>今話した内容が書かれていたの。<line-break>　<line-break>また、こういう伝令を<line-break>頼むかもしれないわ。<end>"
EN: "Yes, the letter I had delivered contained what I just told you. I may ask you to carry another message like this.<end>"
KO: "그래, 네가 옮겨 준 편지에는 방금 말한 내용이 적혀 있었어. 앞으로도 이런 전령 일을 부탁할지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1040044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "手紙は届けたのかしら？　　　　　<line-break>本部のあとは、ロセン支部にも<line-break>行ってもらうことになるはずよ。<line-break>こんなところに顔を出すより、<line-break>手紙を運ぶのが優先ではなくて？<line-break>怠慢な仕事ぶりは、許さないわよ。<end>"
EN: "Have you delivered the letter? After headquarters, you'll likely have to go to the Rocen branch as well. Shouldn't delivering it take priority over showing your face here? I will not tolerate such negligence.<end>"
KO: "편지는 전했어? 본부 다음에는 로센 지부에도 가 줘야 할 텐데. 이런 데 들를 시간에 편지를 나르는 게 우선 아니야? 태만한 일 처리는 용서 못 해.<end>"

## 1040045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、早く行きなさい！<end>"
EN: "Now hurry up and go!<end>"
KO: "자, 어서 가세요!<end>"

## 1040046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと待ちな！　　　<line-break>あんたが持ってる手紙、<line-break>いただくぜ！<end>"
EN: "Hold it right there! I'll be taking that letter you've got!<end>"
KO: "잠깐 기다려! 네가 가진 편지, 내가 받아가겠다!<end>"
