It seems a little suspicious, but let's deliver it first.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191<value:$28>, how do you think they knew about the letter? Aren't you curious? Still, there's no use dwelling on it. Let's deliver the letter first.<end>They knew we were carrying the letter. That's strange. Either way, we need to deliver it quickly. Come on, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2우리가 편지를 가지고 있다는 걸 어떻게 알았지? …아무튼 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3저놈들은 우리가 편지를 가지고 있다는 걸 어떻게 안 게냐? …어쨌든 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4음, 우리가 편지를 가진 걸 어떻게 알았을까? 뭐, 그런 건 아무래도 좋으려나. 서두르는 게 좋겠어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5저놈들, 우리가 편지를 나르는 걸 어떻게 안 거야. 뭐, 지금은 그럴 때가 아니지. 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6저 사람들, 우리가 편지를 나르는 걸 어떻게 알았을까? 아, 지금은 그럴 때가 아니지. 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…이상하군. 저놈들, 왜 편지 일을 알고 있지…? …뭔가 수상하지만 지금은 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10이상하네. 우리가 편지를 나르는 걸 어떻게 알았지? 뭐 됐어. 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14저놈들은 우리가 편지를 가지고 있다는 걸 어떻게 안 게냐? …뭐, 됐다. 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15우리가 편지를 나르는 걸 알고 있었어…. 왜지…? 아, 지금은 편지를 전하는 게 먼저였지. 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐야, 저놈들은! 어떻게 아타쿠시들이 편지를 가지고 있다는 걸 안 거야! 뭐 좋아. 저 하등한 녀석들이 무슨 근거로 덤볐든 지금은 편지만 전달하면 충분해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19저 사람들, 왜 편지 일을 알고 있었을까…? 아, 그래도 지금은 빨리 편지를 전달해야 하지. 서두르자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20편지 일을 알고 있다…. 이상하군. …뭐 됐다. 서둘러라.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11이상해… 왜 편지 일을 알고 있지? 아무튼 서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26우리가 편지를 가지고 있다는 걸 어떻게 안 게냐? …서두르자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193어떻게 저 사람들이 우리가 편지를 가진 걸 알았을까요? 좀 수상하지만… 일단 먼저 편지를 전달하죠.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191<value:$28>. 왜 편지 일을 알고 있었던 것 같아? 신경 안 쓰여? 그래도 생각만 해 봐야 소용없겠지. 일단 편지를 전달하자.<end>우리가 편지를 나르는 걸 알고 있었어…. 이상하네. …어쨌든 지금은 빨리 편지를 전달해야 해. 서두르자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1040048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キャーッ！<end>"
EN: "Aah!<end>"
KO: "꺄악!<end>"

## 1040049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>！！<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>!!<end>"

## 1040050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ！<end>"
EN: "Damn!<end>"
KO: "칫!<end>"

## 1040051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "た、助かったわ…。<end>"
EN: "Y-you saved me...<end>"
KO: "사, 살았네….<end>"

## 1040052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイス様！<line-break>どうしました？<end>"
EN: "Lady Khryuseis! What happened?<end>"
KO: "크류세이스 님! 무슨 일이십니까?<end>"

## 1040053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、侵入者です。<line-break>おそらく私の命をねらったのでしょう。<line-break>今、出ていった男です。<end>"
EN: "A-an intruder. He was likely after my life. It was the man who just left.<end>"
KO: "치, 침입자입니다. 아마 제 목숨을 노렸겠죠. 방금 나간 남자입니다.<end>"

## 1040054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え、本当ですか！？<line-break>も、申しわけありません！<line-break>しかし…、あの男は…。<end>"
EN: "What? Truly?! I-I'm terribly sorry! But... that man...<end>"
KO: "네, 정말입니까!? 죄, 죄송합니다! 하지만… 그 남자는….<end>"

## 1040055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんですか？<end>"
EN: "What about him?<end>"
KO: "뭡니까?<end>"

## 1040056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、あの男、アンティノ様のお屋敷で<line-break>見たことのある男でして、それで、<line-break>なんの警戒もしなかったのです。<end>"
EN: "Well, I had seen that man at Lord Antino's estate before, so I had no reason to be wary of him.<end>"
KO: "아니요, 그 남자는 안티노 님 저택에서 본 적이 있어서 아무 경계도 하지 않았습니다.<end>"

## 1040057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おじさまのお屋敷で見た？<line-break>何を言っているのです。<line-break>そんなはずは…<end>"
EN: "You saw him at Uncle's estate? What are you talking about? That can't be...<end>"
KO: "아저씨 저택에서 봤다고요? 무슨 말씀이세요. 그럴 리가…<end>"

## 1040058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おじさまを疑えと言うの！？<line-break>言葉を慎みなさい！<line-break>おじさまの手の者のわけがないわ！<line-break>おじさまはお父様の親友です。<line-break>お父様が亡くなる前にもあとにも<line-break>わたくしたちを助けてくれたわ。<end>"
EN: "Are you saying I should suspect Uncle?! Mind what you say! That man couldn't possibly be one of Uncle's men! Uncle was my father's closest friend. He helped us before my father's death, and afterward as well.<end>"
KO: "아저씨를 의심하라는 거야!? 말조심해! 아저씨 부하일 리가 없어! 아저씨는 아버님의 친우야. 아버님이 돌아가시기 전에도 후에도 우리를 도와주셨다고.<end>"

## 1040059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのおじさまが解放軍を裏切る…<line-break>いえ、わたくしを襲うようなマネを<line-break>するはずがありません。<line-break>解放軍の士気に関わります。<line-break>軽はずみな言動はつつしんで<line-break>いただきたいわ。おわかり？<end>"
EN: "Uncle, betray the Liberation Army...? No. He would never do anything like have me attacked. Such talk could damage the Liberation Army's morale. I must ask you to refrain from making careless remarks. Is that understood?<end>"
KO: "그 아저씨가 해방군을 배신… 아니, 저를 습격할 리 없습니다. 해방군의 사기에 관계된 일이에요. 경솔한 말은 삼가 주셨으면 해요. 아시겠어요?<end>"

## 1040060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、わたくしはあなたのことを<line-break>責めているつもりではなくて、<line-break>ただ…<end>"
EN: "I-I didn't mean to blame you. I only...<end>"
KO: "저, 저는 당신을 탓하려는 게 아니라, 그저….<end>"

## 1040061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、我々も<line-break>屋敷周辺の警戒を強化いたします。<end>"
EN: "Then we will strengthen security around the estate as well.<end>"
KO: "그럼 저희도 저택 주변의 경계를 강화하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1040062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…助けてもらったお礼も言わないで<line-break>あんな事を言うなんて…。<end>"
EN: "I said all that without even thanking you for saving me...<end>"
KO: "…도움을 받고도 감사 인사도 안 하고 그런 말을 하다니….<end>"

## 1040063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…でも、ありえないわ。<line-break>おじさまが、わたくしを襲うなど…。<end>"
EN: "But it can't be true. Uncle would never attack me...<end>"
KO: "…하지만 말도 안 돼요. 아저씨가 저를 습격하다니….<end>"

EN: ""
KO: "빈 메시지·크류세이스 V3-1<end>"

## 1040235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナイトメアの雫を奪われた。<end>"
EN: "The Nightmare's Tear was stolen.<end>"
KO: "나이트메어의 물방울을 빼앗겼다.<end>"

## 1040236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec104-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイスに手紙を返した。<end>"
EN: "Returned the letter to Khryuseis.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스에게 편지를 돌려주었다.<end>"

## 1040237

## 1050000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイスメッセージＶ３－２用開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "크류세이스 메시지 V3-2용 시작<end>"

## 1050001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例の件ですが…。<end>"
EN: "About that matter...<end>"
KO: "그 건 말입니다….<end>"

## 1050002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "暗殺者がアンティノおじさまの　　<line-break>手の者じゃないかという件…。<line-break>　<line-break>今から、アンティノおじさまの所へ<line-break>行って話をうかがってくるわ。<line-break>そうすれば解決することだもの。<end>"
EN: "You mean the possibility that the assassin was one of Uncle Antino's people... I'm going to see him now and ask him about it. That should settle the matter.<end>"
KO: "암살자가 안티노 아저씨의 수하일지도 모른다는 건…. 지금 안티노 아저씨한테 가서 이야기를 들어 볼 거야. 그러면 해결될 일이니까.<end>"

## 1050003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍の有力者であるおじさまが<line-break>疑われていては、不都合ですからね。<line-break>　<line-break>言っておきますけど、あなたの言葉を<line-break>鵜呑みにしたわけじゃなくてよ。<line-break>そこのところ、理解してほしいわ。<end>"
EN: "It would hardly do for my uncle, a leading figure in the Liberation Army, to remain under suspicion. But let me make one thing clear: I haven't simply taken your word for it. I hope you understand that.<end>"
KO: "해방군의 유력자인 아저씨가 의심받아서는 곤란하니까요. 말해 두겠지만 당신 말을 곧이곧대로 믿은 건 아니에요. 그 점은 이해해 줬으면 해요.<end>"

## 1050004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、一応、理解していただけたようね。<line-break>それじゃ、アンティノおじさまの<line-break>屋敷へ向かいましょう。<end>"
EN: "I-it seems you've understood, at least. Then let us go to Uncle Antino's estate.<end>"
KO: "이, 일단 이해해 주신 것 같네요. 그럼 안티노 아저씨의 저택으로 가죠.<end>"

## 1050005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンティノおじさま、<line-break>突然、お訪ねして、すみません。<line-break>ひとつお尋ねしたいことがあって…。<end>"
EN: "Uncle Antino, forgive me for coming without warning. There's something I would like to ask you...<end>"
KO: "안티노 아저씨, 갑자기 찾아와서 죄송해요. 하나 여쭤볼 게 있어서요….<end>"

## 1050006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんでも答えよう。<line-break>お前は我が親友ロティの娘だからな。<line-break>どうしたのだ、クリュセイス？<end>"
EN: "Ask me anything. You are the daughter of my dear friend Lottie, after all. What is it, Khryuseis?<end>"
KO: "무엇이든 답해 주지. 넌 내 절친 로티의 딸이니까. 무슨 일이냐, 크류세이스?<end>"

## 1050009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その暗殺者ですが、<line-break>同志のひとりが、おじさまの屋敷で<line-break>見たことのある人物だと言うのです。<line-break>これは、どういうことなのでしょう。<line-break>おじさま、何かおっしゃってください。<line-break>このままでは…<end>"
EN: "The assassin is someone one of our comrades has seen before at your estate. What am I to make of that? Uncle, please say something. At this rate...<end>"
KO: "그 암살자 말인데요, 동지 한 명이 숙부님의 저택에서 본 적 있는 인물이라고 합니다. 이게 대체 어떻게 된 일입니까. 숙부님, 뭐라도 말씀해 주세요. 이대로는….<end>"

## 1050010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…うっとうしかったのだ。<end>"
EN: "I was sick of it.<end>"
KO: "…성가셨던 거다.<end>"

## 1050011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　今、なんて…<end>"
EN: "What? What did you just say...?<end>"
KO: "어? 방금 뭐라고…<end>"

## 1050012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっとうしかったと言っているのだよ、<line-break>クリュセイス。<line-break>　<line-break>ロティの奴が…、<line-break>そして、お前もな！<end>"
EN: "I said I was sick of you, Khryuseis. Lottie... and you too!<end>"
KO: "귀찮았다고 말하는 거다, 크류세이스. 로티 그 녀석이… 그리고 너도 말이지!<end>"

## 1050013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ど、どういうこと…。<end>"
EN: "Wh-what do you mean...?<end>"
KO: "무, 무슨 뜻이야….<end>"

## 1050014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ、気づかんのか。<line-break>ロティを殺したのはこの俺だと<line-break>言っているんだよ！<end>"
EN: "You still haven't realized? I'm telling you that I killed Lottie!<end>"
KO: "아직도 눈치 못 챘나. 로티를 죽인 건 바로 나라고 말하는 거다!<end>"

## 1050015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんなことはありえません。<line-break>だって、おじさまは…。<end>"
EN: "Th-that can't be true. You were...<end>"
KO: "그, 그럴 리가 없습니다. 아저씨는….<end>"

## 1050016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、お前の親父の親友だったさ。　　<line-break>そのおかげで、さんざん<line-break>苦い思いをさせてもらった。<line-break>外面がよく、人気取りがうまい。<line-break>同じことをやっていても奴は讃えられ<line-break>俺はあいつの分まで非難された。<end>"
EN: "Oh, yes. I was your father's close friend. And I suffered no end of bitterness for it. He knew how to charm people, how to win their favor. We could do the very same thing, and he would be praised while I took the blame for both of us.<end>"
KO: "그래, 네 아버지의 절친한 친구였지. 덕분에 아주 쓰라린 경험을 많이 했다. 겉모습이 좋고 인기를 끄는 데 능했지. 같은 일을 해도 녀석은 칭송받고 나는 그 녀석 몫까지 비난받았다.<end>"

## 1050017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいかげんにあいつのそばに<line-break>いるのにがまんできなくなってな。　　<line-break>…だから、殺してやったのだ。<line-break>俺はようやくあいつに勝ったと思った。<line-break>あいつの呪縛から逃れたと思った。<line-break>ところが今度はお前だ。クリュセイス！<end>"
EN: "In the end, I could no longer bear to be near him. So I killed him. At last, I thought I had beaten him. I thought I had escaped his hold over me. But then you came along, Khryuseis!<end>"
KO: "이제는 그 녀석 곁에 있는 걸 견딜 수 없게 됐지. 그래서 죽여 버렸어. 드디어 그 녀석에게 이겼다고, 그 속박에서 벗어났다고 생각했지. 그런데 이번에는 네가 나타났구나. 크류세이스!<end>"

## 1050018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前はロティの奴と<line-break>外面のよさから何までそっくりだ。<line-break>おまけに最近は小賢しくなってきて…<line-break>ついには、俺をここまで追いつめた！<line-break>…これ以上は放っておけんのでな、<line-break>お前にも消えてもらおうと思っている。<end>"
EN: "You're exactly like Lottie, right down to how good you are at making yourself look good. And lately you've become too clever by half... You've finally driven me into a corner! I can't leave you be any longer. So I've decided you'll have to disappear too.<end>"
KO: "너는 로티 녀석과 겉으로 사람 좋은 척하는 것까지 전부 닮았다. 게다가 요즘은 잔꾀까지 늘어서… 마침내 나를 여기까지 몰아붙였지! …이 이상 내버려 둘 수 없으니 너도 사라져 줘야겠다.<end>"

## 1050019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クックック。お前も相変わらず<line-break>度し難いバカだな、<value:$28>。<line-break>お前はその女に何をされたか知らんのだ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. You're still as hopeless a fool as ever, <value:$28>. You have no idea what that woman did to you.<end>"
KO: "크크크. 너도 여전히 구제불능의 바보구나, <value:$28>. 넌 그 여자에게 무슨 일을 당했는지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1050020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前が失敗したギルドの仕事。<line-break>あれはなぁ、クリュセイスが<line-break>お前をハメるために仕組んだのだよ。<line-break>そうして、お前の身柄を拘束し、<line-break>闘技場の戦いで<line-break>お前を始末しようとしたのだ。<end>"
EN: "That Guild job you failed―Khryuseis arranged the whole thing to entrap you. She meant to have you taken into custody, then dispose of you in the arena.<end>"
KO: "네가 실패한 길드 일 말이다. 그건 크류세이스가 너를 함정에 빠뜨리려고 꾸민 일이야. 그렇게 네 신병을 구속하고 투기장 싸움에서 널 없애려 했지.<end>"

## 1050021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだ、<value:$28>。<line-break>事実を知ってもなお、命を懸けて<line-break>守るほどの女か、そいつは？<end>"
EN: "Well, <value:$28>? Now that you know the truth, is she still worth risking your life to protect?<end>"
KO: "어떠냐, <value:$28>. 진실을 알고도 목숨을 걸고 지킬 만큼의 여자냐, 그 녀석은?<end>"

## 1050022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことはどうでもいい<end>"
EN: "That doesn't matter.<end>"
KO: "그런 건 아무래도 좋아<end>"

## 1050024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっはっは。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>そうだよな。迷うよなぁ。<line-break>　<line-break>だがな、事実を知られたからには、<line-break>お前も生かしておくわけには<line-break>いかんのだよ！<end>"
EN: "Ha ha ha! I understand. It's a difficult choice. But now that you know the truth, I can't very well let you live!<end>"
KO: "하하하. 그렇지. 망설여지겠지. 하지만 사실을 알아 버린 이상 너도 살려 둘 수는 없다!<end>"

## 1050025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まもなくディンガル軍が<line-break>リベルダム市内にやってくる。<end>"
EN: "The Dyneskal Army will enter Liberdam shortly.<end>"
KO: "곧 딘갈군이 리벨덤 시내로 들어올 거다.<end>"

## 1050026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クックック。<line-break>もう、町の門が開いているころだ。<line-break>ディンガル軍が入ってこれるようにな。<line-break>見返りに、リベルダム市街には一切<line-break>手をつけないこと、そして、俺が<line-break>ここの総督になることが決まっている。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. The city gates should be open by now, allowing the Dyneskal Army to march in. In return, Liberdam itself will be left completely untouched, and I'll be made governor here.<end>"
KO: "크크크. 이제 마을 문이 열렸을 때다. 딘갈군이 들어올 수 있게 말이지. 대가로 리벨덤 시가지에는 일절 손대지 않고, 내가 이곳 총독이 되기로 되어 있어.<end>"

## 1050027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうやら、俺の勝ちだな。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>さぁ、こいつらを<line-break>引っとらえてくれ！<end>"
EN: "It seems I've won. Now, seize them!<end>"
KO: "아무래도 내 승리군. 자, 이 녀석들을 붙잡아 줘!<end>"

## 1050028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンティノ・マモン！<line-break>貴様を逮捕する！<end>"
EN: "Antino Mammon! You are under arrest!<end>"
KO: "안티노 마몬! 너를 체포한다!<end>"

## 1050029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ば、バカな！　カルラは<line-break>俺の身柄を保障すると…。<end>"
EN: "I-impossible! Karula promised I would be safe...<end>"
KO: "마, 말도 안 돼! 카를라는 내 신병을 보장한다고….<end>"

## 1050030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞いておらんな。<line-break>ただ、逮捕しろと命令されただけだ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I heard nothing about that. My orders were simply to arrest you.<end>"
KO: "들은 바 없다. 그저 체포하라는 명령만 받았다.<end>"

## 1050031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、カルラめ！<line-break>だましおったな！<end>"
EN: "Damn you, Karula! You tricked me!<end>"
KO: "큭, 카를라 녀석! 속였구나!<end>"

## 1050034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、わたくし…。<end>"
EN: "I-I...<end>"
KO: "저, 저는….<end>"

## 1050035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この辺りはリベルダムの有力者の家だ。<line-break>全員逮捕しろ！　ひとりも逃がすな。<line-break>抵抗するようなら殺せ！<end>"
EN: "These houses belong to Liberdam's leading citizens. Arrest everyone! Don't let a single one escape. Kill anyone who resists!<end>"
KO: "이 주변은 리벨덤 유력자들의 집이다. 전원 체포해라! 한 명도 놓치지 마. 저항하면 죽여라!<end>"

## 1050036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…このままここにいては、<line-break>捕まるのも時間の問題…。<line-break>　<line-break>仕方ありません。<line-break>フゴーを頼りましょう。<end>"
EN: "...If we remain here, it's only a matter of time before we're caught. We have no choice. We'll have to ask Hugo for help.<end>"
KO: "…이대로 여기 있으면 잡히는 것도 시간문제…. 어쩔 수 없군요. 휴고를 의지해 봅시다.<end>"

## 1050037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、早く！<end>"
EN: "Hurry!<end>"
KO: "자, 어서!<end>"

## 1050038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ…、なるほど。<line-break>リベルダムを脱出させてほしいと。<end>"
EN: "Hmm... I understand. You want our help escaping Liberdam.<end>"
KO: "흠… 그렇군. 리벨덤에서 탈출시켜 달라는 거군.<end>"

## 1050039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル軍もあなた方には　　　　　<line-break>手を出せないはず。<line-break>　<line-break>リベルダムを脱出して、<line-break>解放軍本部まで送り届けてもらえれば、<line-break>それで十分です。<end>"
EN: "Even the Dyneskal Army should be unable to lay a hand on you. All we ask is that you help us escape Liberdam and see us safely to Liberation Army Headquarters.<end>"
KO: "딘갈군도 여러분에게는 손을 댈 수 없을 겁니다. 리벨덤을 빠져나가 해방군 본부까지 데려다주기만 하면 충분합니다.<end>"

## 1050040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その後の我々の活動にまでは<line-break>助力を求めません。<end>"
EN: "We won't ask you to assist with our activities after that.<end>"
KO: "그 이후 우리의 활동까지 도움을 부탁드리지는 않겠습니다.<end>"

## 1050041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クロイスコレクションが<line-break>欲しいのぉ。<end>"
EN: "I want the Krois Collection.<end>"
KO: "크로이스 컬렉션을 갖고 싶구나.<end>"

## 1050042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーほっほっほ。<line-break>そーですわねぇ。<end>"
EN: "Ho, ho, ho, ho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호. 그렇군요오.<end>"

## 1050043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それは…。<end>"
EN: "B-but that...<end>"
KO: "그, 그건….<end>"

## 1050044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたのお父上ロティの美術品。<line-break>あれをすべていただければ、<line-break>手をお貸しいたしましょう。<end>"
EN: "Your father Lottie's art collection. Give us all of it, and we shall lend you our aid.<end>"
KO: "당신의 아버님 로티가 가진 미술품. 그것을 전부 주신다면 힘을 보태 드리지요.<end>"

## 1050045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの中には、父の形見もあります。<line-break>全部というわけには…。<end>"
EN: "The collection includes mementos of my father. I can't give you all of it...<end>"
KO: "그 안에는 아버지의 유품도 있습니다. 전부는 좀….<end>"

## 1050046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぜんぶ、ぜんぶ。<end>"
EN: "All of it. All of it.<end>"
KO: "전부, 전부.<end>"

## 1050047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "全部だそうです。<end>"
EN: "All of it, apparently.<end>"
KO: "전부라고 합니다.<end>"

## 1050048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、我々は脱出の手配までで十分。<line-break>脱出後の護衛はあなたがやると？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Oh? So we need only arrange the escape, and you will provide the escort afterward?<end>"
KO: "호오, 우리는 탈출 준비까지만 하면 충분하다. 탈출 후 호위는 당신이 맡겠다는 건가?<end>"

## 1050049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その代わり、<line-break>形見の分はまけろと<line-break>言うわけですな？<end>"
EN: "And in return, you want us to waive the price of the keepsakes. Is that it?<end>"
KO: "그 대신 유품 몫만큼은 깎아 달라는 말씀이군요?<end>"

## 1050050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>あなた…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 당신….<end>"

## 1050051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よけいなお世話だのう。<end>"
EN: "No one asked you to interfere.<end>"
KO: "쓸데없는 참견이구먼.<end>"

## 1050052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々はもともと最初から最後まで<line-break>脱出を手伝うように依頼されています。<line-break>半分とは言え、横取りしようとは…<line-break>商売敵と見なして<line-break>よろしいのですね？<end>"
EN: "We were hired from the outset to see the escape through from beginning to end. Yet you mean to take half the job from us... We may regard you as a business rival, then?<end>"
KO: "우리는 처음부터 끝까지 탈출을 돕도록 의뢰받았습니다. 절반이라 해도 가로채려 하다니… 경쟁 상대로 봐도 되는 거죠?<end>"

## 1050053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>護衛は依頼者を<line-break>より安全に守る力がある方に<line-break>受ける権利があるもの。<end>"
EN: "Very well. The right to serve as escort belongs to whoever can keep the client safer.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 호위는 의뢰인을 더 안전하게 지킬 능력이 있는 쪽이 맡을 권리가 있는 법이지요.<end>"

## 1050054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どちらがより護衛者に<line-break>ふさわしいか…、<line-break>決着をつけましょうか。<end>"
EN: "Shall we settle which of us is better suited to the task?<end>"
KO: "어느 쪽이 더 수호자에 어울리는지… 결판을 내 볼까요.<end>"

## 1050055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むにゅー…。<end>"
EN: "Mnyuu...<end>"
KO: "우뉴우….<end>"

## 1050056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっ、ほっほっ、<line-break>むにゅー、ですわねぇー。<end>"
EN: "Ho, ho, ho... Mnyuu, indeed.<end>"
KO: "호호호, 말랑말랑하군요~.<end>"

## 1050057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お見事です。<line-break>脱出後の護衛はあなたに<line-break>お任せいたします。<line-break>我々は、脱出までの手配を<line-break>いたしましょう。<end>"
EN: "Well done. We shall leave the post-escape escort to you. We will handle the escape arrangements.<end>"
KO: "훌륭합니다. 탈출 후 호위는 당신께 맡기겠습니다. 우리는 탈출까지 준비를 하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1050058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴー美術館の美術品を運び出します。<line-break>その際に、美術品と一緒に<line-break>リベルダムを脱出していただきます。<end>"
EN: "We will be transporting the works of art from the Hugo Museum. You will leave Liberdam along with them.<end>"
KO: "휴고 미술관의 미술품을 반출합니다. 그때 미술품과 함께 리벨덤을 탈출해 주셔야 합니다.<end>"

## 1050059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勝った。勝った。<end>"
EN: "We won! We won!<end>"
KO: "이겼어. 이겼어.<end>"

## 1050060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まことに申しわけありませんが、<line-break>我々が護衛までお引き受けいたします。<line-break>よろしいですかな？<end>"
EN: "My sincerest apologies, but we shall be handling the escort as well. Is that acceptable?<end>"
KO: "정말 죄송합니다만 저희가 호위까지 맡겠습니다. 괜찮으시겠습니까?<end>"

## 1050061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかりました。<line-break>それで、どのような方法で？<end>"
EN: "...Very well. How do you intend to do it?<end>"
KO: "…알겠습니다. 그래서 어떤 방법으로?<end>"

## 1050062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴー美術館の美術品を運び出します。<line-break>その際に、美術品と一緒に<line-break>リベルダムを脱出していただきます。<line-break>手配いたしますので、<line-break>しばらくお待ちください。<end>"
EN: "We will be transporting the works of art from the Hugo Museum. You will leave Liberdam along with them. We will make the arrangements, so please wait a little while.<end>"
KO: "휴고 미술관의 미술품을 반출합니다. 그때 미술품과 함께 리벨덤을 탈출해 주세요. 준비할 테니 잠시 기다려 주세요.<end>"

## 1050063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界の商業と文化の中心地、リベルダム。　　<line-break>これまで、いかなる大国も手出しできず、<line-break>商人の富によって中立を保った自由都市。<end>"
EN: "Liberdam, the center of world commerce and culture. A free city whose merchants' wealth had preserved its neutrality, keeping even the mightiest powers at bay.<end>"
KO: "세계 상업과 문화의 중심지 리벨덤. 지금까지 어느 대국도 손대지 못했고 상인의 부로 중립을 지켜 온 자유도시.<end>"

## 1050064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、その美しい町並みを守るための門は<line-break>市民アンティノ・マモンの内通により<line-break>外敵に破られることなく内側から開かれた。<end>"
EN: "But the gates protecting its beautiful cityscape were opened from within through the treachery of one of its citizens, Antino Mammon, without ever being breached by an outside enemy.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그 아름다운 거리를 지키던 문은 시민 안티노 마몬의 내통으로 외적에게 부서진 것이 아니라 안쪽에서 열렸다.<end>"

## 1050065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンからひそかに進軍していたカルラ軍は、<line-break>たった一夜で、リベルダムを制圧し、<line-break>帝国に刃向かう者への見せしめとして、<line-break>リベルダムを徹底的に破壊した。<end>"
EN: "Karula's Army, which had been advancing secretly from Rocen, seized Liberdam in a single night and utterly destroyed it as a warning to all who would defy the Empire.<end>"
KO: "로센에서 은밀히 진군한 칼라군은 단 하룻밤 만에 리벨덤을 제압하고, 제국에 거역하는 자들에게 본보기를 보이기 위해 리벨덤을 철저히 파괴했다.<end>"

## 1050066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、解放軍の首謀者と目されていた<line-break>クリュセイス・クロイスは捕らえられず、<line-break>内通者アンティノの行方も<line-break>つかむことはできなかった。<end>"
EN: "Yet Khryuseis Krois, believed to be the mastermind of the Liberation Army, was not captured, nor could the whereabouts of Antino, the collaborator, be ascertained.<end>"
KO: "하지만 해방군의 수괴로 지목된 크류세이스 크로이스는 붙잡지 못했고, 내통자 안티노의 행방도 알아낼 수 없었다.<end>"

## 1050067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こうして、自由都市リベルダムの繁栄は、<line-break>ディンガル帝国の軍靴によって、<line-break>あえなく踏みつぶされた。<end>"
EN: "Thus, the prosperity of Free City Liberdam was all too easily crushed beneath the boots of the Dyneskal Empire.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 자유도시 리벨덤의 번영은 딘갈 제국의 군홧발에 허무하게 짓밟혔다.<end>"

## 1050068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回、命がけでわたくしを<line-break>助け出してくれたあなたの<line-break>勇気ある行動に感謝します。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>これからも、<line-break>わたくしを助けてくださいますか？<end>"
EN: "I am grateful for the courage you showed in risking your life to rescue me. <value:$28>, will you continue to help me?<end>"
KO: "이번에 목숨을 걸고 저를 구해 준 당신의 용기 있는 행동에 감사드립니다. <value:$28>, 앞으로도 저를 도와주시겠습니까?<end>"

## 1050069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>あなたがいてくれるのなら、<line-break>我々はまだ戦える。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. With you beside us, we can still fight.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 네가 있어 준다면, 우리는 아직 싸울 수 있다.<end>"

## 1050070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたは、英雄だ。<line-break>あんたさえいれば、<line-break>いつかは俺らが勝つ日が来る！<end>"
EN: "You're a hero. As long as we've got you, our day will come!<end>"
KO: "당신은 영웅이야. 당신만 있으면 언젠가 우리가 이길 날이 올 거야!<end>"

## 1050071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頼りにしています。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>わたくしには…<line-break>いえ、わたくしたち解放軍には<line-break>あなたのような人が必要なのです。<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you. I need... no, the Liberation Army needs someone like you.<end>"
KO: "믿고 있습니다. 저에겐… 아니, 저희 해방군에겐 당신 같은 사람이 필요합니다.<end>"

## 1050072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リベルダムの陥落により、解放軍も<line-break>大変ですが、がんばりましょう。<line-break>では、いったん解散です。<end>"
EN: "The fall of Liberdam has left the Liberation Army in dire straits, but we must press on. That will be all for now.<end>"
KO: "리벨덤 함락으로 해방군도 힘들지만 힘냅시다. 그럼 일단 해산입니다.<end>"

## 1050073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1050074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ごめんなさい。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>謝っても許してもらえるとは<line-break>思っていません。でも、一度、<line-break>はっきりと言っておきたかった。<end>"
EN: "…I'm sorry. I don't expect an apology to earn your forgiveness. But I wanted to say it plainly, just once.<end>"
KO: "…미안해요. 사과한다고 용서받을 거라 생각하진 않아요. 그래도 한 번은 분명히 말해 두고 싶었어요.<end>"

## 1050075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おじさまの言ったことは、<line-break>…本当です。　<line-break>　<line-break>わたくしには、<line-break>あなたに守ってもらえる資格など、<line-break>本当はないのです。<end>"
EN: "What he said... is true. The truth is, I don't deserve your protection.<end>"
KO: "아저씨가 말한 것은… 사실입니다. 저에게는 당신의 보호를 받을 자격 같은 건 사실 없습니다.<end>"

## 1050076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それだけ聞ければ、それでいい<end>"
EN: "That's all I needed to hear.<end>"
KO: "그것만 들으면 됐어.<end>"

## 1050077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この恨みは忘れない<end>"
EN: "I'll never forget this.<end>"
KO: "이 원한은 잊지 않겠어<end>"

## 1050078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え…、<value:$28>…。<line-break>本当に…、許してくれるの？<end>"
EN: "What...? <value:$28>... Will you really forgive me?<end>"
KO: "어… <value:$28>…. 정말… 용서해 주는 거야?<end>"

## 1050079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう……。<end>"
EN: "Thank you...<end>"
KO: "고마워요…….<end>"

## 1050081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あなたが解放軍を抜けることは、<line-break>同志には、わたくしから<line-break>伝えておきます。<end>"
EN: "Very well. I will inform our comrades myself that you are leaving the Liberation Army.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 당신이 해방군을 떠난다는 건 동지들에게 제가 전해 두겠습니다.<end>"

## 1050082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な…、なんですって！？<end>"
EN: "W-what did you say?!<end>"
KO: "뭐… 뭐라고요!?<end>"

## 1050083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、わかったわ。<line-break>今は逃げることだけを考えましょう。<end>"
EN: "O-okay. For now, let's just focus on getting away.<end>"
KO: "아, 알았어. 지금은 도망치는 것만 생각하자.<end>"

## 1050084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、こっちにいるのは<line-break>クリュセイス・クロイス！<line-break>こいつも逮捕しろ！<end>"
EN: "Hm? Khryuseis Krois is here! Arrest this one too!<end>"
KO: "음, 이쪽에 있는 건 크류세이스 크로이스! 이 녀석도 체포해!<end>"

## 1050085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふん、お人好しめ。<line-break>ならば、一緒に死ぬがいい。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. You softhearted fool. Then you can die together.<end>"
KO: "흥, 인정 많은 녀석. 그렇다면 함께 죽어라.<end>"

## 1050086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナイトメアの雫はどうした？<end>"
EN: "What happened to the Nightmare's Tear?<end>"
KO: "나이트메어의 물방울은 어떻게 됐지?<end>"

## 1050087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ないだと！<line-break>まさか、貴様が<line-break>盗んだんじゃないだろうな！？<end>"
EN: "It's gone?! Don't tell me you stole it!<end>"
KO: "없다고!? 설마 네가 훔친 건 아니겠지!?<end>"

## 1050088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいわけは依頼人の前でしろ！<line-break>この冒険者の面汚しが！<line-break>とらえろ！<end>"
EN: "Save your excuses for the client! You're a disgrace to adventurers! Seize this one!<end>"
KO: "변명은 의뢰인 앞에서 해라! 이 모험자의 망신아! 잡아라!<end>"

## 1050089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気がついたかしら？<line-break>まぁ、あれだけ殴られたら、<line-break>気を失うのもムリないわ。<end>"
EN: "Awake at last? Well, after a beating like that, no wonder you passed out.<end>"
KO: "정신이 들었나 봐? 뭐, 그렇게 두들겨 맞았으니 기절해도 무리는 아니지.<end>"

## 1050090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おはよう、荷物泥棒さん。<line-break>気分はいかが。<end>"
EN: "Good morning, baggage thief. How are you feeling?<end>"
KO: "좋은 아침, 짐 도둑 씨. 기분은 어때?<end>"

## 1050091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう言われても仕方がない状況よ。<line-break>非市民なんて、しょせんそんなものよ。<line-break>　<line-break>それより、感謝なさい。わたくしが、<line-break>あなたのために補償金を払って、<line-break>犯罪者ではなくしてあげたのだから。<end>"
EN: "Under the circumstances, you can hardly object to being called that. That's just what non-citizens are like, in the end. More importantly, you should be grateful. I paid the compensation for you and kept you from becoming a criminal.<end>"
KO: "그런 말을 들어도 어쩔 수 없는 상황이야. 비시민이란 결국 그런 존재니까. 그것보다 감사하도록 해. 내가 너를 위해 보상금을 내서 범죄자가 아니게 해 줬으니까.<end>"

## 1050092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このリベルダムではお金で<line-break>買えないものはないのよ。<end>"
EN: "There is nothing in Liberdam that money cannot buy.<end>"
KO: "이 리벨덤에서는 돈으로 살 수 없는 건 없어.<end>"

## 1050093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ちなさい。わたくしが<line-break>お金を払ったって言ったでしょう？<end>"
EN: "Wait. I told you that I paid for you, didn't I?<end>"
KO: "기다리세요. 제가 돈을 냈다고 했잖아요?<end>"

## 1050094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その分のお金を支払うですって？<line-break>ムリよ。あなたのような冒険者が<line-break>払える額ではないわ。<end>"
EN: "You intend to repay me? Impossible. No adventurer like you could afford such a sum.<end>"
KO: "그만큼의 돈을 내겠다고? 무리야. 너 같은 모험가가 낼 수 있는 액수가 아니야.<end>"

## 1050095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だからわたくし、<line-break>かわいそうなあなたに<line-break>チャンスを用意してあげたの。<end>あなたにはこれから、闘技場の<line-break>剣闘士として戦ってもらうわ。<line-break>もちろん、補償金の返済のために。<end>"
EN: "So I have graciously arranged an opportunity for you, poor thing.<end>From now on, you will fight as a gladiator in the arena. To repay the compensation, of course.<end>"
KO: "그래서 내가 불쌍한 당신에게 기회를 마련해 줬어.<end>이제부터 투기장의 검투사로 싸워 줘. 물론 보상금을 갚기 위해서지.<end>"

## 1050096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたに選択や主張の権利はないの。<line-break>さあ、魔道具屋、あの腕輪を。<end>"
EN: "You have no right to choose or object. Now, Magic-Item Dealer, bring me that bracelet.<end>"
KO: "당신에게 선택이나 주장할 권리는 없어. 자, 마도구상, 그 팔찌를.<end>"

## 1050097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フヒュヒュ。<line-break>クリュセイスお嬢様。<line-break>こちらに。<end>"
EN: "Fuh-hyu-hyu. Lady Khryuseis, over here.<end>"
KO: "후히히. 크류세이스 아가씨, 이쪽으로.<end>"

## 1050098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この腕輪は<line-break>夢魔の羽と呼ばれる魔道具よ。<line-break>　<line-break>夜、眠っている間に<line-break>あなたの体は闘技場に運ばれるの。<line-break>腕輪を外さない限り、ずっと。<end>"
EN: "This bracelet is a magic item called the Dream Demon Feather. At night, while you sleep, your body will be carried to the arena. It will happen every night until the bracelet is removed.<end>"
KO: "이 팔찌는 몽마의 날개라고 불리는 마도구야. 밤에 잠든 사이 네 몸은 투기장으로 옮겨져. 팔찌를 벗지 않는 한 계속.<end>"

## 1050099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ムリに外そうとしてもダメよ。<line-break>それは解錠の言葉なしでは外れない<line-break>呪いの品なのだから。<end>戦い、勝ちつづけて、補償金をすべて<line-break>わたくしに返してくれたら、その時に<line-break>外してあげるわ。いいわね？<end>"
EN: "Don't bother trying to force it off. It is cursed and cannot be removed without the unlocking words.<end>Keep fighting and winning until you've repaid every last coin of the compensation. Then I'll take it off for you. Is that clear?<end>"
KO: "억지로 빼려고 해도 소용없어요. 그건 해제의 말 없이는 벗길 수 없는 저주의 물건이니까요.<end>싸우고 계속 이겨서 보상금을 전부 제게 갚으면 그때 풀어드릴게요. 알겠죠?<end>"

## 1050100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を言ってもムダね。<line-break>あなたの試合は明日から組んであるわ。<line-break>あなたの休む部屋も用意してあります。<line-break>せいぜい、そこでゆっくり休むことね。<line-break>あなたには、夜はもう二度と<line-break>訪れないかもしれないのだから。<end>"
EN: "Nothing you say will make any difference. Your matches have already been arranged, starting tomorrow. A room has also been prepared for you. Make the most of it and get some rest. Night may never come for you again.<end>"
KO: "무슨 말을 해도 소용없어. 네 시합은 내일부터 잡혀 있어. 네가 쉴 방도 준비해 뒀고. 거기서 실컷 푹 쉬도록 해. 너에겐 밤이 두 번 다시 찾아오지 않을지도 모르니까.<end>"

## 1050101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec105-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、早く行きなさい。<line-break>あなたには迷う権利すらないのよ。<end>"
EN: "Now hurry up and go. You don't even have the right to hesitate.<end>"
KO: "자, 빨리 가. 넌 망설일 권리조차 없어.<end>"

## 1060000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml

## 1060001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来た、<value:$28>よ。　　<line-break>話はすでに聞いている。<line-break>手紙を渡してくれ。<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>. I've already heard the report. Give me the letter.<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다, <value:$28>여. 이야기는 이미 들었다. 편지를 다오.<end>"

## 1060002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君のような勇敢な同志が<line-break>我ら解放軍にいることを<line-break>感謝している。<line-break>我らの活動は、君が命がけで<line-break>救ってくれたクリュセイス様の<line-break>存在あってこそのものだ。<end>"
EN: "We are grateful to have a brave comrade like you in the Liberation Army. Our cause could not endure without Lady Khryuseis, whom you risked your life to save.<end>"
KO: "자네 같은 용감한 동지가 우리 해방군에 있어 감사하네. 우리 활동은 자네가 목숨 걸고 구해 낸 크류세이스 님이 있기에 가능한 것이지.<end>"

## 1060003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからも苦しい状況が続こうが、<line-break>ロセンの血統が存続する限り、<line-break>我らも戦っていくことができる。<line-break>君の今後の活躍に期待している。<line-break>これからも、よろしく頼む。<end>"
EN: "Hard times may lie ahead, but as long as Rocen's royal line survives, we can continue the fight. I expect great things from you. We will continue to rely on your help.<end>"
KO: "앞으로도 힘든 상황이 계속되겠지만 로센의 혈통이 이어지는 한 우리도 싸워 나갈 수 있다. 앞으로의 활약을 기대하겠다. 계속 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1060004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君のような勇敢な同志が<line-break>我ら解放軍にいることを感謝している。<line-break>　<line-break>君の勇気ある行動が、<line-break>我ら解放軍の士気と結束を<line-break>ますます高めてくれるに違いない。<end>"
EN: "We are grateful to have a brave comrade like you in the Liberation Army. Your courage will surely raise our morale and bind us more closely together.<end>"
KO: "자네 같은 용감한 동지가 우리 해방군에 있어 감사하네. 자네의 용기 있는 행동이 우리 해방군의 사기와 결속을 더욱 높여 줄 걸세.<end>"

## 1060005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういう意味でも<line-break>君のような英雄的存在は、　　　　　　<line-break>必要不可欠なものなのだ。<line-break>これからも、いくたの苦難に<line-break>立ち向かわねばならぬ<line-break>我らロセンの愛国者にとってはな。<end>"
EN: "That is another reason why a heroic figure like you is indispensable to us patriots of Rocen, who must face so many hardships yet to come.<end>"
KO: "그런 의미에서도 자네 같은 영웅적인 존재는 반드시 필요하네. 앞으로도 수많은 고난에 맞서야 하는 우리 로센의 애국자들에게는 말이지.<end>"

## 1060006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の今後の活躍に期待している。　　　<line-break>これからも、よろしく頼む。<line-break>我らが同志、ロセンの鐘の英雄よ。<end>"
EN: "I expect great things from you. We will continue to rely on your help, our comrade and hero of the Bell of Rocen.<end>"
KO: "앞으로의 활약을 기대하겠다. 앞으로도 잘 부탁한다. 우리의 동지, 로센의 종의 영웅이여.<end>"

## 1060007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君のような勇敢な同志が<line-break>我ら解放軍にいることを感謝している。<line-break>　<line-break>我らも君の闘志にあやかり<line-break>１日も早いロセンの復興を<line-break>成し遂げるつもりだ。<end>"
EN: "We are grateful to have a brave comrade like you in the Liberation Army. Inspired by your fighting spirit, we intend to restore Rocen as soon as possible.<end>"
KO: "자네 같은 용감한 동지가 우리 해방군에 있어 감사하고 있다. 우리도 자네의 투지를 본받아 하루라도 빨리 로센을 복구하겠다.<end>"

## 1060008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンの復興がなったあかつきには　　<line-break>君は伝説となり、ロセン国民の<line-break>子々孫々に語り継がれるだろう。<line-break>ロセン王国中興の女王<line-break>クリュセイスを救った<line-break>輝ける英雄<value:$28>、とね。<end>"
EN: "When Rocen is restored, you will become a legend, remembered by the people of Rocen for generations to come: the radiant hero <value:$28>, savior of Khryuseis, the queen who restored the Kingdom of Rocen.<end>"
KO: "로센이 부흥하는 날 자네는 전설이 되어 로센 국민의 자손 대대로 전해질 걸세. 로센 왕국 중흥의 여왕 크류세이스를 구한 빛나는 영웅 <value:$28>라고 말이지.<end>"

## 1060009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の今後の活躍に期待している。<line-break>これからも、よろしく頼む。<line-break>我らが同志、ロセンの鐘の英雄よ。　　<end>"
EN: "I expect great things from you. We will continue to rely on your help, our comrade and hero of the Bell of Rocen.<end>"
KO: "앞으로의 활약을 기대하겠다. 앞으로도 잘 부탁한다. 우리의 동지, 로센의 종의 영웅이여.<end>"

## 1060010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、ロティの決意が<line-break>揺らいでいるようだ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>娘の命を思う親の気持ちというのも、<line-break>わからないでもないが、これしきの<line-break>苦難でへこたれていたのでは…。<end>"
EN: "Lottie's resolve seems to be wavering of late. I can understand a parent's concern for his daughter's life, but if a hardship of this degree is enough to break him...<end>"
KO: "요즘 로티의 결심이 흔들리는 모양이다. 딸의 목숨을 생각하는 부모 마음을 이해 못 하는 건 아니지만 이 정도 고난에 꺾여서야….<end>"

## 1060011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しょせん、彼は武器商人にすぎない、<line-break>ということか…。<line-break>いや、すまん、こちらの話だ。<line-break>ロティ殿には今回のことに負けず、<line-break>よりいっそうの協力を願いたいと<line-break>お伝えしてもらいたい。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps he is nothing more than an arms merchant after all. No, forgive me. I was speaking to myself. Please tell Master Lottie that I hope he will not be defeated by this setback, and that we ask for even greater cooperation from him.<end>"
KO: "결국 그는 무기상에 지나지 않는다는 건가…. 아니, 미안. 이쪽 이야기다. 로티 공에게 이번 일에 꺾이지 말고 앞으로도 더욱 협력해 달라고 전해 주게.<end>"

## 1060013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おそらく、彼がおそれているのは　　　<line-break>娘の命ではなく、<line-break>自身の危険なのだろうな。<line-break>いずれにしても、我らにとって<line-break>彼の協力は必要だ。<line-break>これからも、よろしく頼む。<end>"
EN: "I suspect it is not his daughter's life he fears for, but his own safety. Whatever the truth, we need his cooperation. We will continue to rely on your help.<end>"
KO: "아마 그가 두려워하는 건 딸의 목숨이 아니라 자신의 위험이겠지. 어쨌든 우리에게는 그의 협력이 필요하다. 앞으로도 잘 부탁하네.<end>"

## 1060014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残虐なるディンガルの将軍カルラにより<line-break>ロセンの王族はすべて処刑された。<line-break>　<line-break>現在、ロセン王家の血を引くのは<line-break>ペウダ王の叔母君の娘に当たられる<line-break>クロイス家のクリュセイス様のみだ。<end>"
EN: "Karula, the ruthless general of Dyneskal, had every member of Rocen's royal family executed. The only person of Rocen's royal blood who remains is Lady Khryuseis of House Krois, the daughter of King Peuda's aunt.<end>"
KO: "잔혹한 딘갈의 장군 카를라에 의해 로센 왕족은 모두 처형됐다. 현재 로센 왕가의 피를 잇는 분은 페우다 왕의 숙모의 딸인 크로이스 가문의 크류세이스 님뿐이다.<end>"

## 1060015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我らロセンの存続は彼女に　　　　　　<line-break>かかっているのだ！<line-break><end>"
EN: "Rocen's very survival depends upon her!<end>"
KO: "우리 로센의 존속은 그녀에게 달려 있다!<end>"

## 1060016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ブゾリのやつめ、意外なところに<line-break>落とし穴を作ってくれたものだ。　<line-break>　<line-break>見た目通りの強欲ぶりを<line-break>発揮するかと思えば、こんなに早く<line-break>ロセンを復興させてしまった。<end>"
EN: "That wretch Buzori has thwarted us in a most unexpected way. I thought he would prove every bit as greedy as he looks, yet he has rebuilt Rocen with remarkable speed.<end>"
KO: "부조리 녀석, 뜻밖의 곳에 함정을 파 놓았군. 생긴 대로 탐욕만 부릴 줄 알았더니 이렇게 빨리 로센을 복구해 버리다니.<end>"

## 1060017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "皮肉なことに<line-break>ロセン臣民にもブゾリの支配を　　<line-break>受けいれる動きもあるらしい。<line-break>こんなことで反ディンガルの気運が<line-break>下がってしまっては、それこそ、<line-break>ディンガルの思うつぼではないか！<end>"
EN: "Ironically, it seems that even some of Rocen's subjects are beginning to accept Buzori's rule. If this weakens the will to resist Dyneskal, we will have played straight into Dyneskal's hands!<end>"
KO: "아이러니하게도 로센 신민들 사이에서도 부조리의 지배를 받아들이려는 움직임이 있는 모양이다. 이런 일로 반딘갈 기세가 꺾이면 그야말로 딘갈이 바라는 대로 아닌가!<end>"

## 1060018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "青竜将軍カルラ…、青い死神め。<line-break>奴はいったい何者なのだ。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>たった数週間で我らロセンをはじめ<line-break>東方国家６カ国を<line-break>滅ぼしてしまうとは…。<end>"
EN: "Karula, the Azure Dragon General... that blue reaper. Who in the world is she? In a matter of weeks, she destroyed six eastern nations, Rocen among them.<end>"
KO: "청룡장군 카를라… 푸른 사신. 녀석은 대체 누구란 말인가. 불과 몇 주 만에 우리 로센을 비롯해 동방 6개국을 멸망시키다니….<end>"

## 1060019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "信じられぬスピードだ。<line-break>ディンガルでは、あの青い死神のことを<line-break>神速の用兵家と呼んでいるようだ。<line-break>我らロセンの人材の窮乏と比べて<line-break>なぜ、ディンガルにはこうも<line-break>すぐれた人材が登場してくるのだ。<end>"
EN: "The speed is beyond belief. In Dyneskal, they apparently call that blue reaper a lightning-swift strategist. Why does Dyneskal produce one gifted leader after another while Rocen suffers such a dearth of talent?<end>"
KO: "믿기 힘든 속도다. 딩갈에서는 저 푸른 사신을 신속의 용병가라고 부르는 모양이군. 우리 로센의 인재 부족과 비교하면, 어째서 딩갈에는 이토록 뛰어난 인물이 계속 나타나는 것인가.<end>"

## 1060020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来た、<value:$28>よ。<line-break>話は、すでに聞いている。<line-break>手紙を。<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>. I've already heard the report. The letter.<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다, <value:$28>. 이야기는 이미 들었다. 편지를.<end>"

## 1060021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君のような勇敢な同志が<line-break>我ら解放軍にいることを感謝している。<line-break>　<line-break>我らの活動は、君が命がけで<line-break>救ってくれたクリュセイス様の<line-break>存在あってこそのもの。<end>"
EN: "We are grateful to have a brave comrade like you in the Liberation Army. Our cause could not endure without Lady Khryuseis, whom you risked your life to save.<end>"
KO: "자네 같은 용감한 동지가 우리 해방군에 있어 감사하네. 우리 활동은 자네가 목숨 걸고 구해 낸 크류세이스 님이 있기에 가능한 것이지.<end>"

## 1060022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからも苦しい状況が続こうが、　　<line-break>ロセンの血統が存続する限り、<line-break>我らも戦っていくことができる。<line-break>君の今後の活躍に期待している。<line-break>これからも、よろしく頼む。<line-break>我らが同志、ロセンの鐘の英雄よ。<end>"
EN: "Hard times may lie ahead, but as long as Rocen's royal line survives, we can continue the fight. I expect great things from you. We will continue to rely on your help, our comrade and hero of the Bell of Rocen.<end>"
KO: "앞으로도 힘든 상황은 계속되겠지만 로센의 혈통이 이어지는 한 우리도 계속 싸울 수 있다. 자네의 앞으로의 활약을 기대하겠다. 앞으로도 잘 부탁한다. 우리의 동지, 로센의 종의 영웅이여.<end>"

## 1060023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君のような勇者が解放軍にいることは<line-break>心強い限りだ。<line-break>　<line-break>君の勇気ある行動が、<line-break>我ら解放軍の士気と結束を<line-break>ますます高めてくれるに違いない。<end>"
EN: "It is deeply reassuring to have a hero like you in the Liberation Army. Your courage will surely raise our morale and bind us more closely together.<end>"
KO: "자네 같은 용자가 해방군에 있다는 건 든든하기 그지없네. 자네의 용기 있는 행동이 우리 해방군의 사기와 결속을 더욱 높여 줄 게 틀림없어.<end>"

## 1060024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういう意味でも<line-break>君のような英雄的存在は、　　　　<line-break>必要不可欠なものなのだ。<line-break>これからも、いくたの苦難に<line-break>立ち向かわねばならぬ<line-break>我ら解放軍にとってはな。<end>"
EN: "That is another reason why a heroic figure like you is indispensable to the Liberation Army, for we must face so many hardships yet to come.<end>"
KO: "그런 의미에서도 자네 같은 영웅적인 존재는 반드시 필요하네. 앞으로도 수많은 고난에 맞서야 하는 우리 해방군에게는 말이지.<end>"

## 1060025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の今後の活躍に期待している。　<line-break>これからも、よろしく頼む。<line-break>我らがロセンの鐘の英雄よ。<end>"
EN: "I expect great things from you. We will continue to rely on your help, our hero of the Bell of Rocen.<end>"
KO: "자네의 앞으로의 활약을 기대하겠네. 앞으로도 잘 부탁한다. 우리 로센의 종의 영웅이여.<end>"

## 1060026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君のような勇者が<line-break>解放軍にいることは心強い限りだ。　<line-break>　<line-break>我らの理想が大きければ大きいほど<line-break>ふりかかる困難は厳しくなるだろう。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "It is deeply reassuring to have a hero like you in the Liberation Army. The greater our ideals, the harsher the trials that will befall us.<end>"
KO: "너 같은 용자가 해방군에 있다는 건 든든한 일이야. 우리의 이상이 크면 클수록 닥쳐올 어려움도 더 혹독하겠지.<end>"

## 1060027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の今後の活躍に期待している。　　<line-break>これからも、よろしく頼む。<line-break>ロセンの鐘の英雄よ。<end>"
EN: "I expect great things from you. We will continue to rely on your help, hero of the Bell of Rocen.<end>"
KO: "앞으로의 활약을 기대하고 있다. 앞으로도 잘 부탁한다. 로센의 종의 영웅이여.<end>"

## 1060028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、雇い主であるロティ殿の決意が　　　　<line-break>揺らいでいるようだ。<line-break>　<line-break>しょせん、彼は武器商人にすぎないからな…。<line-break>我らもどうなるか…。<line-break>いや、すまん、こちらの話だ。<end>"
EN: "Our employer, Master Lottie, seems to be losing his resolve of late. He is only an arms merchant, after all. What will become of us now...? No, forgive me. I was speaking to myself.<end>"
KO: "최근 고용주인 로티 님의 결의가 흔들리는 것 같아. 결국 그는 무기상인에 불과하니까…. 우리도 어떻게 될지…. 아니, 미안하다. 이쪽 이야기다.<end>"

## 1060030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回のことは、彼が初めて知った　　　<line-break>我が身に及ぶ恐怖だったのだろう。<line-break>　<line-break>おそらく、彼がおそれているのは<line-break>娘の命ではなく、<line-break>自身の危険なのだろうな。<end>"
EN: "This must have been the first time he truly felt danger closing in on him. I suspect it is not his daughter's life he fears for, but his own safety.<end>"
KO: "이번 일은 그가 처음으로 느낀 자기 목숨에 대한 공포였겠지. 아마 그가 두려워하는 건 딸의 목숨이 아니라 자신의 위험일 거야.<end>"

## 1060031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いずれにしても、我らは彼に　　　　　<line-break>雇われた身だ。<line-break>これからも、よろしく頼む。<end>"
EN: "Whatever the truth, we are in his employ. We will continue to rely on your help.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 우리는 그에게 고용된 몸이다. 앞으로도 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1060032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml

## 1060033

## 1060038

## 1060039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、ダメだよ、ヴァン。<end>"
EN: "W-we can't, Van.<end>"
KO: "아, 안 돼, 반.<end>"

## 1060040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっさいんだよ、ナッジ。<line-break>ナッジえ、とめる？<end>"
EN: "Shut up, Nadge. What'd you have to nudge in and stop me for?<end>"
KO: "시끄러워, 나지. 나지나 멈추라고?<end>"

## 1060041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（うっ！　寒い！　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>その上、イヤすぎる…！）<line-break>　<line-break>（ナッジと、なぜ、をかけてるのは<line-break>　かろうじてわかるけれど、<line-break>　く、苦しくてつまらない…！　　　）<end>"
EN: "(Ugh! That was cold! And just plain awful!) (I can barely tell he's trying to make a pun on “Nadge” and “nudge,” but it's so strained and boring...!)<end>"
KO: "(윽! 추워! 게다가 너무 싫어…!) (나지와 '왜'를 엮은 건 간신히 알겠지만, 아, 너무 억지스럽고 재미없어…!)<end>"

## 1060042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（その上、このできの悪いギャグに<line-break>　僕の名前が使われているなんて…<line-break>　むごいよ、むごすぎるよ、ヴァン。）<line-break>（僕たち、友達じゃなかったのかい？<line-break>　僕、君になんかひどいことした？<line-break>　理不尽だよ、ひどいよ、ヴァン…！）<end>"
EN: "(And he used my name for such a terrible joke... That's cruel, Van. Too cruel.) (Aren't we friends? Did I do something awful to you? It's so unfair. This is horrible, Van...!)<end>"
KO: "(게다가 이 형편없는 농담에 내 이름까지 쓰이다니… 너무해, 너무하다고, 반.) (우리 친구 아니었어? 내가 너한테 뭘 그렇게 심하게 했니? 말도 안 돼, 너무해, 반…!)<end>"

## 1060043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さーて、<line-break>みんな凍ってるすきにっと…。<end>"
EN: "Right, while everyone's frozen stiff...<end>"
KO: "자, 그럼. 다들 얼어붙어 있는 틈에….<end>"

## 1060044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、遅いぜ。<line-break>手紙は開けちまったもんねー！<end>"
EN: "Too late! I've already opened it, so there!<end>"
KO: "이미 늦었어. 편지는 열어 버렸거든~!<end>"

## 1060045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…自分のギャグが寒いこと、<line-break>自覚してたんだね、ヴァン。<end>"
EN: "So you knew how cold your own joke was, Van.<end>"
KO: "…자기 개그가 썰렁하다는 건 알고 있었구나, 반.<end>"

## 1060046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すっげえ！　ディンガルの玄武将軍<line-break>ザギヴがロセン巡察に来る日程と行程が<line-break>めちゃ詳しく書いてある！<end>"
EN: "Whoa! This gives the exact schedule and route for Zagiv, Dyneskal's Black Tortoise General, to inspect Rocen!<end>"
KO: "대박! 딘갈의 현무장군 자기브가 로센 순찰에 오는 일정과 경로가 엄청 자세히 적혀 있어!<end>"

## 1060047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへっ、こんだけのものがあれば、<line-break>ロセンの奴らがヘボでも、<line-break>ザギヴは絶対にとれるな！<end>"
EN: "Heh! With information this good, even if those Rocen fools are useless, they'll be sure to get Zagiv!<end>"
KO: "헤헷, 이만한 게 있으면 로센 녀석들이 형편없어도 자기브는 반드시 잡겠어!<end>"

## 1060048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、こうやって手紙の中身を見るのって<line-break>悪いことかな？<end>"
EN: "Hey, is it really wrong to look inside a letter like this?<end>"
KO: "있지, 이렇게 편지 내용을 보는 건 나쁜 짓일까?<end>"

## 1060049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫だ。封なら簡単に元に戻る。<line-break>今までだってバレなかったんだ。<end>"
EN: "It's fine. The seal is easy to put back. No one's ever noticed before.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 봉인은 간단히 원래대로 돌아와. 지금까지도 들키지 않았잖아.<end>"

## 1060050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、そう怒るなって。<line-break>用心に越したことはないと思ってな。<line-break>少しチェックさせてもらってただけだ。<line-break>それよりも、この中身だ。<line-break>今までのチンケな情報とは精度が違う。<end>"
EN: "Come on, don't get so angry. I thought it was better to be cautious, so I took a quick look. More importantly, look what's inside. This is far more precise than the paltry scraps we've had until now.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그렇게 화내지 마. 조심해서 나쁠 건 없으니까. 조금 확인해 본 것뿐이야. 그보다 이 내용이 중요해. 지금까지의 시시한 정보와는 정확도가 달라.<end>"

## 1060051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルの内政統括、玄武将軍<line-break>ザギヴのロセン巡察日程及び行程だ。<line-break>　<line-break>ここまでの情報、ディンガルでも<line-break>四天王クラスじゃないと<line-break>手に入らないぜ？<end>"
EN: "It's the schedule and route for Zagiv's inspection of Rocen―the Black Tortoise General who oversees Dyneskal's domestic affairs. Even in Dyneskal, only someone on the level of the Four Heavenly Kings could get information like this.<end>"
KO: "딘갈 내정 총괄 현무장군 자기브의 로센 시찰 일정과 행로다. 이 정도 정보는 딘갈에서도 사천왕급이 아니면 못 구해.<end>"

## 1060052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お前さんには、教えといた方が　<line-break>よさそうだと思ってな。<line-break>　<line-break>…お前さんのディンガルのお友達、<line-break>結構、敵が多いらしいな。<end>"
EN: "I thought you ought to know. It seems your friend in Dyneskal has quite a few enemies.<end>"
KO: "…자네한테는 알려 두는 게 좋을 것 같아서 말이지. …자네 딘갈 친구들, 적이 꽤 많은 모양이더군.<end>"

## 1060053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねぇ、<value:$28>。<line-break>ボク、ピーンときちゃった。　<line-break>　<line-break>ほら、ロセン支部に届けろって<line-break>言われたあの手紙だよ。<line-break>ね、ね。絶対なんかあるよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. I've got a hunch. Remember that letter we were told to deliver to the Rocen branch? Come on. There's definitely something in it.<end>"
KO: "있지, <value:$28>. 나 딱 감이 왔어. 로센 지부에 전하라고 했던 그 편지 말이야. 있지, 분명 뭔가 있어.<end>"

## 1060054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、でもダメだよ。<line-break>人の手紙を勝手にのぞいちゃ。<end>"
EN: "No, wait. We can't. We can't just peek at someone else's letter.<end>"
KO: "아니, 그래도 안 돼. 남의 편지를 멋대로 보면 안 되지.<end>"

## 1060055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダメダメ、やっぱり<line-break>そんなことしちゃダメだよ。<end>"
EN: "No, no. We really shouldn't do that.<end>"
KO: "안 돼, 안 돼. 역시 그런 짓을 하면 안 돼.<end>"

## 1060056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああん、もぅ！<line-break>本当にダメなんだったら！<end>"
EN: "Oh, come on! If this really isn't allowed...!<end>"
KO: "아앙, 정말! 진짜 안 된다니까!<end>"

## 1060058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見てよ！<line-break>ザギヴって、ディンガルの<line-break>玄武将軍のことだよね。<end>"
EN: "Look! Zagiv is Dyneskal's Black Tortoise General, right?<end>"
KO: "봐! 자기브라면 딘갈의 현무장군 말이지?<end>"

## 1060059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのザギヴ将軍がロセンを巡察する<line-break>日程とかがすごい詳しく書いてある。<end>"
EN: "It gives a really detailed schedule and route for General Zagiv's inspection of Rocen.<end>"
KO: "그 자기브 장군이 로센을 순찰하는 일정 같은 게 엄청 자세히 적혀 있어.<end>"

## 1060060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…でも、普通、こういうのって<line-break>機密事項だよね？　ザギヴ将軍って<line-break>味方の中にも敵が多い人なのかな…？<end>"
EN: "But information like this would normally be secret, wouldn't it? Does General Zagiv have a lot of enemies even among her own allies?<end>"
KO: "…그래도 보통 이런 건 기밀 사항이지? 자기브 장군은 아군 중에도 적이 많은 사람인 걸까…?<end>"

## 1060061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ザギヴ将軍のところに　<line-break>行ってきてあげなよ。<line-break>　<line-break>…ボク、知ってるよ。<line-break>ザギヴ将軍とは…その…<line-break>いい、お友達…なんだろ？<end>"
EN: "You should go see General Zagiv. I know about you two. She's... well... a good friend of yours, isn't she?<end>"
KO: "…자기브 장군에게 다녀와 줘. …나 알고 있어. 자기브 장군과는… 그… 좋은 친구…인 거지?<end>"

## 1060062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえねえ、<value:$28>。<line-break>この手紙ちょっとだけ見てみようよぉ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>, let's take just a tiny peek at this letter.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, <value:$28>. 이 편지 조금만 보자.<end>"

## 1060063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなに固いこと言わなくてもいいと　<line-break>思うけどなぁ。ぼくは<value:$28><line-break>のことを心配してるんだよ？<end>"
EN: "You don't have to be so strict about it. I'm only worried about you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 딱딱하게 굴 필요는 없잖아. 난 <value:$28>이 걱정돼서 그러는 거야.<end>"

## 1060064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁ…。<line-break>ぼくって<line-break>どうして理解されないんだろ…。　<line-break>さびしいなぁ。<value:$28>も<line-break>わかってくれないなんて…。<end>"
EN: "Sigh... Why does nobody understand me? It's so lonely. Even you don't understand, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "하아…. 난 왜 이렇게 이해받지 못할까…. 외롭다. <value:$28>마저 이해해 주지 않다니….<end>"

## 1060065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え～、ぼくにその手紙をのぞけって　　<line-break>言うの？　ぼく、そういう<line-break>きれいじゃないことはイヤだなぁ。<line-break>でも、<value:$28>が<line-break>ど～してもって言うなら仕方ないよね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "What? You want me to peek at the letter? I don't like doing anything so underhanded. But if you absolutely insist, <value:$28>, I suppose I have no choice.<end>"
KO: "에~ 나한테 그 편지를 훔쳐보라는 거야? 난 그런 깨끗하지 못한 일은 싫은데. 그래도 <value:$28>가 꼭 해 달라고 한다면 어쩔 수 없지.<end>"

## 1060066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぁ～んだ。<line-break>別にきれいなことなんて書いてないや。<line-break>　<line-break>ディンガルのザギヴってえらい人が<line-break>ロセンを視察するときに使う行程が<line-break>書いてあるだけだよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that's all? There's nothing decent in it. It only gives the route some important person from Dyneskal named Zagiv will take while inspecting Rocen.<end>"
KO: "뭐야~. 딱히 예쁜 내용은 없네. 딘갈의 자기브라는 높은 사람이 로센을 시찰할 때 쓰는 행로가 적혀 있을 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1060067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ。貴様。<line-break>ブゾリ様に何か密告することがあるのか？<end>"
EN: "What do you want? Have you come to report something to Lord Buzori?<end>"
KO: "뭐냐, 네놈. 부조리 님께 밀고할 거라도 있나?<end>"

## 1060068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍本部の場所を密告する<end>"
EN: "Give away the location of Liberation Army Headquarters<end>"
KO: "해방군 본부 위치를 밀고한다<end>"

## 1060069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍本部の場所を密告しない<end>"
EN: "Don't give away the location of Liberation Army Headquarters<end>"
KO: "해방군 본부 위치를 밀고하지 않는다<end>"

## 1060070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何だと、テロリストどもの本拠を…！？<line-break>すぐブゾリ様にお知らせせねば…！<end>"
EN: "What? The terrorists' headquarters...!? I must inform Lord Buzori at once!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고, 테러리스트들의 본거지를…!? 당장 부조리 님께 알려야 해…!<end>"

## 1060071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーらいらっしゃい。どうしたのお、<line-break><value:$28>ちゃーん？　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>テロリストどもの親玉<line-break>ロティの命を救っといて、<line-break>今度はその子分どもを売りに来たの？<end>"
EN: "Well, hello there. What brings you here, <value:$28>, darling? You saved Lottie, the terrorists' ringleader, and now you've come to sell out his underlings?<end>"
KO: "어머, 어서 와. 무슨 일이니, <value:$28>~? 테러리스트 두목 로티 목숨은 살려 놓고 이번엔 그 부하들을 팔러 온 거야?<end>"

## 1060072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それとも、おバカさんにも、<line-break>アタシの命を奪うために、うそついて<line-break>ここまで入ってきたのかしら？<end>"
EN: "Or did you lie to the idiot too, just to get in here and kill me?<end>"
KO: "아니면 바보라도 나를 죽이려고 거짓말해서 여기까지 들어온 걸까?<end>"

## 1060073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロティたちを売りに来た<end>"
EN: "Here to sell Lottie and the others.<end>"
KO: "로티 일행을 팔러 왔다<end>"

## 1060074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ブゾリを殺しに来た<end>"
EN: "Here to kill Buzori.<end>"
KO: "부조리를 죽이러 왔다<end>"

## 1060075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ、賢いやり方ねえ？<line-break>全然美しくないけど。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ま、いいわ。<line-break>世の中のほとんどが<line-break>ゲスで成り立ってるんですもの。<end>"
EN: "Oho, clever, isn't it? Not the least bit elegant, though. Well, never mind. Most of the world is made up of scum anyway.<end>"
KO: "후후, 영리한 방법이네? 전혀 아름답진 않지만. 뭐, 됐어. 세상 대부분이 비열한 것들로 이루어져 있으니까.<end>"

## 1060076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがと。参考になったわ。<line-break>ところで、アタシの配下になるって<line-break>いうのは考えてくれてるのかしら？<end>"
EN: "Thanks. That was helpful. By the way, have you given any thought to serving under me?<end>"
KO: "고마워. 참고가 됐어. 그런데 내 부하가 되는 건 생각해 봤어?<end>"

## 1060077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ブゾリの配下になる<end>"
EN: "Serve under Buzori<end>"
KO: "부조리의 부하가 된다<end>"

## 1060078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ブゾリの配下にならない<end>"
EN: "Do not serve under Buzori<end>"
KO: "부조리의 부하가 되지 않는다<end>"

## 1060079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、わかったわ。<line-break>とにかく芯ってものがないのね。<end>"
EN: "I-I understand. You really don't stand for anything, do you?<end>"
KO: "그래, 알겠어. 아무튼 중심이란 게 없네.<end>"

## 1060080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。何か一定した立場も<line-break>ないようだし、単なる金目当てかしら？<line-break>いいわ、これがお礼よ。<end>"
EN: "Yes, you don't seem to have any firm allegiance. Are you only in it for the money? Fine. Here's your reward.<end>"
KO: "그래. 일정한 입장도 없는 것 같고, 그냥 돈이 목적인가? 좋아, 이게 사례야.<end>"

## 1060081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自由を愛する冒険者って<line-break>そんなものかもしれないけれど、　　　<line-break>　<line-break>あんたが今売り飛ばした<line-break>テロリストどもにも、<line-break>今まで生きてきただけの人生があるの。<end>"
EN: "Maybe that's what it means to be a freedom-loving adventurer. But even those terrorists you just sold off have lived whole lives of their own.<end>"
KO: "자유를 사랑하는 모험자란 그런 걸지도 모르지만, 네가 방금 팔아넘긴 테러리스트들에게도 지금까지 살아온 삶이 있어.<end>"

## 1060082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その中でいろんな人と出会ってきたの。<line-break>そして、父親と母親がいたはずなの。<line-break>　<line-break>もう少し、思いやりがあっても<line-break>いいんじゃない？<line-break>たとえ、あんなクズどもに対してでも。<end>"
EN: "They met all sorts of people along the way. They must have had mothers and fathers, too. Couldn't you show a little more compassion? Even to scum like that?<end>"
KO: "그 안에서 여러 사람을 만나 왔잖아. 그리고 아버지와 어머니도 있었을 거야. 조금은 배려하는 마음이 있어도 되지 않아? 설령 저런 쓰레기들에게라도.<end>"

## 1060083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行ってちょうだい。<line-break>優しいと生きにくいだろうけど、<line-break>優しくなければ生きてる価値もないわ。<end>"
EN: "Go on. Kindness may make life harder, but without it, life isn't worth living.<end>"
KO: "가 줘. 다정하면 살아가기 힘들겠지만, 다정하지 않으면 살아갈 가치도 없어.<end>"

## 1060084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ、おバカちゃんねえ。<line-break>ちょっとかわいいけど。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>うわあー、心が痛むわあ…。<line-break>こーんなおバカでかわいいコを<line-break>ぶち殺さないといけないなんて！<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, aren't you a silly little thing? A little cute, though. Oh, how my heart aches... To think I have to slaughter such an adorable little fool!<end>"
KO: "우후후, 바보네. 조금 귀엽긴 하지만. 와아, 마음이 아파라…. 이렇게 바보 같고 귀여운 아이를 죽여야 한다니!<end>"

## 1060085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やるじゃなーい？　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、テロリストに対して、<line-break>アタシが公正である必要なんて<line-break>何もないってこと、気づいてたァ？<end>"
EN: "Not bad at all. But did you realize I have no reason whatsoever to play fair with terrorists?<end>"
KO: "제법인데? 하지만 테러리스트 상대로 내가 공정해야 할 이유 같은 건 전혀 없다는 거, 알고 있었어?<end>"

## 1060086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様からあなたのこと聞いてるわ。<line-break>バカ王ペウダを懲らしめたんですって。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、マヌケよね。<line-break>同じ罠に２度引っかかるなんて。<end>"
EN: "Lady Karula told me about you. I hear you taught that idiot King Peuda a lesson. But you're quite the fool yourself, falling for the same trap twice.<end>"
KO: "칼라 님께 네 얘기는 들었어. 바보 왕 페우다를 혼내 줬다며. 하지만 멍청하네. 같은 함정에 두 번이나 걸리다니.<end>"

## 1060087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これってねえ、ペウダってバカな王様が<line-break>国民を怪物に食わせるために<line-break>作った仕掛けなの。<end>"
EN: "You see, this contraption was built by that fool King Peuda so he could feed his own people to monsters.<end>"
KO: "이거 말이지, 페우다라는 바보 왕이 국민을 괴물한테 먹이려고 만든 장치야.<end>"

## 1060088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "度胸だけは買ったげる。<line-break>命を助けてあげるわ。<line-break>また、会いましょ。じゃあね。<end>"
EN: "I'll give you credit for courage, at least. I'll spare your life. Until we meet again. Bye-bye.<end>"
KO: "배짱 하나는 인정해 줄게. 목숨은 살려 주지. 또 만나자. 잘 가.<end>"

## 1060089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーら、おかえりなさい。<line-break>どう？　テロリストどものこと、　　　<line-break>何かわかったのかしら？<line-break>うふふ、そう。<line-break>アジト、そんなところにあったのねー。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Well, look who's back. So? Did you learn anything about those terrorists? Hee hee, I see. So that's where their hideout was.<end>"
KO: "어머, 돌아왔네. 어때? 테러리스트들에 대해 뭔가 알아냈어? 후후, 그래. 아지트가 그런 곳에 있었구나.<end>"

## 1060090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、あなたの立場もあることだし、　　<line-break>今はそっちはほっときましょ？<line-break>これは報酬。また、情報お願いね？<end>"
EN: "Well, you have your own position to consider, so let's leave that alone for now, shall we? Here's your reward. Do bring me more information sometime.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 당신 입장도 있으니 지금은 그쪽은 내버려 두자고. 이건 보수야. 또 정보 부탁해?<end>"

## 1060091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、入り口でそんなことがあったの。<line-break>…新兵ね。<line-break>　<line-break>…しつけがなってないんだから。<line-break>あとで、たっぷり締め上げてあげるわ。<line-break>あなたに無礼を働いたんですもの。<end>"
EN: "So that's what happened at the entrance. A new recruit, I suppose. No discipline at all. I'll give that one a thorough dressing-down later for being so rude to you.<end>"
KO: "그래, 입구에서 그런 일이 있었구나. …신병이네. …교육이 영 안 돼 있군. 나중에 아주 단단히 혼내 줄게. 당신에게 무례를 저질렀으니까.<end>"

## 1060092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コウモリさんコウモリさん、<line-break>あなたはどっちの味方なの？<line-break>本当にどっちつかずの裏切り者さん？<end>"
EN: "Little bat, little bat, whose side are you on? Are you really a treacherous little fence-sitter?<end>"
KO: "박쥐 씨 박쥐 씨, 당신은 어느 편이야? 정말 어느 쪽도 아닌 배신자 씨야?<end>"

## 1060093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様の誘いには屈しなかったのに、<line-break>アタシの所には来てくれたのね？<line-break>　<line-break>うふふ、アタシの<line-break>大人の魅力のなせる技ってとこかしら？<line-break>それともオトコの色気？<end>"
EN: "You resisted Lady Karula's invitation, yet you came to me? Hee hee. Perhaps you couldn't resist my mature charms. Or was it my masculine allure?<end>"
KO: "카를라 님의 권유에는 넘어가지 않았으면서 내게는 와 줬네? 후후, 내 어른의 매력 덕분인가? 아니면 남자의 색기?<end>"

## 1060094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーら、いらっしゃーい。<line-break>どうしたのかしら？　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ロティ暗殺できなかったのを<line-break>挽回できるほどのものを<line-break>もってきてくれたのかしらあ？<end>"
EN: "Well, welcome. What brings you here? Have you brought me something good enough to make up for failing to assassinate Lottie?<end>"
KO: "어머, 어서 와아. 무슨 일이니? 로티 암살에 실패한 걸만회할 만한 걸가져온 거니이?<end>"

## 1060095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーら、いらっしゃーい。<line-break>どうしたの？　アタシの配下には<line-break>ならないって、出ていったのに。<end>"
EN: "Well, welcome back. What brings you here? You walked out after saying you wouldn't serve under me.<end>"
KO: "어머, 어서 와. 무슨 일이야? 내 부하는 안 되겠다고 나갔으면서.<end>"

## 1060096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんか、リベルダムの武器商人に<line-break>恨みでもあるわけ？<end>"
EN: "Do you have some sort of grudge against the arms dealers of Liberdam?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 리벨덤의 무기상인에게 원한이라도 있어?<end>"

## 1060097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、自由な冒険者って、こんな風に<line-break>金次第で自由に動くものなのかもね。<line-break>　<line-break>アタシ、テロリストみたいな狂信者は<line-break>醜いと思うけど、虚無的な無神論者も<line-break>あまり美しいと思わないの。<end>"
EN: "Well, perhaps this is what free adventurers do: go wherever the money leads. I find fanatics like those terrorists ugly, but I don't find nihilistic atheists especially beautiful, either.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 자유로운 모험가란 이렇게 돈에 따라 자유롭게 움직이는 존재일지도 모르지. 난 테러리스트 같은 광신자는 추하다고 생각하지만 허무주의적인 무신론자도 그다지 아름답다고 생각하지 않아.<end>"

## 1060098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロティを守れなかったじゃない。<line-break>情けないわね。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>その上、今度はその娘を裏切るの？<line-break>いい根性してるわね？<end>"
EN: "You couldn't even protect Lottie. How pathetic. And now you're going to betray the girl as well? You've got some nerve.<end>"
KO: "로티를 지키지도 못했잖아. 한심하네. 게다가 이번엔 그 딸까지 배신할 거야? 배짱 한번 좋네?<end>"

## 1060099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "暗殺を止めもしない。<line-break>次はその娘を売って金にしよう…。　<line-break>　<line-break>ま、自由な冒険者って、こんな風に<line-break>金次第で自由に動くものなのかもね。<line-break><end>"
EN: "You didn't even try to stop the assassination, and now you mean to sell the girl for money... Well, perhaps this is what free adventurers do: go wherever the money leads.<end>"
KO: "암살을 막지도 않아. 다음엔 그 아이를 팔아 돈을 벌자…. 뭐, 자유로운 모험가란 이렇게 돈에 따라 자유롭게 움직이는 건지도 모르지.<end>"

## 1060100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アタシ、テロリストみたいな狂信者は<line-break>醜いと思うけど、虚無的な無神論者も<line-break>あまり美しいと思わないの。<end>"
EN: "I find fanatics like those terrorists ugly, but I don't find nihilistic atheists especially beautiful, either.<end>"
KO: "난 테러리스트 같은 광신자는 추하다고 생각하지만, 허무적인 무신론자도 그다지 아름답다고 생각하지 않아.<end>"

## 1060101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーら、いらっしゃい。<line-break>情報を持ってきてくれたんですって？<end>"
EN: "Well, welcome. I hear you've brought me some information?<end>"
KO: "어머, 어서 와요. 정보를 가져왔다면서요?<end>"

## 1060102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何でもないだと？　こっちは仕事しているんだ！<line-break>お前のような暇人の相手をしている暇はない。<line-break>とっとと行け！<end>"
EN: "Nothing, you say? I'm trying to work! I have no time to entertain an idler like you. Now get out!<end>"
KO: "아무것도 아니라고? 이쪽은 일하는 중이다! 너 같은 한가한 사람 상대할 시간 없어. 얼른 가!<end>"

## 1060103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…解放軍の本部が破壊されたわ。　<line-break>ブゾリの仕業よ。<line-break>　<line-break>ところで、わたくしに<line-break>何か言うことがあるんじゃなくて？<line-break><end>"
EN: "The Liberation Army headquarters has been destroyed. Buzori was behind it. Now, isn't there something you ought to tell me?<end>"
KO: "…해방군 본부가 파괴됐어. 부조리 짓이야. 그런데 나한테 할 말이 있는 거 아니야?<end>"

## 1060104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセン総督府を見張っていた同志から、<line-break>あなたがブゾリと接触するのを<line-break>見たという報告が入ってるわ。<end>"
EN: "A comrade keeping watch on the Rocen Governor-General's Office reported seeing you make contact with Buzori.<end>"
KO: "로센 총독부를 감시하던 동지에게서 당신이 부조리와 접촉하는 걸 봤다는 보고가 들어왔어.<end>"

## 1060105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "裏切り者！<line-break>よくも、ぬけぬけと<line-break>わたくしの前に出られたものね！<end>"
EN: "Traitor! How dare you brazenly show yourself before me!<end>"
KO: "배신자! 뻔뻔하게도 내 앞에 얼굴을 내밀다니!<end>"

## 1060106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くやしいけど、逃がすしかないわ…。<line-break>あいつにかなう者がいないのだから…。<end>"
EN: "It galls me, but we have no choice but to let that one escape... No one can prevail against such an opponent.<end>"
KO: "분하지만 놓아줄 수밖에 없어…. 그 녀석을 당해낼 사람이 없으니까….<end>"

## 1060107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍本部が破壊されたよ。<end>"
EN: "Liberation Army Headquarters has been destroyed.<end>"
KO: "해방군 본부가 파괴됐어.<end>"

## 1060108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセン提督、ブゾリの手の者の<line-break>仕業だろう<end>"
EN: "Admiral of Rocen, this was likely the work of Buzori's agents.<end>"
KO: "로센 제독, 부조리의 부하들이 한 짓이겠지.<end>"

## 1060109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>釈明できるなら、してくれたまえ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>ロセン総督府を見張っていた同志から、<line-break>君がブゾリと接触していたという報告が<line-break>入っているのだがな。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. If you can explain yourself, then please do. A comrade keeping watch on the Rocen Governor-General's Office reports that you made contact with Buzori.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 해명할 수 있다면 해 보게. 로센 총독부를 감시하던 동지에게서 자네가 부조리와 접촉했다는 보고가 들어왔네.<end>"

## 1060110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゲスな裏切り者め！<line-break>よくも、私の前に出られたものだな！<end>"
EN: "You vile traitor! How dare you show yourself before me!<end>"
KO: "비열한 배신자 놈! 감히 내 앞에 모습을 드러내다니!<end>"

## 1060111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんな…。<line-break><value:$28>様が…、そんな…。<end>"
EN: "N-no... <value:$28>... It can't be...<end>"
KO: "그, 그럴 수가…. <value:$28> 님이… 그런….<end>"

## 1060112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイス、ショックだったろう。<end>"
EN: "Khryuseis, this must have come as a terrible shock.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스, 충격이었겠지.<end>"

## 1060113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの者があんなに卑劣な人物だと<line-break>見抜けなかった私が悪かったのだ。<line-break>つらい思いをさせたな。クリュセイス。<line-break><value:$28>め…。<end>"
EN: "The fault is mine for failing to see how despicable that person was. I'm sorry you've had to suffer, Khryuseis. Curse you, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "그자가 그렇게 비열한 인물인 줄 알아보지 못한 내 잘못이다. 힘들게 했구나, 크류세이스. <value:$28> 녀석….<end>"

## 1060114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひとりの生存者もいなかった。<line-break>悲しむべき事態だが…。<line-break>ん、どうしたのかね？<end>"
EN: "There wasn't a single survivor. A tragic state of affairs, but... Hm? What is it?<end>"
KO: "생존자는 한 명도 없었다. 슬픈 일이지만…. 음, 왜 그러는가?<end>"

## 1060115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まさか、<value:$28>様？<end>"
EN: "Surely not... <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "…설마, <value:$28> 님?<end>"

## 1060116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君が裏切り者だったのか。<line-break>悲しいよ、なぜそんなことを…！<end>"
EN: "So you were the traitor. This grieves me. Why would you do such a thing...?<end>"
KO: "네가 배신자였나. 슬프군, 왜 그런 짓을…!<end>"

## 1060117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec106-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>どうして…！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, why...?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 어째서…!<end>"

## 1060118

## 1070019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ。<line-break>お金をこの魔法の宝箱に入れてみないかい？<line-break>持ち金を増やすチャンスだよ。<end>"

KO: "안녕. 돈을 이 마법 보물상자에 넣어 보지 않을래? 가진 돈을 불릴 기회야.<end>"

## 1070020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たしか、<value:$28>さんだよね。<line-break>お金をこの魔法の宝箱に入れてみないかい？<line-break>持ち金を増やすチャンスだよ。<end>"
EN: "You're <value:$28>, if I remember correctly. Why not put some money in this Magic Treasure Chest? You might end up with more money than you started with.<end>"
KO: "분명 <value:$28>님이었지. 돈을 이 마법의 보물상자에 넣어 보지 않을래? 가진 돈을 늘릴 기회야.<end>"

## 1070021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "有名人の<value:$28>さんだね。<line-break>お金をこの魔法の宝箱に入れてみないかい？<line-break>持ち金を増やすチャンスだよ。<end>"
EN: "Why, it's the famous <value:$28>. Why not put some money in this Magic Treasure Chest? You might end up with more money than you started with.<end>"
