## 1070030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、罠の難易度を決めるよ。<line-break><value:$15>を押したら、数字が止まる。<line-break>それが、今回の難易度になるんだ。<end>"
EN: "All right, let's set the trap's difficulty. Press <value:$15> to stop the number. The number it stops on will be the difficulty this time.<end>"
KO: "그럼 함정 난이도를 정할게. <value:$15>을 누르면 숫자가 멈춰. 그게 이번 난이도가 되는 거야.<end>"

## 1070031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回の罠の難易度は<value:$1A>だね。<line-break>解除に成功すれば中身のお金は<line-break>２倍になるから、がんばって！<end>"
EN: "This trap's difficulty is <value:$1A>. If you disarm it successfully, the money inside will be doubled, so good luck!<end>"
KO: "이번 함정 난이도는 <value:$1A>야. 해제에 성공하면 안의 돈이 두 배가 되니까 힘내!<end>"

## 1070032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "左右に移動する判定ラインは<line-break><value:$15>で止まります。<line-break>青色の領域に止まれば解除成功です。<end>"
EN: "The timing line moves left and right. Press <value:$15> to stop it. Stop it in the blue area to disarm the trap.<end>"
KO: "좌우로 움직이는 판정선은 <value:$15>에서 멈춥니다. 파란 영역에 멈추면 해제 성공입니다.<end>"

## 1070033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おめでとう。これで箱の中身は<line-break><value:$1A>ギアになったわけだ。<line-break>もう宝箱がいっぱいだよ。<end>"
EN: "Congratulations. That brings the chest's contents to <value:$1A> Gea. The treasure chest is full now.<end>"
KO: "축하해. 이제 상자 안의 내용물은 <value:$1A>기어가 됐어. 보물상자가 벌써 가득 찼네.<end>"

## 1070034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう少し宝箱を大きくしようかな。<line-break>また、持ち金を増やしたくなったら<line-break>来てくれよな。待ってるからさ。<end>"
EN: "Maybe I should make the treasure chest a little bigger. Come back whenever you want to build up your funds again. I'll be waiting.<end>"
KO: "보물상자를 좀 더 크게 해 볼까. 또 가진 돈을 늘리고 싶어지면 와 줘. 기다리고 있을 테니까.<end>"

## 1070035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おめでとう。これで箱の中身は<line-break><value:$1A>ギアになったわけだ。<line-break>これを箱に戻して、さらに増やしてみないかい？<end>"
EN: "Congratulations. The box now contains <value:$1A> Gea. How about putting it back in the box and seeing if you can make even more?<end>"
KO: "축하해. 이제 상자 안의 내용물은 <value:$1A>기어가 됐어. 이걸 다시 상자에 넣고 더 늘려 볼래?<end>"

## 1070036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめちゃうのかい。残念だな。<line-break>最後まで挑戦してほしかったのに。<end>"
EN: "You're quitting? That's a shame. I was hoping you'd see the challenge through to the end.<end>"
KO: "그만두는 거야? 아쉽네. 끝까지 도전했으면 했는데.<end>"

## 1070037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、持ち金を増やしたくなったら<line-break>来てくれよな。待ってるからさ。<end>"
EN: "If you ever feel like making some more money, come on back. I'll be waiting.<end>"
KO: "또 가진 돈을 불리고 싶으면 와. 기다리고 있을게.<end>"

## 1070038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "惜しい！　ちょっとタイミングがずれたね。<line-break>残念だけど、箱の中身は消えちゃったよ。<line-break>どうだい、もう一度挑戦してみないかい？<end>"
EN: "So close! Your timing was just a little off. I'm afraid everything in the chest has vanished. How about giving it another try?<end>"
KO: "아깝다! 타이밍이 조금 어긋났네. 유감이지만 상자 안 내용물은 사라졌어. 어때, 다시 한번 도전해 볼래?<end>"

## 1070039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかい、残念だな。<line-break>また、その気になったら来てよ。<end>"
EN: "All right. That's a shame. Come back if you ever change your mind.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 아쉽네. 또 마음이 생기면 와.<end>"

## 1070040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは魔法の宝箱でね。中にお金を入れてから、<line-break>仕掛けられている罠を解除して、開けると、<line-break>なんと！　お金が２倍に増えているんだ。<line-break>ただし、罠の解除に失敗すると、<line-break>中に入れたお金は消えちゃうけどね。<line-break>それにしても、不思議な箱だろ？<end>"
EN: "This is a Magic Treasure Chest. Put some money inside, disarm the trap, and open it―and behold! Your money will have doubled. But if you fail to disarm the trap, all the money you put inside will vanish. Quite a mysterious chest, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "이건 마법의 보물상자야. 안에 돈을 넣고 설치된 함정을 해제해 열면, 놀랍게도 돈이 두 배로 늘어나지. 단, 함정 해제에 실패하면 넣은 돈은 사라져 버려. 정말 신기한 상자지?<end>"

## 1070042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml

EN: "Paralysis gas burst out, and<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1070044

EN: "Curse gas burst out, and<end>"
KO: "저주 가스가 뿜어져 나왔다<end>"

## 1070045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は呪われた。<end>"
EN: "was cursed.<end>"
KO: "는 저주에 걸렸다.<end>"

## 1070046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "宝箱を開けるには、<color:e>ディテクター<color:f>のスキルが<line-break>使えなくてはいけません。<line-break><color:b>グレモリイ<color:f>のソウルを宿してください。<end>"
EN: "To open treasure chests, you must be able to use the <color:e>Detector<color:f> skill. Harbor <line-break><color:b>Gremory<color:f>'s Soul.<end>"
KO: "보물상자를 열려면 <color:e>디텍터<color:f> 스킬을 사용할 수 있어야 합니다. <color:b>그레모리<color:f>의 소울을 깃들게 하십시오.<end>"

## 1070047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（ダメだ…。<line-break>罠の仕組みが複雑すぎて<line-break>解除できそうにない。）<end>"
EN: "(No... The trap's mechanism is too complicated. I don't think I can disarm it.)<end>"
KO: "(안 돼…. 함정 구조가 너무 복잡해서 해제할 수 없을 것 같아.)<end>"

## 1070048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この罠って難しすぎるわよ。<line-break>剣には自信があるけど、<line-break>こーゆー細かい作業って苦手なのよね。<end>"
EN: "This trap is far too difficult. I'm confident with a sword, but I've never been good at fiddly work like this.<end>"
KO: "이 함정 너무 어려워. 검에는 자신 있지만 이런 세세한 작업은 서툴거든.<end>"

## 1070049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うむ…これは難解な罠じゃな。<line-break>残念じゃが、わしの太い指では<line-break>やるだけ無駄というものじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm... This is a fiendishly difficult trap. I'm afraid there is little point in even trying with fingers as thick as mine.<end>"
KO: "음… 이건 어려운 함정이군. 아쉽지만 내 굵은 손가락으로는 해 봐야 소용없겠어.<end>"

## 1070050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えー！！　む、無理です！<line-break>こんなに難しそうな罠なんて<line-break>解除できっこないですよ。<end>"
EN: "What?! I-I can't! There's no way I could disarm a trap that looks this difficult!<end>"
KO: "에에!! 무, 무리예요! 이렇게 어려워 보이는 함정은 절대 해제 못 해요.<end>"

## 1070051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんじゃこりゃぁぁ！<line-break>複雑すぎて、さっぱりわかんねぇ。<line-break>解除できるヤツなんているのかよ。<end>"
EN: "What the hell is this?! It's so complicated, I can't make head or tail of it. Can anyone actually disarm this thing?<end>"
KO: "이게 뭐야아아! 너무 복잡해서 하나도 모르겠어. 이걸 해제할 수 있는 녀석이 있기는 해?<end>"

## 1070052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴメン…。<line-break>ボクじゃ役にたちそうもないや。<line-break>もっと修行しとけばよかったなー。<end>"
EN: "Sorry... I don't think I'll be much help. I should've trained harder.<end>"
KO: "미안…. 나로선 도움이 안 될 것 같아. 좀 더 수련해 둘걸.<end>"

## 1070053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "恥ずかしいけど、ダメみたい…。<line-break>私の手に負える罠じゃないわ。<end>"
EN: "I'm embarrassed to admit it, but I can't do this... This trap is beyond me.<end>"
KO: "부끄럽지만 안 될 것 같아…. 내가 감당할 수 있는 함정이 아니야.<end>"

## 1070054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや？　私にやらせるのですか？<line-break>でも、失敗すると思いますよ。<line-break>罠の解除は苦手なもので。<end>"
EN: "Oh? You want me to do it? I expect I'll fail, though. Disarming traps isn't my forte.<end>"
KO: "어라? 저한테 시키는 겁니까? 하지만 실패할 것 같은데요. 함정 해제는 서툴러서요.<end>"

## 1070055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無理無理、こんな複雑な罠。<line-break>あたしってば負ける戦は<line-break>しない主義なのよねぇ。<end>"
EN: "No, no. Not a trap this complicated. I make it a rule never to fight a losing battle.<end>"
KO: "무리야, 무리. 이렇게 복잡한 함정은. 난 질 싸움은 안 하는 주의거든.<end>"

## 1070056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃんゴメン！<line-break>俺、解除する自信がないんだ。<line-break>だから勘弁してくれよ。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, sis! I'm not confident I can disarm it, so let me off this time, all right?<end>"
KO: "누나 미안! 나, 해제할 자신이 없어. 그러니까 봐줘.<end>"

## 1070057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この罠、ずいぶん複雑な構造ね…。<line-break>正直言って私には解除できないわ。<line-break>もっと器用な人を選ぶべきじゃない？<end>"
EN: "This trap has an awfully complicated mechanism... To be honest, I can't disarm it. Shouldn't you choose someone with better hands for this?<end>"
KO: "이 함정, 구조가 꽤 복잡하네…. 솔직히 나는 해제 못 하겠어. 더 손재주 좋은 사람을 고르는 게 낫지 않아?<end>"

## 1070058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まいったな…。<line-break>悪いが俺には解除不可能だ。<line-break>構造がさっぱりわかんねえよ。<end>"
EN: "This is bad... Sorry, but there's no way I can disarm it. I can't make any sense of the mechanism.<end>"
KO: "곤란한데…. 미안하지만 난 해제할 수 없어. 구조를 전혀 모르겠어.<end>"

## 1070059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くそっ！<line-break>俺の手に負えない罠とはな…。<end>"
EN: "Damn it! A trap even I can't handle...<end>"
KO: "젠장! 나로서도 감당 못 할 함정이라니….<end>"

## 1070060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うむ、この罠はずいぶんと<line-break>やっかいな構造になっておるのう。<line-break>今のわしでは失敗しそうじゃぞ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. This trap has a most troublesome mechanism. As I am now, I fear I would fail.<end>"
KO: "음, 이 함정은 꽤 까다로운 구조구먼. 지금의 나로선 실패할 것 같아.<end>"

## 1070061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめん、<value:$28>…。<line-break>今の僕じゃ解除できそうもない。<line-break>役に立てなくてくやしいよ…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, <value:$28>... I don't think I can disarm it as I am now. I hate not being able to help.<end>"
KO: "미안해, <value:$28>…. 지금의 나로선 해제할 수 없을 것 같아. 도움이 못 돼서 분해….<end>"

## 1070062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…<line-break>これは厄介な罠だな。<line-break>悪いが役に立てそうもない。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... This is a troublesome trap. Sorry, but I doubt I'll be of any use.<end>"
KO: "후후후… 이건 까다로운 함정이군. 미안하지만 도움이 될 것 같진 않아.<end>"

## 1070063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なによ、なによ！<line-break>どーして高貴なエルフのあたくしが<line-break>汚らしい箱を触らなきゃいけないのよ！<end>"
EN: "What? What is this?! Why should a noble Elf such as I have to touch this filthy box?!<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 뭐야! 왜 고귀한 엘프인 이 몸이 더러운 상자를 만져야 하는 거야!<end>"

## 1070064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふ、不覚なりぃ！<line-break>罠の仕組みが複雑すぎて、<line-break>ダジャレが思いつきませぬぅ！<end>"
EN: "A-a disgrace! The trap's mechanism is so complicated that I cannot even think of a pun!<end>"
KO: "부, 불찰이옵니다! 함정 구조가 너무 복잡해서 말장난이 떠오르지 않사옵니다!<end>"

## 1070065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんねぇ、<value:$28>。<line-break>ルルアンタじゃ解除するのは、<line-break>無理みたいなの。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, <value:$28>. It looks like disarming this is too much for Lulluantha.<end>"
KO: "미안해, <value:$28>. 루루안타로는 해제하기 힘든가 봐.<end>"

## 1070066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>無理だ…。<end>"
EN: "...... I can't do it...<end>"
KO: "………………. 무리야….<end>"

## 1070067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ！<line-break>俺ともあろうものが、<line-break>罠のひとつも解除できんとはな…<end>"
EN: "Tch! To think that even I can't disarm a mere trap...<end>"
KO: "칫! 나 정도 되는 사람이 함정 하나도 해제하지 못하다니…<end>"

## 1070068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う～ん困ったなぁ。<line-break>手先の器用さには自信があるけど、<line-break>この罠は手に負えないみたい。<end>"
EN: "Hmm, this is a problem. I'm proud of my dexterity, but this trap seems beyond even me.<end>"
KO: "으음, 곤란하네. 손재주에는 자신 있지만 이 함정은 내 손에 벅찬가 봐.<end>"

## 1070069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！　<line-break>この私に解除できない罠があるとは…。<end>"
EN: "Gah! To think there could be a trap that even I cannot disarm...<end>"
KO: "큭! 이 내가 해제하지 못하는 함정이 있다니….<end>"

## 1070070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは…<line-break>申し訳ありません。<line-break>私の手には負えないようです。<end>"
EN: "This is... My apologies. It appears to be beyond my abilities.<end>"
KO: "이건… 죄송합니다. 제 힘으로는 어쩔 수 없겠군요.<end>"

## 1070071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんね。お兄ちゃん。<line-break>難しすぎて私には解除できないよ。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, big brother. It's too difficult for me to disarm.<end>"
KO: "미안해, 오빠. 너무 어려워서 난 해제할 수 없어.<end>"

## 1070072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むぅ…<line-break>絵画のようにはいかんようじゃ。<line-break>ワシの手に負えそうにないわい。<end>"
EN: "Hmm... This doesn't work like painting. It seems to be beyond me.<end>"
KO: "음… 그림처럼은 안 되는 모양이군. 내 힘으로는 어쩔 수 없겠어.<end>"

## 1070073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ…<line-break>ここまで複雑だとお手上げだ。<line-break>私も修行が足らないな。<end>"
EN: "Good grief... Something this complicated is beyond me. I still have much to learn.<end>"
KO: "이런… 여기까지 복잡하면 손쓸 수 없군. 나도 수련이 부족해.<end>"

## 1070074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この罠、複雑すぎて俺には無理だ。<line-break>昔からこういう作業は<line-break>得意じゃないんだ。<end>"
EN: "This trap is too complicated for me. I've never been good at this sort of work.<end>"
KO: "이 함정은 너무 복잡해서 나한텐 무리야. 예전부터 이런 작업은 영 소질이 없었거든.<end>"

## 1070075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こういう作業は得意なんだけど、<line-break>この罠は複雑すぎて手に負えないわ。<line-break><end>"
EN: "I'm usually good at this sort of work, but this trap is too complicated for me.<end>"
KO: "이런 작업은 자신 있는데, 이 함정은 너무 복잡해서 손쓸 수가 없네.<end>"

## 1070076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私ではこの罠を<line-break>解除できそうにありません。<end>"
EN: "I don't believe I can disarm this trap.<end>"
KO: "저로서는 이 함정을 해제할 수 없을 것 같습니다.<end>"

## 1070077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "難しくって私だと失敗しそうです。<line-break>お役に立てなくてすみません。<end>"
EN: "It's so difficult that I would probably fail. I'm sorry I can't be of help.<end>"
KO: "너무 어려워서 제가 하면 실패할 것 같아요. 도움이 못 돼서 죄송해요.<end>"

## 1070078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは僕の手には負えないみたいだ。<line-break>力になれなくてすまない。<end>"
EN: "It seems this is beyond me. I'm sorry I can't help.<end>"
KO: "이건 내 힘으로는 어려울 것 같아. 도움이 못 돼서 미안해.<end>"

## 1070079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec107-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1070080

## 1080000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（エンシャントの南東にある賢者の森。<line-break>　その奥深くに猫屋敷と呼ばれる<line-break>　誰も近づけない館があるらしい）<line-break>（噂によると、唯一運命に選ばれた者<line-break>　だけが、猫屋敷を見つけることが<line-break>　できるという）<end>"
EN: "(Sage's Forest lies southeast of Ancient. Deep within it, there is said to be a manor known as Cat Manor that no one can approach.) (Rumor has it that only one chosen by fate can find Cat Manor.)<end>"
KO: "(엔샨트 남동쪽에 있는 현자의 숲. 그 깊숙한 곳에는 고양이 저택이라 불리는 누구도 다가갈 수 없는 저택이 있다고 한다.) (소문에 따르면 오직 운명에 선택받은 자만이 고양이 저택을 찾을 수 있다고 한다.)<end>"

## 1080001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森にあるっていう謎の屋敷ね。<line-break>そこに入った人も、見た人もいないのに<line-break>あるってことだけ知られてる。<line-break>不思議な話よね。運命に選ばれた者<line-break>だけが発見できて、館に入れるって<line-break>いうけど本当かしら。<end>"
EN: "You mean the mysterious manor said to be in Sage's Forest. No one has ever seen it or gone inside, yet its existence is known. Strange, isn't it? They say only one chosen by fate can find the manor and enter it, but I wonder if that's true.<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲에 있다는 수수께끼의 저택 말이지. 들어간 사람도 본 사람도 없는데 존재한다는 것만 알려져 있어. 이상한 이야기야. 운명에 선택된 자만 발견해서 저택에 들어갈 수 있다는데 정말일까.<end>"

## 1080002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森の奥深くにひっそりと建つ、<line-break>誰も見つけることのできない謎の<line-break>屋敷のことじゃな。<line-break>唯一運命に選ばれた者のみが見つける<line-break>ことができると言われておる。<line-break>お主も挑戦してみてはどうじゃ？<end>"
EN: "Ah, the mysterious manor hidden deep within Sage's Forest, which no one can find. They say only one chosen by fate can discover it. Why not try your luck?<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲 깊숙한 곳에 조용히 서 있는, 누구도 찾을 수 없는 수수께끼의 저택 말이군. 오직 운명에 선택받은 자만 찾을 수 있다고 하지. 자네도 도전해 보는 게 어떤가?<end>"

## 1080003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫がたくさんいるから猫屋敷なんです。<line-break>うん、きっとそうに違いありません。<line-break>そして屋敷には猫顔の主人がひとり…。<line-break>私ってけっこう想像力豊かですよね。<line-break>たくさんの猫に囲まれた猫顔の主人…。<line-break>ちょっと見てみたくなっちゃいました。<end>"
EN: "It must be called Cat Manor because there are lots of cats there. Yes, that has to be it. And there is a single cat-faced owner at the manor... I have quite the imagination, don't I? A cat-faced owner surrounded by lots of cats... Now I rather want to see it.<end>"
KO: "고양이가 많으니까 고양이 저택인 거예요. 응, 분명 그래요. 그리고 저택에는 고양이 얼굴을 한 주인이 한 명…. 저 상상력이 꽤 풍부하죠? 수많은 고양이에 둘러싸인 고양이 얼굴 주인…. 조금 보고 싶어졌어요.<end>"

## 1080005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東にある賢者の森、<line-break>そこの奥深くに建っている屋敷のこと<line-break>だよね。誰も見つけられないって噂の。<line-break>ボク、その屋敷に興味あるな。<line-break>運命に選ばれた人しか見つけられない<line-break>屋敷なんて不思議だものね。<end>"
EN: "You mean the manor deep in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient? The one no one can find. I'm curious about it. A manor that only someone chosen by fate can find sounds so mysterious.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽에 있는 현자의 숲, 그 깊숙한 곳에 있다는 저택 말이지? 아무도 찾지 못한다는 소문의. 나, 그 저택에 관심 있어. 운명에 선택된 사람만 찾을 수 있는 저택이라니 신기하잖아.<end>"

## 1080006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森はエンシャントの南東よ。<line-break>しかし、その奥にあるという屋敷には<line-break>まだ誰もたどりつけていない…。<end>"
EN: "Sage's Forest is southeast of Ancient. But no one has yet reached the manor said to lie deep within it...<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲은 엔샨트 남동쪽이야. 하지만 그 안쪽에 있다는 저택에는 아직 아무도 도착하지 못했어….<end>"

## 1080008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者様が住んでるって噂の屋敷ね。<line-break>場所はエンシャントの南東にある<line-break>賢者の森だったかな？<line-break>もしかして、運命に選ばれた者のみが<line-break>発見できるって話を聞いて挑戦する気？<line-break>けっこう自信家なんだね。<end>"
EN: "The manor where the sage is rumored to live? Wasn't it in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient? Don't tell me you heard that only one chosen by fate can find it and now mean to try. You're pretty confident, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "현자님이 산다는 소문의 저택 말이지. 장소는 엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲이었던가? 설마 운명에 선택받은 자만 발견할 수 있다는 말을 듣고 도전하려는 거야? 꽤 자신만만하네.<end>"

## 1080009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東に賢者の森って<line-break>あるだろ。その森の奥に建ってる館が<line-break>猫屋敷らしいよ。<line-break>でも、姉ちゃん。その館には誰も<line-break>入れないっていうぜ。あっ、でも運命に<line-break>選ばれた人間なら入れるんだったかな。<end>"
EN: "You know Sage's Forest southeast of Ancient, right? Apparently the manor deep in that forest is Cat Manor. But listen, sis, they say no one can enter it. Oh, wait... Maybe a person chosen by fate can.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽에 현자의 숲이 있잖아. 그 숲 깊숙한 곳에 있는 저택이 고양이 저택이라나 봐. 하지만 누나, 그 저택엔 아무도 들어갈 수 없대. 아, 그래도 운명에 선택된 사람이라면 들어갈 수 있었나.<end>"

## 1080010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森は確かにエンシャントの<line-break>南東にある。ただし、その奥の<line-break>猫屋敷までは、誰も至っていないわ。<end>"
EN: "Sage's Forest is indeed southeast of Ancient. However, no one has ever made it as far as Cat Manor deep within it.<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲은 확실히 엔샨트 남동쪽에 있어. 하지만 그 안쪽의 고양이 저택까지 간 사람은 아무도 없어.<end>"

## 1080011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、運命に選ばれた者だけが<line-break>見つけることのできるって噂の屋敷ね。<line-break>賢者の森の奥深くにあるんだろ。<line-break>ま、噂なんてのは正誤を確認するまでは<line-break>嘘と一緒だからな。どこまでホントか<line-break>わからんが行ってみるかい？<end>"
EN: "Oh, the manor rumored to be found only by one chosen by fate. It's deep in Sage's Forest, right? Well, until a rumor's been proven true or false, it may as well be a lie. No telling how much of this one's true, but shall we go see?<end>"
KO: "아, 운명에 선택된 자만 찾을 수 있다는 소문의 저택 말이지. 현자의 숲 깊숙한 곳에 있다던데. 뭐, 소문은 사실인지 확인하기 전까진 거짓말이나 마찬가지니까. 어디까지 진짜인지 모르겠지만 가 볼래?<end>"

## 1080012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命に選ばれた者のみが発見できる<line-break>という屋敷のことか…。<line-break>そんなものに興味を持ってどうする。<line-break>俺にはそんなくだらぬ噂を確かめる<line-break>暇などない。<end>"
EN: "The manor that supposedly can be found only by one chosen by fate...? Why take an interest in such a thing? I have no time to investigate such foolish rumors.<end>"
KO: "운명에 선택받은 자만 발견할 수 있다는 저택 말인가… 그런 것에 관심을 가져서 뭘 하겠다는 거지. 난 그런 시시한 소문을 확인할 시간 따윈 없어.<end>"

## 1080013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森の奥にある屋敷のことじゃ。<line-break>そこには運命に選ばれた者しか<line-break>入れんと言われておる。<line-break>お主が運命に選ばれた者かどうか<line-break>確かめてみるためにも行ってみよう<line-break>ではないか。<end>"
EN: "The manor deep in Sage's Forest. It is said that only one chosen by fate may enter. Let us go, if only to see whether fate has chosen you.<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲 깊숙한 곳에 있는 저택 말이다. 그곳에는 운명에 선택받은 자만 들어갈 수 있다고 하지. 네가 운명에 선택받은 자인지 확인해 보기 위해서라도 가 보지 않겠나.<end>"

## 1080014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東、賢者の森にある<line-break>謎の屋敷のことだよ。賢者様が住むと<line-break>いう噂もあるみたいだね。<line-break>でも、誰もそこにたどり着いていない。<line-break>運命に選ばれた者だけが猫屋敷に<line-break>入ることができるんだって。<end>"
EN: "It's the mysterious manor in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient. There even seems to be a rumor that a sage lives there. But no one has ever reached it. They say only one chosen by fate can enter Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲에 있는 수수께끼의 저택 말이야. 현자님이 산다는 소문도 있는 모양이네. 하지만 아무도 거기에 도착하지 못했어. 운명에 선택된 자만 고양이 저택에 들어갈 수 있대.<end>"

## 1080015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東に、<line-break>賢者の森はある。<line-break><line-break>が、そこにある賢者の屋敷にまで<line-break>たどりつけるのは、運命に選ばれた<line-break>者だけだという話だ…。<end>"
EN: "Sage's Forest lies southeast of Ancient. But they say only one chosen by fate can reach the sage's manor within it...<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽에 현자의 숲이 있다. 하지만 그곳의 현자의 저택까지 도달할 수 있는 건 운명에 선택받은 자뿐이라는 이야기다….<end>"

## 1080016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫屋敷ですって？　どーーーして<line-break>高貴なエルフのアタクシが人間の<line-break>噂話を知ってなくてはならないのよ！<line-break>高貴で気品あふれるエルフには<line-break>人間のように噂をするなんてみにくい<line-break>習慣はないの。覚えておきなさい。<end>"
EN: "Cat Manor? Whyyy should a noble Elf such as myself be expected to know human gossip?! Noble, refined Elves do not have the ugly human habit of gossiping. Remember that.<end>"
KO: "고양이 저택이라고? 도대체 왜 고귀한 엘프인 이 몸이 인간들의 소문 따위를 알아야 하는 거야! 고귀하고 기품 넘치는 엘프에게는 인간처럼 소문이나 떠드는 추한 습관은 없어. 기억해 둬.<end>"

## 1080017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫屋敷ボゲー<end>"
EN: "Cat Manor Bogee<end>"
KO: "고양이 저택 보게<end>"

## 1080018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東にある賢者の森の<line-break>奥に森の賢者さんが住んでる屋敷が<line-break>あるの。それが猫屋敷だよぉ。<line-break>でもね、運命に選ばれた人しか入ること<line-break>できないんだって。不思議でしょ？<line-break>ね、行ってみようよぉ。<end>"
EN: "Deep in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient, there's a manor where the sage of the forest lives. That's Cat Manor. But only someone chosen by fate can go inside. Mysterious, isn't it? Come on, let's go see!<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲 깊숙한 곳에 숲의 현자님이 사는 저택이 있어. 그게 고양이 저택이야아. 그런데 운명에 선택된 사람만 들어갈 수 있대. 신기하지? 자, 가 보자아.<end>"

## 1080019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>エンシャントの南東にある賢者の森。<line-break>その奥に建つと言われる屋敷のことだ。<line-break>運命に選ばれた者しか入れないという。<end>"
EN: "............... The manor said to stand deep within Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient. Only one chosen by fate is said to be able to enter.<end>"
KO: "………………. 엔샨트 남동쪽에 있는 현자의 숲. 그 깊숙한 곳에 있다고 전해지는 저택이다. 운명에 선택받은 자만 들어갈 수 있다고 하지.<end>"

## 1080020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森はエンシャントの南東だ。<line-break>その奥に賢者が住まっているとも<line-break>聞くが、俺は興味がないな。<end>"
EN: "Sage's Forest is southeast of Ancient. I've also heard that a sage lives deep within it, but I have no interest.<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲은 엔샨트 남동쪽이야. 그 안쪽에 현자가 산다는 말도 들었지만 난 관심 없군.<end>"

## 1080021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャント南東に賢者の森があるのは<line-break>知ってる？　その森の奥に建っていると<line-break>いわれる屋敷のことだよ。<line-break>運命に選ばれた者のみ行き着くことの<line-break>できる謎の屋敷…興味あるでしょ？<line-break>じゃ、さっそく行ってみようよ！<end>"
EN: "You know Sage's Forest is southeast of Ancient, right? It's the manor said to stand deep within that forest. A mysterious manor that only one chosen by fate can reach... You're curious, aren't you? Then let's go right away!<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽에 현자의 숲이 있는 건 알아? 그 숲 깊숙한 곳에 있다고 하는 저택 말이야. 운명에 선택된 자만 도달할 수 있는 수수께끼의 저택… 흥미롭지? 그럼 바로 가 보자!<end>"

## 1080022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャント南東の賢者の森には、<line-break>賢者様の屋敷が隠されている。<line-break>…有名な噂話ではある。<line-break>運命に選ばれた者しかそこへ<line-break>至れないとも聞くな。<line-break>私も一度は試してみたいものだ。<end>"
EN: "A sage's manor is said to be hidden in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient. It is a famous rumor... I've also heard that only one chosen by fate can reach it. I would like to put that to the test at least once.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲에는 현자의 저택이 숨겨져 있다. …유명한 소문이지. 운명에 선택받은 자만 그곳에 이를 수 있다고도 들었다. 나도 한 번 시험해 보고 싶군.<end>"

## 1080023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その話は、聞いたことがあります。<line-break>エンシャント南東、賢者の森には、<line-break>賢者様の屋敷が隠されていると…。<line-break>しかし、そこへは運命に選ばれないと<line-break>永遠にたどりつくことができないとも<line-break>聞いております。<end>"
EN: "I have heard that story. A sage's manor is said to be hidden in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient... But I have also heard that anyone not chosen by fate will never reach it.<end>"
KO: "그 이야기는 들은 적이 있습니다. 엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲에는 현자님의 저택이 숨겨져 있다고…. 하지만 운명에 선택받지 않으면 영원히 그곳에 도달할 수 없다고도 들었습니다.<end>"

## 1080024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その話なら、前に聞いたことがあるよ。<line-break>エンシャント南東、賢者の森には、<line-break>賢者様の屋敷が隠されているだって。<line-break>ただ、そこへは、運命に選ばれないと<line-break>たどりつけないみたい。<end>"
EN: "I've heard that story before. They say a sage's manor is hidden in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient. But it seems you can't reach it unless fate has chosen you.<end>"
KO: "그 이야기는 전에 들은 적 있어. 엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲에는 현자의 저택이 숨어 있대. 다만 운명에 선택받지 않으면 거기까지 갈 수 없는 모양이야.<end>"

## 1080025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森に隠れ住む賢者とな。<line-break>森は、運命に選ばれた者のみを<line-break>その元へ通すのだという。<line-break>静かに暮らしたいという気持ちは<line-break>わからんでもないが、いささか<line-break>気まぐれなところがあるようじゃの。<end>"
EN: "A sage living in seclusion in Sage's Forest, eh? They say the forest allows only those chosen by fate to pass through to the sage. I can understand the desire for a quiet life, but it does seem rather capricious.<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲에 숨어 산다는 현자라. 그 숲은 운명에 선택받은 자만 현자에게 통과시킨다지. 조용히 살고 싶은 마음은 이해 못 할 것도 없지만, 조금 변덕스러운 면이 있는 모양이군.<end>"

## 1080026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東に広がる<line-break>賢者の森は、運命に選ばれた者<line-break>だけを賢者のもとへと通すという。<line-break>試してみたいのは私も同じだが、<line-break>いざたどり着けないとなると、<line-break>少なからず自信を失いそうだな。<end>"
EN: "They say Sage's Forest, stretching southeast of Ancient, allows only those chosen by fate to reach the sage. I would like to test it too, but if I failed to reach the place, it would shake my confidence more than a little.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽에 펼쳐진 현자의 숲은 운명에 선택받은 자만 현자에게로 통과시킨다고 하지. 나도 시험해 보고 싶은 건 같지만, 막상 도달하지 못한다면 자신감이 꽤 꺾일 것 같군.<end>"

## 1080028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "凄腕の冒険者の間じゃ有名な噂よ。<line-break>エンシャント南東の森の奥にある<line-break>賢者が住んでるって屋敷の噂。<line-break>運命に選ばれた者しかたどりつけない…<line-break>って話なんだけど。<line-break>気になるなら行ってみない？<end>"
EN: "It's a famous rumor among accomplished adventurers. They say there's a manor deep in the forest southeast of Ancient where a sage lives, and that only one chosen by fate can reach it... If you're curious, why don't we go?<end>"
KO: "실력 있는 모험자들 사이에선 유명한 소문이야. 엔샨트 남동쪽 숲 깊숙한 곳에 현자가 산다는 저택 이야기지. 운명에 선택받은 자만 도착할 수 있다는데, 궁금하면 가보지 않을래?<end>"

## 1080030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東にある森に、<line-break>そういうお屋敷があるらしいです。<line-break>そこには賢者様が住んでるとか。<line-break>運命に選ばれた人しか入れないって、<line-break>聞いたことがあるんですけど…<line-break>わ、私じゃあ無理ですよね…。<end>"
EN: "Apparently there's such a manor in the forest southeast of Ancient. They say a sage lives there. I've heard that only someone chosen by fate can enter, but... s-someone like me could never do it, right...?<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽 숲에 그런 저택이 있다고 해요. 거기엔 현자님이 산다나 봐요. 운명에 선택된 사람만 들어갈 수 있다고 들었는데… 저, 저는 무리겠죠….<end>"

## 1080032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（レムオンに賢者の森へ<line-break>　弟のエストを捜しにいくよう<line-break>　頼まれてしまった）<line-break>（エストのメモには、エンシャント、<line-break>　南東、賢者の森、奥、<line-break>　そして猫屋敷、とあったそうだ）<end>"
EN: "(Lemghon asked me to go to Sage's Forest and look for his younger brother, Est.) (Est's note apparently said: Ancient, southeast, Sage's Forest, deep within, and Cat Manor.)<end>"
KO: "(레무온에게 현자의 숲으로 가서 동생 에스트를 찾아 달라는 부탁을 받았다.) (에스트의 메모에는 엔샨트, 남동쪽, 현자의 숲, 깊숙한 곳, 그리고 고양이 저택이라고 적혀 있었다고 한다.)<end>"

## 1080033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森にあるっていう謎の屋敷ね。<line-break>そこに入った人も、見た人もいないのに<line-break>あるってことだけ知られてる。<line-break>不思議な話よね。運命に選ばれた者<line-break>だけが発見できて、館に入れるって<line-break>いうけど本当かしら。<end>"
EN: "You mean the mysterious manor said to be in Sage's Forest. No one has ever seen it or gone inside, yet its existence is known. Strange, isn't it? They say only one chosen by fate can find the manor and enter it, but I wonder if that's true.<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲에 있다는 수수께끼의 저택 말이지. 들어간 사람도 본 사람도 없는데 존재한다는 것만 알려져 있어. 이상한 이야기야. 운명에 선택된 자만 발견해서 저택에 들어갈 수 있다는데 정말일까.<end>"

## 1080034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森の奥深くにひっそりと建つ、<line-break>誰も見つけることのできない謎の<line-break>屋敷のことじゃな。<line-break>唯一運命に選ばれた者のみが見つける<line-break>ことができると言われておる。<line-break>お主も挑戦してみてはどうじゃ？<end>"
EN: "Ah, the mysterious manor hidden deep within Sage's Forest, which no one can find. They say only one chosen by fate can discover it. Why not try your luck?<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲 깊숙한 곳에 조용히 서 있는, 누구도 찾을 수 없는 수수께끼의 저택 말이군. 오직 운명에 선택받은 자만 찾을 수 있다고 하지. 자네도 도전해 보는 게 어떤가?<end>"

## 1080035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫がたくさんいるから猫屋敷なんです。<line-break>うん、きっとそうに違いありません。<line-break>そして屋敷には猫顔の主人がひとり…。<line-break>私ってけっこう想像力豊かですよね。<line-break>たくさんの猫に囲まれた猫顔の主人…。<line-break>ちょっと見てみたくなっちゃいました。<end>"
EN: "It must be called Cat Manor because there are lots of cats there. Yes, that has to be it. And there is a single cat-faced owner at the manor... I have quite the imagination, don't I? A cat-faced owner surrounded by lots of cats... Now I rather want to see it.<end>"
KO: "고양이가 많으니까 고양이 저택인 거예요. 응, 분명 그래요. 그리고 저택에는 고양이 얼굴을 한 주인이 한 명…. 저 상상력이 꽤 풍부하죠? 수많은 고양이에 둘러싸인 고양이 얼굴 주인…. 조금 보고 싶어졌어요.<end>"

## 1080037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東にある賢者の森、<line-break>そこの奥深くに建っている屋敷のこと<line-break>だよね。誰も見つけられないって噂の。<line-break>ボク、その屋敷に興味あるな。<line-break>運命に選ばれた人しか見つけられない<line-break>屋敷なんて不思議だものね。<end>"
EN: "You mean the manor deep in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient? The one no one can find. I'm curious about it. A manor that only someone chosen by fate can find sounds so mysterious.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽에 있는 현자의 숲, 그 깊숙한 곳에 있다는 저택 말이지? 아무도 찾지 못한다는 소문의. 나, 그 저택에 관심 있어. 운명에 선택된 사람만 찾을 수 있는 저택이라니 신기하잖아.<end>"

## 1080038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森はエンシャントの南東よ。<line-break>でも、その奥にあるという屋敷には<line-break>まだ誰もたどりつけていない…。<end>"
EN: "Sage's Forest is southeast of Ancient. But no one has yet reached the manor said to lie deep within it...<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲은 엔샨트 남동쪽이야. 하지만 그 깊숙한 곳에 있다는 저택에는 아직 아무도 도달하지 못했어….<end>"

## 1080040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者様が住んでるって噂の屋敷ね。<line-break>場所はエンシャントの南東にある<line-break>賢者の森だったかな？<line-break>もしかして、運命に選ばれた者のみが<line-break>発見できるって話を聞いて挑戦する気？<line-break>けっこう自信家なんだね。<end>"
EN: "The manor where the sage is rumored to live? Wasn't it in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient? Don't tell me you heard that only one chosen by fate can find it and now mean to try. You're pretty confident, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "현자님이 산다는 소문의 저택 말이지. 장소는 엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲이었던가? 설마 운명에 선택받은 자만 발견할 수 있다는 말을 듣고 도전하려는 거야? 꽤 자신만만하네.<end>"

## 1080041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東に賢者の森って<line-break>あるだろ。その森の奥に建ってる館が<line-break>猫屋敷らしいよ。<line-break>でも、姉ちゃん。その館には誰も<line-break>入れないっていうぜ。あっ、でも運命に<line-break>選ばれた人間なら入れるんだったかな。<end>"
EN: "You know Sage's Forest southeast of Ancient, right? Apparently the manor deep in that forest is Cat Manor. But listen, sis, they say no one can enter it. Oh, wait... Maybe a person chosen by fate can.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽에 현자의 숲이 있잖아. 그 숲 깊숙한 곳에 있는 저택이 고양이 저택이라나 봐. 하지만 누나, 그 저택엔 아무도 들어갈 수 없대. 아, 그래도 운명에 선택된 사람이라면 들어갈 수 있었나.<end>"

## 1080042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森は確かにエンシャントの<line-break>南東にある。ただし、その奥の<line-break>屋敷には、誰も至っていないわ。<end>"
EN: "Sage's Forest is indeed southeast of Ancient. However, no one has ever reached the manor deep within it.<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲은 분명 엔샨트 남동쪽에 있어. 하지만 그 안쪽 저택까지 간 사람은 아무도 없어.<end>"

## 1080043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、運命に選ばれた者だけが<line-break>見つけることのできるって噂の屋敷ね。<line-break>賢者の森の奥深くにあるんだろ。<line-break>ま、噂なんてのは正誤を確認するまでは<line-break>嘘と一緒だからな。どこまでホントか<line-break>わからんが行ってみるかい？<end>"
EN: "Oh, the manor rumored to be found only by one chosen by fate. It's deep in Sage's Forest, right? Well, until a rumor's been proven true or false, it may as well be a lie. No telling how much of this one's true, but shall we go see?<end>"
KO: "아, 운명에 선택된 자만 찾을 수 있다는 소문의 저택 말이지. 현자의 숲 깊숙한 곳에 있다던데. 뭐, 소문은 사실인지 확인하기 전까진 거짓말이나 마찬가지니까. 어디까지 진짜인지 모르겠지만 가 볼래?<end>"

## 1080044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命に選ばれた者のみが発見できる<line-break>という屋敷のことか…。<line-break>そんなものに興味を持ってどうする。<line-break>俺にはそんなくだらぬ噂を確かめる<line-break>暇などない。<end>"
EN: "The manor that supposedly can be found only by one chosen by fate...? Why take an interest in such a thing? I have no time to investigate such foolish rumors.<end>"
KO: "운명에 선택받은 자만 발견할 수 있다는 저택 말인가… 그런 것에 관심을 가져서 뭘 하겠다는 거지. 난 그런 시시한 소문을 확인할 시간 따윈 없어.<end>"

## 1080045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森の奥にある屋敷のことじゃ。<line-break>そこには運命に選ばれた者しか<line-break>入れんと言われておる。<line-break>お主が運命に選ばれた者かどうか<line-break>確かめてみるためにも行ってみよう<line-break>ではないか。<end>"
EN: "The manor deep in Sage's Forest. It is said that only one chosen by fate may enter. Let us go, if only to see whether fate has chosen you.<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲 깊숙한 곳에 있는 저택 말이다. 그곳에는 운명에 선택받은 자만 들어갈 수 있다고 하지. 네가 운명에 선택받은 자인지 확인해 보기 위해서라도 가 보지 않겠나.<end>"

## 1080046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東、賢者の森にある<line-break>謎の屋敷のことだよ。賢者様が住むと<line-break>いう噂もあるみたいだね。<line-break>でも、誰もそこにたどり着いていない。<line-break>運命に選ばれた者だけが猫屋敷に<line-break>入ることができるんだって。<end>"
EN: "It's the mysterious manor in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient. There even seems to be a rumor that a sage lives there. But no one has ever reached it. They say only one chosen by fate can enter Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲에 있는 수수께끼의 저택 말이야. 현자님이 산다는 소문도 있는 모양이네. 하지만 아무도 거기에 도착하지 못했어. 운명에 선택된 자만 고양이 저택에 들어갈 수 있대.<end>"

## 1080047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東に、<line-break>賢者の森はある。<line-break><line-break>が、そこにある賢者の屋敷にまで<line-break>たどりつけるのは、運命に選ばれた<line-break>者だけだという話だ…。<end>"
EN: "Sage's Forest lies southeast of Ancient. But they say only one chosen by fate can reach the sage's manor within it...<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽에 현자의 숲이 있다. 하지만 그곳의 현자의 저택까지 도달할 수 있는 건 운명에 선택받은 자뿐이라는 이야기다….<end>"

## 1080048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫屋敷ですって？　どーーーして<line-break>高貴なエルフのアタクシが人間の<line-break>噂話を知ってなくてはならないのよ！<line-break>高貴で気品あふれるエルフには<line-break>人間のように噂をするなんてみにくい<line-break>習慣はないの。覚えておきなさい。<end>"
EN: "Cat Manor? Whyyy should a noble Elf such as myself be expected to know human gossip?! Noble, refined Elves do not have the ugly human habit of gossiping. Remember that.<end>"
KO: "고양이 저택이라고? 도대체 왜 고귀한 엘프인 이 몸이 인간들의 소문 따위를 알아야 하는 거야! 고귀하고 기품 넘치는 엘프에게는 인간처럼 소문이나 떠드는 추한 습관은 없어. 기억해 둬.<end>"

## 1080049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫屋敷ボゲー<end>"
EN: "Cat Manor Bogee<end>"
KO: "고양이 저택 보게<end>"

## 1080050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東にある賢者の森の<line-break>奥に森の賢者さんが住んでる屋敷が<line-break>あるの。それが猫屋敷だよぉ。<line-break>でもね、運命に選ばれた人しか入ること<line-break>できないんだって。不思議でしょ？<line-break>ね、行ってみようよぉ。<end>"
EN: "Deep in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient, there's a manor where the sage of the forest lives. That's Cat Manor. But only someone chosen by fate can go inside. Mysterious, isn't it? Come on, let's go see!<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲 깊숙한 곳에 숲의 현자님이 사는 저택이 있어. 그게 고양이 저택이야아. 그런데 운명에 선택된 사람만 들어갈 수 있대. 신기하지? 자, 가 보자아.<end>"

## 1080051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>エンシャントの南東にある賢者の森。<line-break>その奥に建つと言われる屋敷のことだ。<line-break>運命に選ばれた者しか入れないという。<end>"
EN: "............... The manor said to stand deep within Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient. Only one chosen by fate is said to be able to enter.<end>"
KO: "………………. 엔샨트 남동쪽에 있는 현자의 숲. 그 깊숙한 곳에 있다고 전해지는 저택이다. 운명에 선택받은 자만 들어갈 수 있다고 하지.<end>"

## 1080053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャント南東に賢者の森があるのは<line-break>知ってる？　その森の奥に建っていると<line-break>いわれる屋敷のことだよ。<line-break>運命に選ばれた者のみ行き着くことの<line-break>できる謎の屋敷…興味あるでしょ？<line-break>じゃ、さっそく行ってみようよ！<end>"
EN: "You know Sage's Forest is southeast of Ancient, right? It's the manor said to stand deep within that forest. A mysterious manor that only one chosen by fate can reach... You're curious, aren't you? Then let's go right away!<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽에 현자의 숲이 있는 건 알아? 그 숲 깊숙한 곳에 있다고 하는 저택 말이야. 운명에 선택된 자만 도달할 수 있는 수수께끼의 저택… 흥미롭지? 그럼 바로 가 보자!<end>"

## 1080054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャント南東の賢者の森には、<line-break>賢者様の屋敷が隠されている。<line-break>…有名な噂話ではある。<line-break>運命に選ばれた者しかそこへ<line-break>至れないとも聞くな。<line-break>私も一度は試してみたいものだ。<end>"
EN: "A sage's manor is said to be hidden in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient. It is a famous rumor... I've also heard that only one chosen by fate can reach it. I would like to put that to the test at least once.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲에는 현자의 저택이 숨겨져 있다. …유명한 소문이지. 운명에 선택받은 자만 그곳에 이를 수 있다고도 들었다. 나도 한 번 시험해 보고 싶군.<end>"

## 1080055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫屋敷とは、エンシャント南東の<line-break>賢者の森に隠されているという<line-break>賢者様の屋敷のことでしょう。<line-break>しかし、そこへは運命に選ばれないと<line-break>永遠にたどりつくことができないとも<line-break>聞いております。<end>"
EN: "Cat Manor must be the sage's manor said to be hidden in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient. But I have also heard that anyone not chosen by fate will never reach it.<end>"
KO: "고양이 저택이란 엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲에 숨겨져 있다는 현자님의 저택이겠지요. 하지만 운명에 선택받지 못하면 영원히 도착할 수 없다고도 들었습니다.<end>"

## 1080056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その話なら、私も聞いたよ。<line-break>エンシャント南東、賢者の森には、<line-break>賢者様の屋敷が隠されているだって。<line-break>ただ、そこへは運命に選ばれないと<line-break>決してたどりつけないって話だよ。<end>"
EN: "I've heard that story too. They say a sage's manor is hidden in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient. But the story goes that you can never reach it unless fate has chosen you.<end>"
KO: "그 얘기라면 나도 들었어. 엔샨트 남동쪽, 현자의 숲에는 현자님의 저택이 숨겨져 있대. 하지만 운명에 선택받지 못하면 절대 도착할 수 없다는 얘기야.<end>"

## 1080057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン殿に依頼されたのは、<line-break>弟のエスト殿の捜索だったな。<line-break><line-break>彼の残した書き留めによれば、<line-break>エンシャント、南東、賢者の森、奥、<line-break>そして猫屋敷とあったという…。<end>"
EN: "Lemghon's request was to search for his younger brother, Est. The note Est left behind apparently read: Ancient, southeast, Sage's Forest, deep within, and Cat Manor...<end>"
KO: "레무온 님에게 부탁받은 건 동생 에스트 님의 수색이었지. 그가 남긴 메모에는 엔샨트, 남동쪽, 현자의 숲, 안쪽, 그리고 고양이 저택이라고 적혀 있었다더군….<end>"

## 1080058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東、賢者の森が<line-break>運命に選ばれた者のみを通すという、<line-break>賢者の屋敷のことだろう。<line-break>試してみたいのは私も同じだが、<line-break>いざたどり着けないとなると、<line-break>少々、自信を失いそうだな。<end>"
EN: "You mean the sage's manor in Sage's Forest southeast of Ancient, which is said to admit only those chosen by fate. I would like to test it too, but if I failed to reach the place, it would shake my confidence somewhat.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲이 운명에 선택받은 자만 통과시킨다는 현자의 저택 말이겠지. 나도 시험해 보고 싶지만 정작 도착하지 못한다면 조금 자신감을 잃을 것 같군.<end>"

## 1080060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "凄腕の冒険者の間じゃ有名な噂よ。<line-break>エンシャント南東の森の奥にある<line-break>賢者が住んでるって屋敷の噂。<line-break>運命に選ばれた者しかたどりつけない…<line-break>って話なんだけど。<line-break>気になるなら行ってみない？<end>"
EN: "It's a famous rumor among accomplished adventurers. They say there's a manor deep in the forest southeast of Ancient where a sage lives, and that only one chosen by fate can reach it... If you're curious, why don't we go?<end>"
KO: "실력 있는 모험자들 사이에선 유명한 소문이야. 엔샨트 남동쪽 숲 깊숙한 곳에 현자가 산다는 저택 이야기지. 운명에 선택받은 자만 도착할 수 있다는데, 궁금하면 가보지 않을래?<end>"

## 1080062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東にある森に、<line-break>そういうお屋敷があるらしいです。<line-break>そこには賢者様が住んでるとか。<line-break>運命に選ばれた人しか入れないって、<line-break>聞いたことがあるんですけど…<line-break>わ、私じゃあ無理ですよね…。<end>"
EN: "Apparently there's such a manor in the forest southeast of Ancient. They say a sage lives there. I've heard that only someone chosen by fate can enter, but... s-someone like me could never do it, right...?<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽 숲에 그런 저택이 있다고 해요. 거기엔 현자님이 산다나 봐요. 운명에 선택된 사람만 들어갈 수 있다고 들었는데… 저, 저는 무리겠죠….<end>"

## 1080064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（何をすればいいのかわからないときや<line-break>　知りたいことがあるときには、<line-break>　仲間に尋ねるんだけど…）<line-break>（今は相談できそうな人がいないから、<line-break>　無理みたいだ。<line-break>　仲間を捜そうかな…）<end>"
EN: "(When I'm unsure what to do or want to know something, I can ask a companion, but...) (There doesn't seem to be anyone I can consult right now, so I can't. Maybe I should look for a companion...)<end>"
KO: "(뭘 해야 할지 모르거나 알고 싶은 게 있으면 동료에게 물어보는데…) (지금은 상담할 사람이 없는 것 같아 무리네. 동료를 찾아볼까….)<end>"

## 1080065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何かわからないことがあったら私に<line-break>聞きなさいよ。ひとりで悩んでいたって<line-break>しょうがないんだからね。<line-break>もちろん、私の知らないことを<line-break>聞かれたら答えられないかもしれない<line-break>けど…なんとかなるわよ。<end>"
EN: "If there's something you don't understand, ask me. There's no point worrying about it alone. Of course, if you ask about something I don't know, I might not be able to answer... but we'll manage somehow.<end>"
KO: "뭔가 모르는 게 있으면 나한테 물어봐. 혼자 고민해 봐야 소용없잖아. 물론 내가 모르는 걸 물으면 답을 못 할 수도 있지만… 뭐, 어떻게든 되겠지.<end>"

## 1080066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主のわからぬことがあれば<line-break>遠慮なく聞くがよいぞ。<line-break>わしの識者ぶりを披露してやるわい。<line-break>なんといっても、お主より長く生きとる<line-break>からのう。経験の豊富さには自信が<line-break>あるんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "If there is anything you do not understand, do not hesitate to ask. I shall show you just how learned I am. After all, I have lived longer than you. I am quite confident in my wealth of experience.<end>"
KO: "모르는 게 있으면 사양 말고 묻게. 내 박식함을 보여 주지. 아무래도 자네보다 오래 살았으니까. 경험의 풍부함에는 자신이 있네.<end>"

## 1080067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の意見が欲しいのですか？<line-break>私って頼りにされてるのですね。<line-break>とってもうれしいです。<line-break>もう、何だって聞いちゃってください。<line-break>私の才女ぶりをお見せしますから。<end>"
EN: "You want my opinion? That means you rely on me. I'm so happy! Go ahead and ask me anything. I'll show you what a brilliant woman I am.<end>"
KO: "제 의견이 필요하신가요? 저도 의지가 되는 사람이군요. 정말 기뻐요. 뭐든 물어보세요. 제 재녀다운 면모를 보여 드릴게요.<end>"

## 1080068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わからないことがあったら俺に聞きな。<line-break>強いだけじゃなく頭もいい俺様が<line-break>何でも答えてやるぜ。<line-break>ぐじぐじひとりで悩むな。意見を聞い<line-break>たらイケンなんて言わないからよ…。<line-break>意見とイケンをかけてるんだ、ぷぷっ。<end>"
EN: "If there's something you don't know, ask me. I'm not just strong; I'm smart enough to answer anything. Don't sit around fretting by yourself. Ask for my two cents―I won't say it doesn't make cents... Get it? “Sense” and “cents.” Heh heh.<end>"
KO: "모르는 게 있으면 나한테 물어봐. 강하기만 한 게 아니라 머리도 좋은 이 몸이 뭐든 답해 주지. 혼자 끙끙대지 마. 의견을 듣는다고 이견 있냐고 하진 않을 테니까…. 의견이랑 이견을 엮은 거야, 푸훗.<end>"

## 1080069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何かわからないことがあったら<line-break>遠慮なくボクに聞いてよね。<line-break>こう見えても物知りなんだから。<end>"
EN: "If there's something you don't understand, don't hesitate to ask me. I know quite a lot, even if I don't look it.<end>"
KO: "모르는 게 있으면 사양 말고 나한테 물어봐. 이래 보여도 아는 게 많거든.<end>"

## 1080070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが知らないことで、<line-break>私が知っていることがあれば教えるわ。<line-break>ひとりで悩むのは時間の無駄だから。<end>"
EN: "If I know something you don't, I'll tell you. Worrying about it alone is a waste of time.<end>"
KO: "네가 모르는 것 중 내가 아는 게 있다면 알려 줄게. 혼자 고민하는 건 시간 낭비니까.<end>"

## 1080071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが疑問に思ったことや、<line-break>わからないことについて答えますよ。<line-break>遠慮せず何でも聞いてください。<line-break>安易に答えを求めるのは、あまり<line-break>誉められたものではないですが、<line-break>ひとりで悩むのは時間の無駄ですから。<end>"
EN: "I'll answer any questions you have or explain anything you don't understand. Please ask whatever you like. Seeking easy answers is hardly commendable, but worrying alone is a waste of time.<end>"
KO: "궁금한 것이나 모르는 것에 대해 답해 드리죠. 사양 말고 뭐든 물어보세요. 쉽게 답부터 구하는 건 그다지 칭찬할 일은 아니지만, 혼자 고민하는 건 시간 낭비니까요.<end>"

## 1080072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたの悩みにズバリとお答えします<line-break>ってことよ。わかんないことをひとりで<line-break>悩んだってしょうがないでしょ。<line-break>まっ、人生経験豊かなあたしにかかれば<line-break>家庭問題から恋の悩みまでスパーっと<line-break>解決よ。<end>"
EN: "It means I'll give you a straight answer to whatever's troubling you. There's no point worrying all by yourself about something you don't understand. With all my life experience, I can solve anything in a flash, from family troubles to romantic woes.<end>"
KO: "네 고민에 딱 잘라 답해 준다는 거야. 모르는 걸 혼자 끙끙 앓아 봐야 소용없잖아. 뭐, 인생 경험 풍부한 나한테 맡기면 가정 문제부터 연애 고민까지 시원하게 해결해 주지.<end>"

## 1080073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん少しは俺の意見も聞いて<line-break>くれよな。俺だって姉ちゃんの役に<line-break>立とうと色々考えてるんだからさ。<end>"
EN: "Sis, you could listen to my opinion once in a while. I think about all sorts of things because I want to be useful to you too.<end>"
KO: "누나도 내 의견 좀 들어 줘. 나도 누나에게 도움이 되려고 이것저것 생각하고 있으니까.<end>"

## 1080074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがわからないことや、<line-break>疑問に思ったことは、<line-break>仲間に尋ねてみるといいわ。<end>"
EN: "If there's anything you don't understand or have questions about, try asking a companion.<end>"
KO: "모르는 것이나 궁금한 게 있으면 동료에게 물어보는 게 좋아.<end>"

## 1080075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リーダーってのは仲間の意見も尊重<line-break>しなきゃいけないからな。たまには<line-break>仲間の意見を聞くことも必要だろな。<line-break>それに自分のわからないことをひとりで<line-break>悩んでもしょうがないしな。俺の知識も<line-break>少しは役に立つと思うぜ。<end>"
EN: "A leader has to respect the opinions of the group, so you ought to listen to your companions now and then. Besides, there's no point agonizing alone over something you don't understand. I think my knowledge might be of some use too.<end>"
KO: "리더라면 동료 의견도 존중해야 하잖아. 가끔은 동료들 말을 듣는 것도 필요하지. 모르는 걸 혼자 고민해 봐야 소용없고. 내 지식도 조금은 도움이 될 거야.<end>"

## 1080076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "質問に対する俺なりの意見は言うが、<line-break>それをどう生かすかはお前次第だ。<line-break>リーダーはお前なのだからな。<end>"
EN: "I'll give you my view on any question, but how you use it is up to you. You're the leader, after all.<end>"
KO: "질문에 대한 내 의견은 말해 주겠지만 그걸 어떻게 활용할지는 네 몫이다. 리더는 너니까.<end>"

## 1080077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何かわからぬことがあれば、遠慮なく<line-break>わしに聞くがよいぞ。わしが的確な<line-break>答えを与えてやるわい。<end>"
EN: "If there is anything you do not understand, do not hesitate to ask me. I shall give you a precise answer.<end>"
KO: "모르는 게 있으면 사양 말고 내게 물어라. 내가 정확한 답을 알려 주지.<end>"

## 1080078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕の意見でよければ何でも聞いてよ。<line-break>それが役に立ってくれるなら、<line-break>僕もうれしいし。<line-break>仲間はお互いの足りない部分を<line-break>おぎなうものだって、お爺ちゃんも<line-break>言っていたしね。<end>"
EN: "If my opinion will do, ask me anything. I'd be happy if it helped. Grandpa always said companions are supposed to make up for one another's shortcomings.<end>"
KO: "내 의견이라도 괜찮다면 뭐든 물어봐. 그게 도움이 된다면 나도 기쁘니까. 동료는 서로 부족한 부분을 채워 주는 거라고 할아버지도 말씀하셨거든.<end>"

## 1080079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "参考になるかどうかはわからないが<line-break>私の意見でよければ聞いてくれ。<line-break>リーダーは君だ。遠慮することはない。<end>"
EN: "I do not know whether it will be useful, but you may ask for my opinion if you wish. You are the leader. There is no need to hesitate.<end>"
KO: "도움이 될지는 모르겠지만 내 의견이라도 괜찮다면 들어 줘. 리더는 너야. 사양할 필요 없어.<end>"

## 1080080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "高貴で気品あふれるアタクシの意見が<line-break>聞きたいですって？<line-break><line-break>仕方ないわね、優雅で知性あふれる<line-break>アタクシが特別に答えてあげるわよ。<end>"
EN: "You want the opinion of someone as noble and refined as me? Fine. Elegant and brilliant as I am, I'll make a special exception and answer you.<end>"
KO: "고귀하고 품격 넘치는 제 의견이 듣고 싶다고요? 어쩔 수 없네요. 우아하고 지성 넘치는 제가 특별히 대답해 드리죠.<end>"

## 1080081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、兄弟子の質問をお題にして<line-break>ダジャレを言えばいいのですな。<line-break>わかり申した。期待してくだされ。<end>"
EN: "I see. I am to use my senior fellow student's question as the subject of a pun. Understood. You may expect great things.<end>"
KO: "과연, 사형의 질문을 소재로 말장난을 하면 되는 것이군요. 알겠습니다. 기대해 주십시오.<end>"

## 1080082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、ねえ、わからないことがあったら<line-break>悩まないでルルアンタに聞いてよぉ。<line-break>ルルアンタ、きっと力になるからぁ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, hey, if there's something you don't understand, don't worry―ask Lulluantha. Lulluantha will surely help!<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 모르는 게 있으면 고민하지 말고 루루안타한테 물어봐. 루루안타가 분명 힘이 되어 줄게.<end>"

## 1080083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>俺の意見が聞きたいのか…。<line-break>わかった。何でも聞け。<end>"
EN: "............... You want my opinion...? Very well. Ask me anything.<end>"
KO: "………………. 내 의견이 듣고 싶은 건가…. 알았다. 뭐든 물어봐.<end>"

## 1080084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何をすればいいのかわからなくなったら<line-break>仲間に尋ねてみるがいい。<line-break>そこから道が開けることもあるだろう。<end>"
EN: "If you become unsure what to do, ask a companion. That may open a way forward.<end>"
KO: "무엇을 해야 할지 모르겠다면 동료에게 물어보는 게 좋다. 거기서 길이 열릴 수도 있을 테니.<end>"

## 1080085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わからないことがあったら、どんどん<line-break>聞いてみてよ。世界中を旅してきた<line-break>ぼくがズバリと答えるから。<end>"
EN: "If there's something you don't understand, ask away. I've traveled all over the world, and I'll give you a straight answer.<end>"
KO: "모르는 게 있으면 뭐든 물어봐. 세계를 돌아다닌 내가 딱 잘라 대답해 줄게.<end>"

## 1080086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "考え込んでわからないことを、<line-break>仲間に聞かず、誰に聞く？<line-break>答えは、案外身近に転がっているぞ。<end>"
EN: "If you've thought and still don't understand, who should you ask if not a companion? The answer may be closer than you think.<end>"
KO: "아무리 고민해도 모르는 걸 동료에게 안 묻고 누구에게 묻겠나? 답은 의외로 가까운 곳에 굴러다니는 법이야.<end>"

## 1080087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何か、迷うようなことがありましたら、<line-break>どうかお気軽にお尋ねください。<line-break>私たちは、仲間なのですから。<end>"
EN: "If ever you are uncertain about something, please feel free to ask. We are companions, after all.<end>"
KO: "무언가 고민되는 일이 있다면 부담 없이 물어봐 주세요. 우리는 동료니까요.<end>"

## 1080088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何か、迷うようなことがあったら、<line-break>気軽に聞いてね。<end>"
EN: "If you're ever uncertain about something, feel free to ask.<end>"
KO: "뭔가 고민되는 게 있으면 편하게 물어봐.<end>"

## 1080089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec108-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を迷うことがあるのじゃ！<line-break>わしのごとき年寄りなぞ、<line-break>助言を言うためにいるようなものじゃ。<line-break>何かわからないことが出てきたら、<line-break>すぐに聞いてくれてよいぞ。<end>"
EN: "What is there to hesitate over? An old man like me practically exists to give advice. If anything comes up that you do not understand, ask me at once.<end>"
KO: "뭘 망설이는 게냐! 나 같은 늙은이는 조언하라고 있는 거나 마찬가지다. 모르는 게 생기면 언제든 물어봐도 좋다.<end>"
